# Effects Library — Stage E1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Users pick one of five polished motion effects (Плавный наезд, Кино-наезд, Резкий наезд, Плавная панорама, Ручная камера) in a new «Эффекты» tab — hover to see it on the selected clips, click or drag to apply — and tune it in the inspector's «Эффекты клипа» section, with undo, keyframes on its numbers, MCP and AI commands, on a forward-compatible effect model (project format 3).

**Architecture:** `kadr-project` stores `AppliedEffect`s on a clip (recipe id, version, typed `ParamValue`s, progress range; unknown data kept verbatim) and keyframe tracks of effect numbers under `fx:<effect-id>:<key>`. A new crate `kadr-effects` holds the recipe registry, parameter definitions and validation, `Noise1D` and the five motion recipes, each compiling to a `PlacementDelta`; the evaluator (`kadr-timeline::fx`) composes those deltas over the clip's placement, so `kadr-scene`, `kadr-render` and `kadr-playback` do not change. `EditEngine` gets `AddEffect` / `RemoveEffect` / `UpdateEffect` / `MoveEffect`; the editor gets the tab (`effects_ui.rs`, `fx_thumbs.rs`, `ui/effects.slint`), the inspector section, point picking on the preview and an `fx N` badge; MCP and the AI assistant reach the same commands.

**Tech Stack:** Rust 2024 workspace, `serde` / `serde_json`, Slint 1.18.1 (`apps/editor/ui/*.slint`), `kadr-i18n` JSON catalogs, FFmpeg through the existing resolver, `cargo test` / `cargo clippy` on Windows (Git Bash).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-11-effects-library-design.md` — this plan is stage **E1** only (spec §10 item 1). Read the spec (§0–§3, §5 «Движение», §6, §7, §9) and the keyframes spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-01-keyframes-design.md` §1–§5 before any task; section numbers below refer to the effects spec unless marked "keyframes spec". Looks and colour primitives (E2), generators and transitions (E3) and audio (E4) are out of scope.

## Global Constraints

- All time is integer flicks (`kadr_core::Time`, 705 600 000 per second). Timeline ↔ source only through `Clip::source_time_at(t)` / `Clip::timeline_time_of(s)`. f64 appears for a fraction only: an effect's progress `p = from + (to − from) · (t − timeline_in) / duration` (integer flicks → one f64), a keyframe segment's `u`.
- Time domains (§1): a whole-clip effect is **clip-bound** — progress runs over the visible clip; trim and speed stretch or compress it and never change `progress`. «Момент» is **source-bound** (`SourceTime`): trim never moves it. Effect-parameter keyframes are source-bound like all keys. **Split** at progress `c` gives the left half `(from, c)`, the right `(c, to)` — the picture at the joint does not change; both halves keep the effect's id.
- Strength (§2): a `Number` in `[0, 1]`, UI 0–100 %. Intensity `k = s / 0.6`: «Обычно» 60 % = the preset as designed, 0 % = no effect, 100 % = ×1.67. Marks «Мягко / Обычно / Сильно» at 30 / 60 / 90 %, the slider snaps to them. Strength 0 or a disabled effect produces nothing — not even a `compile` (culling, §3).
- Motion (§5, at `k = 1`): Плавный наезд — scale 1.00 → 1.10 about the point, curve Smooth (`EaseInOut`) over the whole progress; Кино-наезд — 1.00 → 1.05 about the point, curve Cinematic; Резкий наезд — at the moment 1.00 → 1.15 in 0.12 s (timeline seconds), curve Snap, held to the end of the clip; Плавная панорама — a 6 % shift of the frame with scale 1.06 (no borders), curve Smooth; Ручная камера — `Noise1D` on x, y (0.6 % of the frame) and rotation (0.4°), ~0.7 Hz, "no borders" scale, seed from the effect id. Every motion effect keeps the picture covering the frame it filled ("без полей").
- Curves (§3, saved semantics — never change one, add a variant): `Cinematic` = cubic-bezier(0.45, 0, 0.2, 1); `Snap` = `(1 − 2^(−10u)) / (1 − 2^(−10))`; `Gentle` = `(1 − cos πu) / 2`; all pinned by tests at ¼, ½, ¾ and 0 → 0, 1 → 1.
- `Noise1D { seed, frequency, amplitude }`: value noise, lattice values from a PCG32 (XSH-RR) step over an integer hash, smoothstep blend; pinned by tests so a GPU port matches.
- Defaults (§5): Точка = centre of the clip's picture; Момент = the source frame under the playhead at the moment of applying (a drop: the drop point).
- One instance of a recipe per clip (§2): `AddEffect` of a recipe the clip has replaces it **in place** — same id and list position, new parameters, enabled, progress `(0, 1)`, its `fx:` tracks dropped. Removing an effect removes its `fx:` tracks in the same undo entry.
- Forward compatibility (§2): an unknown recipe, a recipe version newer than the registry's, an unknown `ParamValue` variant, an unknown param of a known recipe and unknown `AppliedEffect` fields survive load → save verbatim and are never evaluated; the inspector shows «Недоступен (создан в более новой версии)». Such an effect cannot be added or updated, only moved or removed (§7).
- `FORMAT_VERSION` 2 → 3 **here, once**. `io::migrate` turns the old never-evaluated `effects: [{id, kind, enabled, params}]` into `AppliedEffect`s (`recipe = kind`); transitions keep `kind` in format 3 — E3 converts them by content (a transition with `kind` and no `recipe`) without another bump. Migration never panics on malformed input: `get_mut(..).and_then(as_*_mut)`, never `IndexMut`; the upgraded document is stamped format 3.
- `EditEngine` records an undo entry for **every** successful command, also one that changed nothing: commands return `EditError::NoOp` when nothing changes, and callers check "nothing would change" before sending. `Batch` swallows `NoOp`, so every command must be atomic on its own (R11): validate fully before mutating.
- One gesture = at most one undo entry: a slider drag commits once on release (`LiveEdit`: live, unrecorded changes are rolled back by any command, undo, redo or AI plan undo; a cancelled drag commits); applying to several clips is one `Batch`.
- The hover preview lives in app state (`PreviewOverride`) and is applied only to the preview's snapshot copy (`ProjectScenes::new_with`): the project, its history, the "modified" flag and autosave are never touched (§6.1).
- `kadr-scene`, `kadr-render`, `kadr-playback` are not modified: E1 composes a `PlacementDelta` into the existing `Placement`.
- Slint: a struct reaches Rust only if listed in `app.slint`'s `export { … }`; interactive repeaters keep stable models (`util::sync_rows_by_key`, `util::reuse_rows`, `inspector::same_or_new`) — a rebuilt row loses a press in flight; `ContextMenuArea` rebuilds its items on every show; never use ◀ ▶-style glyphs (Windows draws them as emoji) — `Icon` paths (viewbox 24), mirrored filled paths wound the same way.
- Every user-facing string is in both `crates/i18n/locales/en.json` and `ru.json` (`crates/i18n/tests/sources.rs` and the library-strings test of Task 9 enforce it); Slint `Tr.t("key")`, Rust `kadr_i18n::{t, tf}`; seconds via `util::fmt_secs`; history labels in the catalog's noun style («Удаление эффекта: Плавный наезд»). New catalog entries go before the closing `}` of each file, keeping the JSON valid.
- MCP house style (`apps/editor/src/mcp_anim.rs`): unknown fields rejected per op, millisecond arguments integers within ±10 000 000 000 before `Time::from_millis`, value ranges checked, "no change" rejected, every rejection names its reason.
- Run tests with `cargo test --workspace --exclude kadr-mcp` and `cargo test -p kadr-mcp`. A headless Kadr may lock `target\debug\kadr.exe`: a build that fails only at replacing it is not a code failure (stop the instance, rebuild).
- Live acceptance is run by the controller through `kadr-mcp` (subagents' kadr MCP calls are refused; kadr-mcp cannot inject arrow keys). Work on branch `feature/effects`. Commits end with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.

**Documented refinements of the spec** (decided here; report them to the owner):
1. Effect-parameter tracks are keyed by `TrackKey::Fx(FxParam { effect, key })` next to `TrackKey::Clip(AnimParam)` instead of a variant `AnimParam::Effect { effect, key }`: `AnimParam` stays the `Copy` enum of the 13 clip properties, so `AnimParam::ALL` / `index()` (the inspector's `KeyState` rows) keep their meaning. The JSON key is the spec's `fx:<effect-id>:<key>`.
2. `CompileCtx` = the spec's fields plus `speed` (source seconds per timeline second, for Punch In's 0.12 timeline seconds) and `aspect` (picture w / h, for Handheld's no-borders scale); `clip_secs` = visible duration ÷ (to − from), so `progress × clip_secs` is the effect's own clock, continuous across a split (Handheld's noise time). `Compiled` in E1 = `{ placement, effects }`; overlays, transition builds and audio ops join in E2–E4.
3. `ParamValue::Color` is straight RGBA `[f64; 4]` (`kadr-project` does not depend on `kadr-scene`); no E1 recipe uses it.
4. The Moment default when the applying time is off the clip (click-apply with the playhead elsewhere, MCP, AI — their validation has no playhead): the clip's visible middle.
5. `UpdateEffect.params` is a map (empty = unchanged) instead of an `Option`.
6. A delta's anchor (Точка) is a fraction of the clip's picture (the content rectangle before crop); deltas compose in list order inside the clip's own placement, so a picture-in-picture clip zooms as a whole.
7. Smooth Pan's direction is the camera's: «вправо» slides the picture left.
8. The tab hides categories without recipes (E1 shows «Все · Движение»); cards scale to at least two columns, their thumbnails are rendered at 160 × 90.
9. The «До» (bypass) view drops motion effects too, not only colour.
10. The preview's point marker shows for the effect last interacted with in the inspector (and while picking).

## Review Focus

1. **Hover preview leaking into the project** — hovering a card and leaving (or switching tabs, changing the selection, opening a project) must leave the project, history, "modified" flag and autosave untouched and restore the preview exactly; an edit while hovering never bakes the hover in (tests: Task 5 `bypass_also_drops_motion_effects` / `new_with_changes_the_snapshot_only`, Task 9 `the_hover_override_lives_in_the_snapshot_only`).
2. **An effect on a split or trimmed clip jumping** — after a split every frame of both halves places the picture where the whole clip did (also at 2× speed and for Handheld's noise); a trim stretches the effect over the new visible clip instead of cutting it (tests: Task 5 `a_split_never_moves_the_picture`, `trimming_stretches_the_effect_over_the_new_visible_clip`; Task 14 `a_split_keeps_every_pixel`).
3. **A drag-applied effect landing on the wrong clip** — a drop applies only to the clip under the pointer on a real row: below the last track, in a gap, on a sound clip or a locked track nothing is applied (no fallback to row 0, which `timeline_ui::drop_target` does for media) (test: Task 9 `a_drop_hits_only_the_clip_under_the_pointer_that_fits`).
4. **A Point or Moment outside the visible clip** — a preview click outside the clip's visible picture keeps pick mode with a hint; the point maps through the placement as shown (zoomed); a Moment before the visible start is already done on the first frame, one after the end never comes; MCP/AI `at_ms` outside the clip is rejected (tests: Task 5 `the_punch_moment_stays_on_its_source_frame`, Task 7 `params_from_json_rejects_a_moment_off_the_clip`, Task 12 `a_click_off_the_picture_picks_nothing`).
5. **Unknown recipe round-trip** — a project from a newer Kadr (unknown recipe, newer version, unknown param variant, extra fields, `fx:` tracks of that effect) loads, is never evaluated, saves equal JSON values, can be removed (with its tracks) but not changed (tests: Task 1 `an_effect_from_a_newer_kadr_survives_load_and_save`, Task 2 `removing_an_effect_drops_its_tracks_readable_or_not`, Task 5 `zero_strength_disabled_and_unknown_effects_change_nothing`, Task 6 `an_unknown_effect_is_kept_moved_or_removed_but_not_changed`).

### Task 1: Effect model and project format 3 (`kadr-project`)

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/project/src/effects.rs`
- Modify: `crates/project/src/lib.rs` (module + re-export)
- Modify: `crates/project/src/sequence.rs` — `Clip.effects` becomes `Vec<AppliedEffect>`; the old `Effect` struct and the now unused `EffectId` import go
- Modify: `crates/project/src/model.rs` (`FORMAT_VERSION = 3`), `crates/project/src/io.rs` (migration, tests)
- Modify: `apps/editor/src/inspector.rs:247` (it listed `e.kind` of the old struct)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `kadr_core::EffectId`, `crate::{SourceTime, MAX_KEY_FLICKS}` (animation.rs).
- Produces:
  - `pub enum ParamValue { Number(f64), Choice(String), Point { x: f64, y: f64 }, Color([f64; 4]), Moment(SourceTime), Unknown(serde_json::Value) }` — JSON `{"number": v}`, `{"choice": s}`, `{"point": {"x", "y"}}`, `{"color": [r, g, b, a]}`, `{"moment": flicks}`, anything else kept verbatim as `Unknown`; `ParamValue::as_number(&self) -> Option<f64>`.
  - `pub struct AppliedEffect { pub id: EffectId, pub recipe: String, pub version: u16, pub params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, pub enabled: bool, pub progress: (f64, f64), pub extra: BTreeMap<String, serde_json::Value> }` (serde: `version` default 1, `enabled` default true, `progress` default `(0, 1)` and not written when full, `extra` = flattened unknown fields); `AppliedEffect::new(recipe: impl Into<String>, version: u16, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>) -> Self`, `progress_at(&self, frac: f64) -> f64`, `split_progress(&self, frac: f64) -> ((f64, f64), (f64, f64))`, `number(&self, key: &str) -> Option<f64>`.
  - `pub fn deserialize_effects<'de, D>(d: D) -> Result<Vec<AppliedEffect>, D::Error>` (lenient list).
  - `Clip { …, pub effects: Vec<AppliedEffect>, … }`; `Clip::effect(&self, EffectId) -> Option<&AppliedEffect>`, `Clip::effect_mut(&mut self, EffectId) -> Option<&mut AppliedEffect>`, `Clip::effect_of(&self, recipe: &str) -> Option<&AppliedEffect>`.
  - `FORMAT_VERSION == 3`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — create `crates/project/src/effects.rs` with only this test module (the types come in Step 3) and add `pub mod effects;` / `pub use effects::*;` to `crates/project/src/lib.rs` (after `decisions`):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_core::Time;
    use serde_json::json;

    #[test]
    fn param_values_are_saved_in_their_tagged_form_and_read_back() {
        let cases = [
            (ParamValue::Number(0.6), json!({"number": 0.6})),
            (ParamValue::Choice("in".into()), json!({"choice": "in"})),
            (ParamValue::Point { x: 0.25, y: 0.75 }, json!({"point": {"x": 0.25, "y": 0.75}})),
            (ParamValue::Color([1.0, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0]), json!({"color": [1.0, 0.5, 0.0, 1.0]})),
            (ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(2))), json!({"moment": 1_411_200_000})),
        ];
        for (v, j) in cases {
            assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&v).unwrap(), j, "{v:?}");
            assert_eq!(serde_json::from_value::<ParamValue>(j).unwrap(), v);
        }
    }

    /// Spec §2: an unknown variant (or a known one in a shape this build
    /// cannot read) is kept as it was and never used.
    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_or_malformed_param_variant_is_kept_verbatim() {
        for j in [
            json!({"curve": {"bezier": [0.1, 0.2]}}),
            json!({"number": "x"}),
            json!({"point": {"x": 0.5}}),
            json!({"point": {"x": 0.5, "y": 0.5, "z": 1.0}}),
            json!({"moment": i64::MAX}),
            json!({"number": 1.5, "unit": "s"}),
            json!(5),
        ] {
            let v: ParamValue = serde_json::from_value(j.clone()).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(v, ParamValue::Unknown(j.clone()));
            assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&v).unwrap(), j);
        }
    }

    /// Review focus 5: a recipe, a parameter variant and a field from a
    /// newer Kadr survive load → save unchanged.
    #[test]
    fn an_effect_from_a_newer_kadr_survives_load_and_save() {
        let future = json!({
            "id": EffectId::new(), "recipe": "glitch_v2", "version": 4, "enabled": true,
            "params": {"amount": {"number": 0.3}, "curve": {"bezier": [0.1, 0.2]}},
            "mask": {"shape": "oval", "feather": 0.2}
        });
        let e: AppliedEffect = serde_json::from_value(future.clone()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!((e.recipe.as_str(), e.version, e.progress), ("glitch_v2", 4, (0.0, 1.0)));
        assert!(matches!(e.params["curve"], ParamValue::Unknown(_)));
        assert_eq!(e.extra["mask"], future["mask"]);
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap(), future);
    }

    /// Spec §2: unknown parameters of a known recipe are kept too.
    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_param_of_a_known_recipe_is_kept() {
        let stored = json!({"id": EffectId::new(), "recipe": "smooth_zoom", "version": 1, "enabled": true, "params": {"strength": {"number": 0.5}, "future_amount": {"number": 2.5}}});
        let e: AppliedEffect = serde_json::from_value(stored.clone()).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(e.number("future_amount"), Some(2.5));
        assert_eq!(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap(), stored);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_partial_range_is_written_and_a_full_one_is_not() {
        let mut e = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::new());
        assert!(serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap().get("progress").is_none());
        e.progress = (0.25, 1.0);
        let v = serde_json::to_value(&e).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(v["progress"], json!([0.25, 1.0]));
        assert_eq!(serde_json::from_value::<AppliedEffect>(v).unwrap(), e);
    }

    #[derive(serde::Deserialize)]
    struct Holder {
        #[serde(deserialize_with = "deserialize_effects")]
        effects: Vec<AppliedEffect>,
    }

    /// Bad effect data never fails the project: entries that are not effects
    /// at all are dropped, ids stay unique per clip.
    #[test]
    fn effects_load_leniently_and_keep_ids_unique() {
        let id = EffectId::new();
        let list = json!([7, {"recipe": "x"}, {"id": id, "recipe": "smooth_zoom"}, {"id": id, "recipe": "handheld"},
                          {"id": EffectId::new(), "recipe": "a", "progress": "bad"}]);
        let h: Holder = serde_json::from_value(json!({ "effects": list })).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(h.effects.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(h.effects[0].recipe, "smooth_zoom", "the first of an id wins");
        assert_eq!((h.effects[0].version, h.effects[0].enabled), (1, true), "defaults");
        for bad in [json!(null), json!("x"), json!({"a": 1})] {
            let h: Holder = serde_json::from_value(json!({ "effects": bad })).unwrap();
            assert!(h.effects.is_empty());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_split_cuts_the_progress_range_where_the_cut_is() {
        let mut e = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::new());
        assert_eq!(e.split_progress(0.25), ((0.0, 0.25), (0.25, 1.0)));
        e.progress = (0.5, 1.0);
        assert_eq!(e.progress_at(0.5), 0.75);
        assert_eq!(e.split_progress(0.5), ((0.5, 0.75), (0.75, 1.0)));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_clip_finds_its_effects_by_id_and_recipe() {
        let mut c = crate::Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "c", kadr_core::TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(1)), Time::ZERO);
        let e = AppliedEffect::new("handheld", 1, BTreeMap::new());
        let id = e.id;
        c.effects.push(e);
        assert_eq!(c.effect(id).map(|e| e.recipe.as_str()), Some("handheld"));
        assert_eq!(c.effect_of("handheld").map(|e| e.id), Some(id));
        c.effect_mut(id).unwrap().enabled = false;
        assert!(!c.effects[0].enabled);
        assert!(c.effect(EffectId::new()).is_none() && c.effect_of("smooth_zoom").is_none());
    }
}
```

Add to the `io.rs` test module (and replace the test `a_format_2_file_is_too_new_for_a_format_1_reader` by the first one below):

```rust
    #[test]
    fn a_format_3_file_is_too_new_for_a_format_2_reader() {
        assert_eq!(FORMAT_VERSION, 3);
        let json = format!(r#"{{"format_version": {}}}"#, FORMAT_VERSION + 1);
        assert!(matches!(parse(json.as_bytes(), Path::new("x.kadr")).unwrap_err(), ProjectIoError::TooNew { .. }));
    }

    /// Format 2 clip JSON with the old, never-evaluated effects list.
    fn v2_project() -> serde_json::Value {
        let p = crate::Project::new("old");
        let mut v = serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap();
        v["format_version"] = serde_json::json!(2);
        let f = |s: i64| s * kadr_core::FLICKS_PER_SECOND;
        let clip = serde_json::json!({
            "id": kadr_core::ClipId::new(), "asset": kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "name": "c",
            "source_in": 0, "source_out": f(10), "timeline_in": 0, "timeline_out": f(10),
            "effects": [
                {"id": kadr_core::EffectId::new(), "kind": "blur", "enabled": false, "params": {"radius": 4.5}},
                {"id": kadr_core::EffectId::new(), "kind": "smooth_zoom", "enabled": true, "params": {"strength": 1}}
            ]
        });
        v["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["clips"] = serde_json::json!([clip]);
        v
    }

    #[test]
    fn format_2_effects_migrate_to_applied_effects() {
        let p = parse(&serde_json::to_vec(&v2_project()).unwrap(), Path::new("old.kadr")).unwrap();
        let fx = &p.sequence().tracks[0].clips[0].effects;
        assert_eq!(fx.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!((fx[0].recipe.as_str(), fx[0].enabled, fx[0].version, fx[0].progress), ("blur", false, 1, (0.0, 1.0)), "an unknown kind is an unknown recipe: kept");
        assert_eq!(fx[0].params["radius"], crate::ParamValue::Number(4.5));
        assert_eq!(fx[1].params["strength"], crate::ParamValue::Number(1.0));
        let saved = serde_json::to_value(&p).unwrap();
        assert!(saved["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["clips"][0]["effects"][0].get("kind").is_none(), "the old field is never written");
        assert_eq!(saved["format_version"], serde_json::json!(3));
    }

    /// The E3 hook: format 3 keeps transitions as they are (E3 converts a
    /// transition with `kind` and no `recipe` by content, no new format).
    #[test]
    fn transitions_keep_their_kind_in_format_3() {
        let mut v = v2_project();
        let track = v["sequences"][0]["tracks"][0]["id"].clone();
        v["sequences"][0]["transitions"] = serde_json::json!([{"id": kadr_core::TransitionId::new(), "kind": "dip_to_black", "track": track, "at": 0, "duration": 100}]);
        let p = parse(&serde_json::to_vec(&v).unwrap(), Path::new("old.kadr")).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(p.sequence().transitions[0].kind, crate::TransitionKind::DipToBlack);
    }

    /// R25: a garbage old effects list never panics the migration.
    #[test]
    fn malformed_format_2_effects_never_panic() {
        for effects in [r#""x""#, "[7]", r#"[{"kind": 5}]"#, r#"[{"id": "nope", "kind": "a"}]"#, r#"[{"kind": "a", "params": [1]}]"#, "null"] {
            let json = format!(r#"{{"format_version":2,"sequences":[{{"tracks":[{{"clips":[{{"effects":{effects}}}]}}]}}]}}"#);
            let r = parse(json.as_bytes(), Path::new("bad.kadr"));
            // The rest of this skeleton is not a project: a parse error, never a panic.
            assert!(matches!(r, Err(ProjectIoError::Parse { .. })), "{effects}: {r:?}");
        }
    }
```

```rust
//! Effects applied to a clip (effects spec §2). A recipe is named by id;
//! what it does lives in `kadr-effects`. Everything here is data: a recipe,
//! a parameter variant or a field this build does not know is kept
//! verbatim and never evaluated (spec §2 «Прямая совместимость»).

use crate::{Clip, SourceTime, MAX_KEY_FLICKS};
use kadr_core::{EffectId, Time};
use serde::de::Deserializer;
use serde::ser::{SerializeMap, Serializer};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::{json, Value};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// One parameter value of an applied effect. The recipe's `ParamDef`
/// (`kadr-effects`) says which variant a parameter takes.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum ParamValue {
    /// Strength 0..1, a duration in seconds…
    Number(f64),
    /// One of the recipe's options: a direction, a preset, a loudness target.
    Choice(String),
    /// A point of the clip's picture as fractions 0..1 (0, 0 = top left).
    Point { x: f64, y: f64 },
    /// Straight RGBA, components 0..1.
    Color([f64; 4]),
    /// A moment of the source media («Момент» of Punch In): source-bound.
    Moment(SourceTime),
    /// A variant from a newer Kadr (or a shape this build cannot read): kept, never used.
    Unknown(Value),
}

impl ParamValue {
    pub fn as_number(&self) -> Option<f64> {
        match self {
            ParamValue::Number(n) => Some(*n),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// A known variant, or `None` (the caller keeps the JSON as `Unknown`).
    fn from_json(v: &Value) -> Option<ParamValue> {
        let o = v.as_object().filter(|o| o.len() == 1)?;
        let (k, p) = o.iter().next()?;
        Some(match k.as_str() {
            "number" => ParamValue::Number(p.as_f64().filter(|n| n.is_finite())?),
            "choice" => ParamValue::Choice(p.as_str()?.to_string()),
            "point" => {
                let o = p.as_object().filter(|o| o.len() == 2)?;
                ParamValue::Point { x: o.get("x")?.as_f64()?, y: o.get("y")?.as_f64()? }
            }
            "color" => {
                let a = p.as_array().filter(|a| a.len() == 4)?;
                let c: Vec<f64> = a.iter().map(Value::as_f64).collect::<Option<_>>()?;
                ParamValue::Color([c[0], c[1], c[2], c[3]])
            }
            // A moment beyond ±MAX_KEY_FLICKS would overflow the time conversions: kept, not used.
            "moment" => ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time(p.as_i64().filter(|f| f.unsigned_abs() <= MAX_KEY_FLICKS)?))),
            _ => return None,
        })
    }
}

impl Serialize for ParamValue {
    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, s: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
        let (k, v) = match self {
            ParamValue::Unknown(v) => return v.serialize(s),
            ParamValue::Number(n) => ("number", json!(n)),
            ParamValue::Choice(c) => ("choice", json!(c)),
            ParamValue::Point { x, y } => ("point", json!({"x": x, "y": y})),
            ParamValue::Color(c) => ("color", json!(c)),
            ParamValue::Moment(m) => ("moment", json!(m.0.flicks())),
        };
        let mut m = s.serialize_map(Some(1))?;
        m.serialize_entry(k, &v)?;
        m.end()
    }
}

impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for ParamValue {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
        let v = Value::deserialize(d)?;
        Ok(ParamValue::from_json(&v).unwrap_or(ParamValue::Unknown(v)))
    }
}

fn yes() -> bool {
    true
}
fn one() -> u16 {
    1
}
fn full() -> (f64, f64) {
    (0.0, 1.0)
}
fn is_full(p: &(f64, f64)) -> bool {
    *p == full()
}

/// An effect on a clip (spec §2). The clip's `effects` list is the order
/// of application.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct AppliedEffect {
    pub id: EffectId,
    /// Registry id, e.g. "smooth_zoom" (`kadr-effects`).
    pub recipe: String,
    /// The recipe's `compile` version this effect was made with.
    #[serde(default = "one")]
    pub version: u16,
    #[serde(default)]
    pub params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>,
    #[serde(default = "yes")]
    pub enabled: bool,
    /// Spec §1: the part of the effect's progress this clip shows.
    #[serde(default = "full", skip_serializing_if = "is_full")]
    pub progress: (f64, f64),
    /// Fields from a newer Kadr, kept verbatim.
    #[serde(flatten)]
    pub extra: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
}

impl AppliedEffect {
    pub fn new(recipe: impl Into<String>, version: u16, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>) -> Self {
        AppliedEffect { id: EffectId::new(), recipe: recipe.into(), version, params, enabled: true, progress: full(), extra: BTreeMap::new() }
    }

    /// Spec §1: the progress at `frac` (0..1) of the clip's visible length.
    pub fn progress_at(&self, frac: f64) -> f64 {
        let (from, to) = self.progress;
        from + (to - from) * frac
    }

    /// The halves' ranges for a split at `frac` of the visible length:
    /// `(from, c)` and `(c, to)`, `c = progress_at(frac)` — the picture at
    /// the joint stays as it was (spec §1).
    pub fn split_progress(&self, frac: f64) -> ((f64, f64), (f64, f64)) {
        let c = self.progress_at(frac);
        ((self.progress.0, c), (c, self.progress.1))
    }

    /// A `Number` parameter's stored value.
    pub fn number(&self, key: &str) -> Option<f64> {
        self.params.get(key).and_then(ParamValue::as_number)
    }
}

/// `Clip::effects` from a file; never fails the load. An entry that is not
/// an effect at all (not an object, no `id` or `recipe`, a field of the
/// wrong type) is dropped with a warning, and so is a later entry with an
/// id already seen (ids are unique per clip). Unknown recipes and fields
/// survive (spec §2).
pub fn deserialize_effects<'de, D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Vec<AppliedEffect>, D::Error> {
    let Value::Array(items) = Value::deserialize(d)? else {
        tracing::warn!("ignoring clip effects that are not a list");
        return Ok(vec![]);
    };
    let mut out: Vec<AppliedEffect> = Vec::with_capacity(items.len());
    for item in items {
        match serde_json::from_value::<AppliedEffect>(item) {
            Ok(e) if out.iter().any(|x| x.id == e.id) => tracing::warn!(id = %e.id, "dropping a duplicate clip effect"),
            Ok(e) => out.push(e),
            Err(err) => tracing::warn!(error = %err, "dropping an unreadable clip effect"),
        }
    }
    Ok(out)
}

impl Clip {
    pub fn effect(&self, id: EffectId) -> Option<&AppliedEffect> {
        self.effects.iter().find(|e| e.id == id)
    }
    pub fn effect_mut(&mut self, id: EffectId) -> Option<&mut AppliedEffect> {
        self.effects.iter_mut().find(|e| e.id == id)
    }
    /// The clip's effect of `recipe` (one instance per recipe, spec §2).
    pub fn effect_of(&self, recipe: &str) -> Option<&AppliedEffect> {
        self.effects.iter().find(|e| e.recipe == recipe)
    }
}
```

In `crates/project/src/io.rs` replace `migrate` and add two helpers (keep `migrate_clip_keyframes` as it is):

```rust
/// Upgrades older JSON layouts in place. Format 1 is the first release;
/// format 2 replaced the never-evaluated string-keyed `keyframes` with
/// source-bound `animation` (keyframes spec §2.2); format 3 replaced the
/// never-evaluated `effects` with `AppliedEffect`s (effects spec §2).
/// Transitions keep their `kind` in format 3: stage E3 converts a
/// transition that has `kind` and no `recipe` by content, in this same
/// function, without another format bump.
fn migrate(mut value: serde_json::Value, from: u32) -> serde_json::Value {
    if from < 2 {
        for_each_clip(&mut value, migrate_clip_keyframes);
    }
    if from < 3 {
        for_each_clip(&mut value, migrate_clip_effects);
        // The upgraded document is a format-3 document in memory too.
        if let Some(obj) = value.as_object_mut() {
            obj.insert("format_version".into(), 3.into());
        }
    }
    value
}

/// Every clip object of every sequence; malformed levels are skipped
/// (the parse after the migration reports them).
fn for_each_clip(value: &mut serde_json::Value, mut f: impl FnMut(&mut serde_json::Value)) {
    use serde_json::Value;
    let Some(seqs) = value.get_mut("sequences").and_then(Value::as_array_mut) else { return };
    for clip in seqs
        .iter_mut()
        .flat_map(|s| s.get_mut("tracks").and_then(Value::as_array_mut).into_iter().flatten())
        .flat_map(|t| t.get_mut("clips").and_then(Value::as_array_mut).into_iter().flatten())
    {
        f(clip);
    }
}

/// Format 2 → 3: `effects: [{id, kind, enabled, params: {name: number}}]`
/// becomes `AppliedEffect`s — `recipe = kind`, numbers become
/// `{"number": v}`, version 1, full progress. An unknown `kind` is an
/// unknown recipe (kept, not run); an entry that is not an object stays as
/// it is and the lenient loader drops it.
fn migrate_clip_effects(clip: &mut serde_json::Value) {
    use serde_json::{json, Value};
    let Some(list) = clip.get_mut("effects").and_then(Value::as_array_mut) else { return };
    for e in list.iter_mut() {
        let Some(o) = e.as_object_mut() else { continue };
        if let Some(kind) = o.remove("kind") {
            o.entry("recipe").or_insert(kind);
        }
        if let Some(params) = o.get_mut("params").and_then(Value::as_object_mut) {
            for v in params.values_mut() {
                if v.is_number() {
                    *v = json!({ "number": v.clone() });
                }
            }
        }
        o.entry("version").or_insert(json!(1));
    }
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn effect_parameter_keyframes_need_their_effect_and_keep_its_static_value() {
    let (mut p, mut e, id) = setup();
    let fx = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, [("strength".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.6))].into());
    let fid = fx.id;
    let k = TrackKey::fx(fid, "strength");
    let set = EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: id, param: k.clone(), at: SourceTime(s(102)), value: 0.2, ease: None };
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, set.clone()), Err(EditError::EffectNotFound), "no effect, no track");
    assert_eq!(EditCommand::error_key(&EditError::EffectNotFound), "err.edit.effect_not_found");
    let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(id).unwrap();
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci].effects.push(fx);
    e.execute(&mut p, set).unwrap();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveKeyframe { clip: id, param: k.clone(), at: SourceTime(s(102)), keep: Some(0.4) }).unwrap();
    let c = p.sequence().clip(id).unwrap();
    assert!(!c.animation.is_animated(&k));
    assert_eq!(c.effect(fid).unwrap().params["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.4), "the last key leaves the picture as it was");
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: id, param: k.clone(), at: SourceTime(s(103)), value: 0.1, ease: None }).unwrap();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::ClearAnimation { clip: id, statics: vec![(k.clone(), 0.7)] }).unwrap();
    let c = p.sequence().clip(id).unwrap();
    assert!(!c.animation.is_animated(&k));
    assert_eq!(c.effect(fid).unwrap().params["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.7));
}
```

```rust
/// A numeric parameter of an effect on the clip (effects spec §2).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub struct FxParam {
    pub effect: EffectId,
    pub key: String,
}

/// What a keyframe track animates: a clip property or an effect parameter.
/// Clip properties sort first, in `AnimParam::ALL` order.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, PartialOrd, Ord, Hash)]
pub enum TrackKey {
    Clip(AnimParam),
    Fx(FxParam),
}

impl From<AnimParam> for TrackKey {
    fn from(p: AnimParam) -> Self {
        TrackKey::Clip(p)
    }
}

impl From<FxParam> for TrackKey {
    fn from(f: FxParam) -> Self {
        TrackKey::Fx(f)
    }
}

impl From<&TrackKey> for TrackKey {
    fn from(k: &TrackKey) -> Self {
        k.clone()
    }
}

impl TrackKey {
    pub fn fx(effect: EffectId, key: impl Into<String>) -> TrackKey {
        TrackKey::Fx(FxParam { effect, key: key.into() })
    }

    /// The JSON key of the track: `scale`, `fx:<effect-id>:strength`.
    pub fn key_string(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            TrackKey::Clip(p) => p.key().to_string(),
            TrackKey::Fx(f) => format!("fx:{}:{}", f.effect, f.key),
        }
    }

    /// Inverse of [`TrackKey::key_string`]; `None` for keys this build cannot run.
    pub fn parse(s: &str) -> Option<TrackKey> {
        if let Some(rest) = s.strip_prefix("fx:") {
            let (id, key) = rest.split_once(':')?;
            let effect = EffectId::parse(id)?;
            return (!key.is_empty()).then(|| TrackKey::fx(effect, key));
        }
        AnimParam::from_key(s).map(TrackKey::Clip)
    }

    pub fn as_clip(&self) -> Option<AnimParam> {
        match self {
            TrackKey::Clip(p) => Some(*p),
            TrackKey::Fx(_) => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn as_fx(&self) -> Option<&FxParam> {
        match self {
            TrackKey::Fx(f) => Some(f),
            TrackKey::Clip(_) => None,
        }
    }
}

impl Serialize for TrackKey {
    fn serialize<S: Serializer>(&self, s: S) -> Result<S::Ok, S::Error> {
        s.serialize_str(&self.key_string())
    }
}

impl<'de> Deserialize<'de> for TrackKey {
    fn deserialize<D: Deserializer<'de>>(d: D) -> Result<Self, D::Error> {
        let s = String::deserialize(d)?;
        TrackKey::parse(&s).ok_or_else(|| serde::de::Error::custom(format!("unknown animation track {s}")))
    }
}
```

Replace the `Animation` struct and its `impl` block (everything from `/// Per-parameter keyframe tracks.` to the end of `impl Animation`) with:

```rust
/// Keyframe tracks of clip properties and effect parameters. Invariant:
/// every stored track is non-empty and sorted by strictly increasing `at`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, Default, PartialEq)]
pub struct Animation {
    tracks: BTreeMap<TrackKey, Vec<Keyframe>>,
    unknown: BTreeMap<String, Value>,
}

impl Animation {
    /// No executable track (unknown ones may still be stored).
    pub fn is_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.tracks.is_empty()
    }
    /// Nothing to write (no known and no unknown tracks).
    pub fn is_stored_empty(&self) -> bool {
        self.tracks.is_empty() && self.unknown.is_empty()
    }
    /// The animated clip properties, in [`AnimParam::ALL`] order (effect
    /// parameters: [`Animation::keys`]).
    pub fn params(&self) -> Vec<AnimParam> {
        self.tracks.keys().filter_map(TrackKey::as_clip).collect()
    }
    /// Every animated track: clip properties first, then effect parameters.
    pub fn keys(&self) -> Vec<TrackKey> {
        self.tracks.keys().cloned().collect()
    }
    /// The keyframes of `p` sorted by time; empty when `p` is not animated.
    pub fn track(&self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>) -> &[Keyframe] {
        self.tracks.get(&p.into()).map_or(&[], Vec::as_slice)
    }
    /// `p` has at least one keyframe.
    pub fn is_animated(&self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>) -> bool {
        self.tracks.contains_key(&p.into())
    }
    /// Number of tracks kept verbatim because this build cannot run them.
    pub fn unknown_len(&self) -> usize {
        self.unknown.len()
    }

    /// Adds a keyframe or, at an existing time, replaces its value (and its
    /// ease when one is given). A new keyframe inside a segment inherits
    /// that segment's ease; elsewhere it gets `ease` or the default.
    pub fn set_key(&mut self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, at: SourceTime, value: f64, ease: Option<Ease>) {
        let p = p.into();
        if !self.tracks.contains_key(&p) {
            // The user's edit supersedes an unreadable stored track of this key.
            self.unknown.remove(&p.key_string());
        }
        let keys = self.tracks.entry(p).or_default();
        match keys.binary_search_by_key(&at, |k| k.at) {
            Ok(i) => {
                keys[i].value = value;
                if let Some(e) = ease {
                    keys[i].ease = e;
                }
            }
            Err(i) => {
                let inherited = if i > 0 && i < keys.len() { keys[i - 1].ease.clone() } else { Ease::default() };
                keys.insert(i, Keyframe { at, value, ease: ease.unwrap_or(inherited) });
            }
        }
    }

    /// The animated value of `p` at source time `s`: the first/last
    /// keyframe's value outside the track, the eased blend between two.
    /// `None` when `p` is not animated.
    pub fn value_at(&self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, s: SourceTime) -> Option<f64> {
        let keys = self.tracks.get(&p.into())?;
        let i = keys.partition_point(|k| k.at <= s);
        Some(match (i.checked_sub(1).map(|j| &keys[j]), keys.get(i)) {
            (None, Some(first)) => first.value,
            (Some(last), None) => last.value,
            (Some(k0), Some(k1)) => {
                let u = (s.0 - k0.at.0).flicks() as f64 / (k1.at.0 - k0.at.0).flicks() as f64;
                k0.value + (k1.value - k0.value) * k0.ease.curve().apply(u)
            }
            (None, None) => return None,
        })
    }

    /// Removes the keyframe at `at`; the track goes away with its last key.
    pub fn remove_key(&mut self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, at: SourceTime) -> Option<Keyframe> {
        let p = p.into();
        let keys = self.tracks.get_mut(&p)?;
        let i = keys.binary_search_by_key(&at, |k| k.at).ok()?;
        let k = keys.remove(i);
        if keys.is_empty() {
            self.tracks.remove(&p);
        }
        Some(k)
    }

    /// Moves the keyframe at `from` to `to` (keeping value and ease).
    pub fn move_key(&mut self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, from: SourceTime, to: SourceTime) -> Result<(), AnimError> {
        let keys = self.tracks.get_mut(&p.into()).ok_or(AnimError::NoKeyframe)?;
        let i = keys.binary_search_by_key(&from, |k| k.at).map_err(|_| AnimError::NoKeyframe)?;
        if from == to {
            return Ok(());
        }
        if keys.iter().any(|k| k.at == to) {
            return Err(AnimError::Collision);
        }
        keys[i].at = to;
        keys.sort_by_key(|k| k.at);
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Sets the curve of the segment after the keyframe at `at`.
    pub fn set_ease(&mut self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, at: SourceTime, ease: Ease) -> Result<(), AnimError> {
        let keys = self.tracks.get_mut(&p.into()).ok_or(AnimError::NoKeyframe)?;
        let i = keys.binary_search_by_key(&at, |k| k.at).map_err(|_| AnimError::NoKeyframe)?;
        keys[i].ease = ease;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Drops the whole track of `p`, returning its keyframes.
    pub fn clear(&mut self, p: impl Into<TrackKey>) -> Vec<Keyframe> {
        self.tracks.remove(&p.into()).unwrap_or_default()
    }

    /// Moves every keyframe of every known track by `delta`.
    pub fn shift_all(&mut self, delta: Time) {
        for keys in self.tracks.values_mut() {
            for k in keys {
                k.at = SourceTime(k.at.0 + delta);
            }
        }
    }

    /// Every distinct keyframe time across the known tracks, sorted.
    pub fn times(&self) -> Vec<SourceTime> {
        let mut t: Vec<SourceTime> = self.tracks.values().flatten().map(|k| k.at).collect();
        t.sort();
        t.dedup();
        t
    }

    /// The tracks with a keyframe exactly at `at`.
    pub fn keys_at(&self, at: SourceTime) -> Vec<TrackKey> {
        self.tracks.iter().filter(|(_, keys)| keys.binary_search_by_key(&at, |k| k.at).is_ok()).map(|(p, _)| p.clone()).collect()
    }

    /// Drops every track of `effect`'s parameters — unreadable ones kept
    /// verbatim too (removing an effect takes its tracks, effects spec §2).
    /// `true` when something went.
    pub fn remove_effect(&mut self, effect: EffectId) -> bool {
        let before = self.tracks.len() + self.unknown.len();
        self.tracks.retain(|k, _| k.as_fx().is_none_or(|f| f.effect != effect));
        let prefix = format!("fx:{effect}:");
        self.unknown.retain(|k, _| !k.starts_with(&prefix));
        before != self.tracks.len() + self.unknown.len()
    }
}
```

In `crates/project/src/sequence.rs` add to `impl Clip` after `anim_value`:

```rust
    /// The static (un-animated) value of a track's property: the clip
    /// field, or the effect's stored `Number`. `None` for an effect or a
    /// parameter the clip does not have.
    pub fn static_value(&self, k: &crate::TrackKey) -> Option<f64> {
        match k {
            crate::TrackKey::Clip(p) => Some(p.read(&self.transform, &self.color)),
            crate::TrackKey::Fx(f) => self.effect(f.effect)?.number(&f.key),
        }
    }

    /// Stores `v` as the static value of `k`; `false` when the clip has no
    /// such effect (nothing is written).
    pub fn write_static(&mut self, k: &crate::TrackKey, v: f64) -> bool {
        match k {
            crate::TrackKey::Clip(p) => {
                p.write(&mut self.transform, &mut self.color, v);
                true
            }
            crate::TrackKey::Fx(f) => match self.effect_mut(f.effect) {
                Some(e) => {
                    e.params.insert(f.key.clone(), crate::ParamValue::Number(v));
                    true
                }
                None => false,
            },
        }
    }

    /// The value of `k` at timeline time `t`: its track (in source time) or
    /// the static value.
    pub fn track_value(&self, k: &crate::TrackKey, t: Time) -> Option<f64> {
        self.animation.value_at(k, crate::SourceTime(self.source_time_at(t))).or_else(|| self.static_value(k))
    }
```

and their arms in `apply`:

```rust
            EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip, param, at, value, ease } => {
                if !value.is_finite() {
                    return Err(EditError::InvalidRange);
                }
                let c = anim_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                // An effect parameter's track needs its effect (its static value lives there).
                if param.as_fx().is_some_and(|f| c.effect(f.effect).is_none()) {
                    return Err(EditError::EffectNotFound);
                }
                c.animation.set_key(param, *at, *value, ease.clone());
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::RemoveKeyframe { clip, param, at, keep } => {
                let c = anim_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                let k = c.animation.remove_key(param, *at).ok_or(EditError::NoOp)?;
                if !c.animation.is_animated(param) {
                    // A track of a removed effect has no static slot: nothing to keep.
                    c.write_static(param, keep.filter(|v| v.is_finite()).unwrap_or(k.value));
                }
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::MoveKeyframes { clip, moves } => {
                let c = anim_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                if moves.is_empty() {
                    return Err(EditError::NoOp);
                }
                // Work on a copy and assign only on success: a failed move must not
                // leave half the keys moved, also inside a `Batch` (which swallows `NoOp`).
                // Every moving keyframe is removed first, so keyframes can swap past each other.
                let mut anim = c.animation.clone();
                let mut taken = vec![];
                for (p, from, to) in moves {
                    let k = anim.remove_key(p, *from).ok_or(EditError::NoOp)?;
                    taken.push((p.clone(), *to, k));
                }
                for (p, to, k) in taken {
                    if anim.track(&p).iter().any(|x| x.at == to) {
                        return Err(EditError::KeyframeCollision);
                    }
                    anim.set_key(p, to, k.value, Some(k.ease));
                }
                c.animation = anim;
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::SetKeyframeEase { clip, param, at, ease } => {
                anim_clip(seq, *clip)?.animation.set_ease(param, *at, ease.clone()).map_err(anim_err)
            }
            EditCommand::ClearAnimation { clip, statics } => {
                let c = anim_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                if statics.is_empty() {
                    return Err(EditError::NoOp);
                }
                if statics.iter().any(|(_, v)| !v.is_finite()) {
                    return Err(EditError::InvalidRange);
                }
                if statics.iter().any(|(p, _)| c.static_value(p).is_none()) {
                    return Err(EditError::EffectNotFound);
                }
                for (p, v) in statics {
                    c.animation.clear(p);
                    c.write_static(p, *v);
                }
                Ok(())
            }
```

```rust
pub fn visible_key_near(c: &Clip, p: Option<TrackKey>, t: Time, frame: Time) -> Option<SourceTime> {
    let half = half_frame(frame);
    let keys = p.map_or_else(|| c.animation.keys(), |p| vec![p]);
    keys.iter()
        .flat_map(|q| c.animation.track(q).iter().map(|k| k.at))
        .map(|at| (at, c.timeline_time_of(at.0)))
        .filter(|(_, tl)| *tl >= c.timeline_in && *tl < c.timeline_out && (*tl - t).abs() < half)
        .min_by_key(|(_, tl)| (*tl - t).abs())
        .map(|(at, _)| at)
}

/// The keyframe of `p` at the playhead (`visible_key_near`), only while
/// the playhead is on the clip.
pub fn key_here(clip: &Clip, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, playhead: Time, frame: Time) -> Option<&Keyframe> {
    let p = p.into();
    let t = edit_time(clip, playhead, frame)?;
    let at = visible_key_near(clip, Some(p.clone()), t, frame)?;
    clip.animation.track(&p).iter().find(|k| k.at == at)
}

/// Keyframe state of `p` at the playhead; `prev`/`next` are the nearest
/// **visible** keyframes (timeline time in `[in, out)`) other than the one here.
pub fn key_info(clip: &Clip, p: impl Into<TrackKey>, playhead: Time, frame: Time) -> KeyInfo {
    let p = p.into();
    if !clip.animation.is_animated(&p) {
        return KeyInfo { state: 0, prev: None, next: None };
    }
    let half = half_frame(frame);
    let visible: Vec<Time> = clip
        .animation
        .track(&p)
        .iter()
        .map(|k| clip.timeline_time_of(k.at.0))
        .filter(|t| *t >= clip.timeline_in && *t < clip.timeline_out)
        .collect();
    KeyInfo {
        state: if key_here(clip, &p, playhead, frame).is_some() { 2 } else { 1 },
        prev: visible.iter().rev().copied().find(|t| *t <= playhead - half),
        next: visible.iter().copied().find(|t| *t >= playhead + half),
    }
}
```

```rust
pub fn moment_ease(c: &Clip, at: SourceTime) -> &'static str {
    let mut keys = c.animation.keys_at(at).into_iter().filter_map(|p| c.animation.track(&p).iter().find(|k| k.at == at).map(|k| ease_key(&k.ease)));
    match keys.next() {
        Some(first) if keys.all(|k| k == first) => first,
        _ => "",
    }
}

/// The tracks whose keyframe at `at` does not have curve `e` yet: the
/// keyframe menu changes only those (none — no command at all).
pub fn ease_targets(c: &Clip, at: SourceTime, e: &Ease) -> Vec<TrackKey> {
    c.animation.keys_at(at).into_iter().filter(|p| c.animation.track(p).iter().any(|k| k.at == at && k.ease != *e)).collect()
}
```

### Task 3: Recipe curves and the `kadr-effects` foundations (noise, placement delta, parameters)

**Files:**

- Create: `crates/effects/Cargo.toml`, `crates/effects/src/lib.rs`, `crates/effects/src/noise.rs`, `crates/effects/src/delta.rs`, `crates/effects/src/params.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `kadr_project::{ParamValue, SourceTime, MAX_KEY_FLICKS}` (Task 1), `kadr_scene::{Placement, Vec2}`.
- Produces:
  - `kadr_core::ease::Curve::{Cinematic, Snap, Gentle}`.


and below `impl Curve`:

```rust
/// y of the CSS-style cubic Bézier through (0, 0), `p1`, `p2`, (1, 1) at
/// x = `u`. x(t) is monotonic for control x in [0, 1], so 60 bisection
/// steps find t to full f64 precision — deterministically (no Newton step
/// whose result could differ between implementations).
fn cubic_bezier(p1: (f64, f64), p2: (f64, f64), u: f64) -> f64 {
    if u <= 0.0 {
        return 0.0;
    }
    if u >= 1.0 {
        return 1.0;
    }
    let b = |a: f64, c: f64, t: f64| 3.0 * a * (1.0 - t) * (1.0 - t) * t + 3.0 * c * (1.0 - t) * t * t + t * t * t;
    let (mut lo, mut hi) = (0.0f64, 1.0f64);
    for _ in 0..60 {
        let mid = (lo + hi) / 2.0;
        if b(p1.0, p2.0, mid) < u {
            lo = mid;
        } else {
            hi = mid;
        }
    }
    b(p1.1, p2.1, (lo + hi) / 2.0)
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    /// The lattice is integer-exact: a GPU port must give these numbers.
    #[test]
    fn lattice_values_are_pinned() {
        assert_eq!(pcg32_hash(7, 0), 751_650_196);
        assert_eq!(pcg32_hash(7, 1), 1_919_132_652);
        assert_eq!(pcg32_hash(7, u64::MAX), 3_682_281_251);
        assert_eq!(pcg32_hash(42, 3), 1_655_746_530);
        let n = Noise1D { seed: 7, frequency: 1.0, amplitude: 1.0 };
        assert!((n.at(0.0) - (-0.6499856020440313)).abs() < 1e-12, "a lattice point is its value");
        assert!((n.at(0.5) - (-0.3781599102956615)).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert!((Noise1D { frequency: 0.7, ..n }.at(1.0) - (-0.22376291738258747)).abs() < 1e-12);
    }

    #[test]
    fn noise_is_bounded_smooth_and_seeded() {
        let n = Noise1D { seed: 99, frequency: 0.7, amplitude: 0.006 };
        let mut prev = n.at(0.0);
        for k in 1..3000 {
            let v = n.at(k as f64 / 300.0);
            assert!(v.abs() <= 0.006 + 1e-15, "bounded at {k}");
            assert!((v - prev).abs() < 0.0001, "smooth at {k}: {prev} → {v}");
            prev = v;
        }
        assert_eq!(n.at(3.21), n.at(3.21), "deterministic");
        assert_ne!(n.at(3.21), Noise1D { seed: 100, ..n }.at(3.21));
        assert_eq!(n.at(f64::NAN), 0.0);
    }
}
```

`crates/effects/src/delta.rs` — test module first:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_scene::{Affine2, SizeU};

    /// The local map a delta stands for: x ↦ P + d + R(θ)·s·(x − P).
    fn local(d: &PlacementDelta, w: f64, h: f64) -> Affine2 {
        let (ax, ay) = (d.anchor.0 * w, d.anchor.1 * h);
        Affine2::translate(ax + d.offset.0 * w, ay + d.offset.1 * h)
            .after(&Affine2::rotate(d.rotation_deg.to_radians()))
            .after(&Affine2::scale(d.scale, d.scale))
            .after(&Affine2::translate(-ax, -ay))
    }

    #[test]
    fn apply_composes_inside_the_clip_placement() {
        let p = Placement { size: Vec2::new(1600.0, 900.0), anchor: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), position: Vec2::new(900.0, 520.0), scale: Vec2::new(0.8, 0.8), rotation: 0.3 };
        let d = PlacementDelta { scale: 1.2, anchor: (0.25, 0.7), offset: (0.03, -0.02), rotation_deg: 2.0 };
        let got = d.apply(&p).to_canvas();
        let want = p.to_canvas().after(&local(&d, 1600.0, 900.0));
        for (x, y) in [(0.0, 0.0), (1600.0, 0.0), (400.0, 630.0), (1600.0, 900.0)] {
            let (a, b) = (got.apply(x, y), want.apply(x, y));
            assert!((a.0 - b.0).abs() < 0.05 && (a.1 - b.1).abs() < 0.05, "{x},{y}: {a:?} vs {b:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_anchor_stays_where_the_clip_shows_it() {
        let p = Placement::fill(SizeU::new(1920, 1080));
        let d = PlacementDelta { scale: 1.5, anchor: (0.2, 0.3), ..PlacementDelta::IDENTITY };
        let a = d.apply(&p).to_canvas().apply(0.2 * 1920.0, 0.3 * 1080.0);
        assert!((a.0 - 384.0).abs() < 0.01 && (a.1 - 324.0).abs() < 0.01, "{a:?}");
        assert_eq!(PlacementDelta::IDENTITY.apply(&p), p);
        assert!(PlacementDelta { anchor: (0.0, 1.0), ..PlacementDelta::IDENTITY }.is_identity(), "an anchor alone moves nothing");
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_core::Time;

    const DEFS: [ParamDef; 4] = [
        ParamDef { key: "strength", kind: ParamKind::Number { min: 0.0, max: 1.0, default: 0.6 } },
        ParamDef { key: "direction", kind: ParamKind::Choice { options: &["in", "out"], default: "in" } },
        ParamDef { key: "point", kind: ParamKind::Point },
        ParamDef { key: "moment", kind: ParamKind::Moment },
    ];

    fn map(v: &[(&str, ParamValue)]) -> BTreeMap<String, ParamValue> {
        v.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect()
    }

    #[test]
    fn validation_names_the_key_and_the_reason() {
        assert_eq!(validate(&DEFS, &map(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.3)), ("point", ParamValue::Point { x: 0.0, y: 1.0 })])), Ok(()));
        let err = |v: &[(&str, ParamValue)]| validate(&DEFS, &map(v)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(err(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(1.2))]).contains("strength must be 0..1"));
        assert!(err(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(f64::NAN))]).contains("strength"));
        assert!(err(&[("direction", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()))]).contains("one of in, out"));
        assert!(err(&[("point", ParamValue::Point { x: 1.5, y: 0.5 })]).contains("point"));
        assert!(err(&[("moment", ParamValue::Number(1.0))]).contains("moment must be a moment"));
        assert!(err(&[("speed", ParamValue::Number(1.0))]).contains("unknown parameter \"speed\""));
        assert!(err(&[("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time(i64::MAX))))]).contains("out of range"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn resolve_fills_defaults_sanitises_and_prefers_tracks() {
        let stored = map(&[
            ("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.3)),
            ("direction", ParamValue::Choice("sideways".into())),
            ("point", ParamValue::Point { x: 1.7, y: -2.0 }),
        ]);
        let p = Params::resolve(&DEFS, &stored, |_| None);
        assert_eq!((p.number("strength"), p.choice("direction"), p.point("point"), p.moment("moment")), (0.3, "in", (1.0, 0.0), None));
        let tracked = Params::resolve(&DEFS, &stored, |k| (k == "strength").then_some(7.0));
        assert_eq!(tracked.number("strength"), 1.0, "a track wins and is clamped into range");
        let bare = Params::resolve(&DEFS, &BTreeMap::new(), |_| None);
        assert_eq!((bare.number("strength"), bare.point("point")), (0.6, (0.5, 0.5)), "defaults");
        assert_eq!(bare.number("nonexistent"), 0.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn strength_zero_turns_an_effect_off_and_intensity_is_relative_to_normal() {
        assert!(Params::resolve(&DEFS, &map(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0))]), |_| None).is_off());
        assert!(!Params::resolve(&DEFS, &BTreeMap::new(), |_| None).is_off());
        assert!(!Params::resolve(&DEFS[1..], &BTreeMap::new(), |_| None).is_off(), "no strength parameter: never off");
        assert_eq!(intensity(NORMAL), 1.0);
        assert!((intensity(1.0) - 1.0 / 0.6).abs() < 1e-12);
        assert_eq!(default_value(&DEFS[3], SourceTime(Time::from_secs(2))), ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(2))));
        assert_eq!(DEFS[0].label_key(), "fx.param.strength");
        assert!(DEFS[0].animatable() && !DEFS[1].animatable());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Implement** — above the test module of `noise.rs`:

```rust
//! `Noise1D` (effects spec §3): smooth value noise for natural motion
//! (Handheld). Lattice values come from one PCG32 (XSH-RR) output step
//! over an integer hash — integer-exact, so a GPU shader reproduces them
//! bit for bit.

/// Value noise in `[−amplitude, amplitude]`: a lattice value every
/// `1 / frequency` seconds, smoothstep-blended in between.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Noise1D {
    pub seed: u64,
    pub frequency: f64,
    pub amplitude: f64,
}

impl Noise1D {
    /// The noise at `t` seconds (0 for a non-finite `t`).
    pub fn at(&self, t: f64) -> f64 {
        let x = t * self.frequency;
        if !x.is_finite() {
            return 0.0;
        }
        let floor = x.floor();
        let f = x - floor;
        let i = floor as i64;
        let (a, b) = (lattice(self.seed, i), lattice(self.seed, i.wrapping_add(1)));
        let s = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
        self.amplitude * (a + (b - a) * s)
    }
}

const PCG_MUL: u64 = 6_364_136_223_846_793_005;

/// PCG32 (XSH-RR) output of one LCG step from `i` on stream `seed`.
pub fn pcg32_hash(seed: u64, i: u64) -> u32 {
    let inc = (seed << 1) | 1;
    let state = i.wrapping_add(inc).wrapping_mul(PCG_MUL).wrapping_add(inc);
    let xorshifted = (((state >> 18) ^ state) >> 27) as u32;
    xorshifted.rotate_right((state >> 59) as u32)
}

/// The lattice value of point `i`, in `[−1, 1]`.
fn lattice(seed: u64, i: i64) -> f64 {
    pcg32_hash(seed, i as u64) as f64 / u32::MAX as f64 * 2.0 - 1.0
}
```

Above the test module of `delta.rs`:

```rust
//! A motion effect's change of the clip's placement (effects spec §3:
//! "PlacementDelta умножается поверх Placement клипа: масштаб вокруг
//! якоря, сдвиг, поворот").

use kadr_scene::{Placement, Vec2};

/// Scale and rotation about `anchor`, then a shift — all inside the clip's
/// own placement, so the content point at `anchor` stays where the clip
/// shows it (moved by `offset`): a zoom keeps its subject in place.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct PlacementDelta {
    /// Uniform scale (1 = none).
    pub scale: f64,
    /// Fixed point of scale and rotation: fractions of the clip's picture
    /// (0, 0 = top left; the content rectangle before crop).
    pub anchor: (f64, f64),
    /// Shift after scaling: fractions of the picture's width and height.
    pub offset: (f64, f64),
    /// Degrees, clockwise on screen.
    pub rotation_deg: f64,
}

impl PlacementDelta {
    pub const IDENTITY: PlacementDelta = PlacementDelta { scale: 1.0, anchor: (0.5, 0.5), offset: (0.0, 0.0), rotation_deg: 0.0 };

    /// Moves nothing (whatever the anchor).
    pub fn is_identity(&self) -> bool {
        self.scale == 1.0 && self.offset == (0.0, 0.0) && self.rotation_deg == 0.0
    }

    /// `p` followed by this delta in its local space: local point `x` lands
    /// where `p` puts `P + d + R(θ)·s·(x − P)` (`P` = anchor, `d` = offset,
    /// in local pixels). Exact for a uniform `p.scale` — the evaluator's.
    pub fn apply(&self, p: &Placement) -> Placement {
        if self.is_identity() {
            return *p;
        }
        let (w, h) = (p.size.x as f64, p.size.y as f64);
        let (ax, ay) = (self.anchor.0 * w, self.anchor.1 * h);
        let (px, py) = p.to_canvas().apply(ax + self.offset.0 * w, ay + self.offset.1 * h);
        Placement {
            size: p.size,
            anchor: Vec2::new(self.anchor.0 as f32, self.anchor.1 as f32),
            position: Vec2::new(px as f32, py as f32),
            scale: Vec2::new((p.scale.x as f64 * self.scale) as f32, (p.scale.y as f64 * self.scale) as f32),
            rotation: (p.rotation as f64 + self.rotation_deg.to_radians()) as f32,
        }
    }
}
```

Above the test module of `params.rs`:

```rust
//! Parameter definitions of recipes (effects spec §2, §5): validation for
//! commands, defaults for new effects, and the values a `compile` sees.

use kadr_project::{ParamValue, SourceTime, MAX_KEY_FLICKS};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// The «Обычно» strength (spec §2): the preset as designed.
pub const NORMAL: f64 = 0.6;

/// Intensity multiplier `k = s / 0.6`: 0 % → none, 60 % → 1, 100 % → 1.67.
pub fn intensity(strength: f64) -> f64 {
    strength / NORMAL
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum ParamKind {
    /// A number in `min..=max`; only numbers can be animated (`fx:` tracks).
    Number { min: f64, max: f64, default: f64 },
    /// One of `options`.
    Choice { options: &'static [&'static str], default: &'static str },
    /// A point of the clip's picture, fractions 0..1; default the centre.
    Point,
    /// A source moment; default: the source frame where the effect is applied.
    Moment,
}

impl ParamKind {
    pub fn type_name(&self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            ParamKind::Number { .. } => "number",
            ParamKind::Choice { .. } => "choice",
            ParamKind::Point => "point",
            ParamKind::Moment => "moment",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct ParamDef {
    pub key: &'static str,
    pub kind: ParamKind,
}

impl ParamDef {
    /// i18n key of the parameter's label: `fx.param.<key>`.
    pub fn label_key(&self) -> String {
        format!("fx.param.{}", self.key)
    }

    pub fn animatable(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self.kind, ParamKind::Number { .. })
    }

    /// Whether `v` is a valid value of this parameter; the error names the
    /// key and the reason (shown to MCP / AI clients).
    pub fn check(&self, v: &ParamValue) -> Result<(), String> {
        let k = self.key;
        match (self.kind, v) {
            (ParamKind::Number { min, max, .. }, ParamValue::Number(n)) => {
                if n.is_finite() && (min..=max).contains(n) { Ok(()) } else { Err(format!("{k} must be {min}..{max}, got {n}")) }
            }
            (ParamKind::Choice { options, .. }, ParamValue::Choice(c)) => {
                if options.contains(&c.as_str()) { Ok(()) } else { Err(format!("{k} must be one of {}, got {c:?}", options.join(", "))) }
            }
            (ParamKind::Point, ParamValue::Point { x, y }) => {
                if [*x, *y].iter().all(|v| v.is_finite() && (0.0..=1.0).contains(v)) { Ok(()) } else { Err(format!("{k} must have x and y in 0..1")) }
            }
            (ParamKind::Moment, ParamValue::Moment(m)) => {
                if m.0.flicks().unsigned_abs() <= MAX_KEY_FLICKS { Ok(()) } else { Err(format!("{k} is out of range")) }
            }
            (kind, _) => Err(format!("{k} must be a {}", kind.type_name())),
        }
    }
}

/// Checks `params` against `defs`: every key declared, every value valid.
pub fn validate(defs: &[ParamDef], params: &BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>) -> Result<(), String> {
    for (k, v) in params {
        let def = defs.iter().find(|d| d.key == k).ok_or_else(|| {
            format!("unknown parameter {k:?} (known: {})", defs.iter().map(|d| d.key).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", "))
        })?;
        def.check(v)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The default of a parameter; `moment` is what a `Moment` defaults to.
pub fn default_value(def: &ParamDef, moment: SourceTime) -> ParamValue {
    match def.kind {
        ParamKind::Number { default, .. } => ParamValue::Number(default),
        ParamKind::Choice { default, .. } => ParamValue::Choice(default.to_string()),
        ParamKind::Point => ParamValue::Point { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 },
        ParamKind::Moment => ParamValue::Moment(moment),
    }
}

/// The values one `compile` sees: each declared parameter at this frame —
/// a number's `fx:` track when there is one, else the stored value; a
/// missing or invalid value falls back to the default, so a file from
/// elsewhere never breaks a frame. Numbers are clamped into range, points
/// into 0..1; a missing moment stays `None` (the recipe treats it as never).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Params {
    values: Vec<(&'static str, Option<ParamValue>)>,
}

impl Params {
    pub fn resolve(defs: &[ParamDef], stored: &BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, animated: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<f64>) -> Params {
        let values = defs
            .iter()
            .map(|d| {
                let v = match d.kind {
                    ParamKind::Number { min, max, default } => {
                        let n = animated(d.key).or_else(|| stored.get(d.key).and_then(ParamValue::as_number)).filter(|n| n.is_finite()).unwrap_or(default);
                        Some(ParamValue::Number(n.clamp(min, max)))
                    }
                    ParamKind::Choice { options, default } => Some(ParamValue::Choice(match stored.get(d.key) {
                        Some(ParamValue::Choice(c)) if options.contains(&c.as_str()) => c.clone(),
                        _ => default.to_string(),
                    })),
                    ParamKind::Point => Some(match stored.get(d.key) {
                        Some(ParamValue::Point { x, y }) if x.is_finite() && y.is_finite() => ParamValue::Point { x: x.clamp(0.0, 1.0), y: y.clamp(0.0, 1.0) },
                        _ => ParamValue::Point { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 },
                    }),
                    ParamKind::Moment => match stored.get(d.key) {
                        Some(ParamValue::Moment(m)) => Some(ParamValue::Moment(*m)),
                        _ => None,
                    },
                };
                (d.key, v)
            })
            .collect();
        Params { values }
    }

    fn get(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&ParamValue> {
        self.values.iter().find(|(k, _)| *k == key).and_then(|(_, v)| v.as_ref())
    }

    /// A number parameter (0 when the recipe has none of that name).
    pub fn number(&self, key: &str) -> f64 {
        self.get(key).and_then(ParamValue::as_number).unwrap_or(0.0)
    }

    pub fn choice(&self, key: &str) -> &str {
        match self.get(key) {
            Some(ParamValue::Choice(c)) => c,
            _ => "",
        }
    }

    pub fn point(&self, key: &str) -> (f64, f64) {
        match self.get(key) {
            Some(ParamValue::Point { x, y }) => (*x, *y),
            _ => (0.5, 0.5),
        }
    }

    pub fn moment(&self, key: &str) -> Option<SourceTime> {
        match self.get(key) {
            Some(ParamValue::Moment(m)) => Some(*m),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    /// Strength 0: the effect contributes nothing — not even a compile
    /// (spec §3). A recipe without a strength is never off.
    pub fn is_off(&self) -> bool {
        self.get("strength").and_then(ParamValue::as_number).is_some_and(|s| s <= 0.0)
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_core::{AssetId, TimeRange};

    #[test]
    fn the_registry_holds_the_five_motion_effects_in_card_order() {
        let ids: Vec<&str> = registry().iter().map(|d| d.id).collect();
        assert_eq!(ids, ["smooth_zoom", "cinematic_push", "punch_in", "smooth_pan", "handheld"]);
        for d in registry() {
            assert_eq!((d.category, d.target, d.version, d.cost), (Category::Motion, Target::Clip, 1, CostClass::Cheap), "{}", d.id);
            assert!(!d.params.is_empty() && d.params.len() <= 5, "{}: 1–5 parameters", d.id);
            assert!(d.param("strength").is_some(), "{} has a strength", d.id);
            assert_eq!(d.name_key(), format!("fx.{}", d.id));
        }
        let keys = |id: &str| recipe(id).unwrap().params.iter().map(|p| p.key).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        assert_eq!(keys("smooth_zoom"), ["direction", "strength", "point"], "spec §5 order");
        assert_eq!(keys("punch_in"), ["strength", "moment", "point"]);
        assert_eq!(keys("smooth_pan"), ["direction", "strength"]);
        assert_eq!(keys("handheld"), ["strength"]);
    }

    /// Spec §2: a recipe or a version from a newer Kadr is kept, not run.
    #[test]
    fn unknown_recipes_and_newer_versions_have_no_definition() {
        let mut e = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::new());
        assert_eq!(definition(&e).map(|d| d.id), Some("smooth_zoom"));
        e.version = 2;
        assert!(definition(&e).is_none());
        assert!(definition(&AppliedEffect::new("glitch_v2", 1, BTreeMap::new())).is_none());
        assert!(recipe("glitch_v2").is_none());
    }

    #[test]
    fn fresh_effects_take_normal_strength_the_centre_and_the_frame_where_applied() {
        // Timeline 10..20 s showing source 30..40 s.
        let c = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(30), Time::from_secs(40)), Time::from_secs(10));
        let e = fresh(recipe("punch_in").unwrap(), &c, Time::from_secs(12));
        assert_eq!(e.params["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.6));
        assert_eq!(e.params["point"], ParamValue::Point { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 });
        assert_eq!(e.params["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(32))));
        assert_eq!((e.enabled, e.progress, e.version, e.recipe.as_str()), (true, (0.0, 1.0), 1, "punch_in"));
        let off = fresh(recipe("punch_in").unwrap(), &c, Time::from_secs(25));
        assert_eq!(off.params["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(35))), "applied off the clip: its middle");
        assert_eq!(fresh(recipe("smooth_pan").unwrap(), &c, Time::ZERO).params["direction"], ParamValue::Choice("right".into()));
        let mut given = BTreeMap::from([("strength".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.9))]);
        complete(recipe("handheld").unwrap(), &mut given, &c, Time::ZERO);
        assert_eq!(given.len(), 1, "a given value stays, nothing else is declared");
        assert_eq!(given["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.9));
    }

    #[test]
    fn effects_fit_their_tracks() {
        assert!(fits(Target::Clip, TrackKind::Video));
        assert!(!fits(Target::Clip, TrackKind::Audio));
        assert!(fits(Target::AudioClip, TrackKind::Audio));
        assert!(!fits(Target::Cut, TrackKind::Video), "a transition is not a clip effect");
        assert_eq!(Category::Motion.label_key(), "fx.cat.motion");
    }
}
```

`crates/effects/src/motion.rs` with only its test module:

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::registry::{recipe, registry, CompileCtx};
    use kadr_core::Time;
    use kadr_project::{ParamValue, SourceTime};
    use kadr_scene::{Placement, SizeU, Vec2};

    fn ctx(progress: f64) -> CompileCtx {
        CompileCtx { progress, clip_secs: 10.0, source_time: SourceTime(Time::from_secs(5)), speed: 1.0, canvas: SizeU::new(1920, 1080), aspect: 16.0 / 9.0, frame: Time::ZERO, seed: 7 }
    }

    fn run(id: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)], c: &CompileCtx) -> PlacementDelta {
        let def = recipe(id).unwrap();
        let stored = set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect();
        (def.compile)(&Params::resolve(def.params, &stored, |_| None), c).placement
    }

    fn close(a: f64, b: f64) -> bool {
        (a - b).abs() < 1e-9
    }

    #[test]
    fn normal_strength_matches_the_spec_table() {
        assert!(close(run("smooth_zoom", &[], &ctx(0.0)).scale, 1.0));
        assert!(close(run("smooth_zoom", &[], &ctx(1.0)).scale, 1.10));
        assert!(close(run("smooth_zoom", &[], &ctx(0.5)).scale, 1.05), "Smooth = ease in-out: half way at ½");
        let out = [("direction", ParamValue::Choice("out".into()))];
        assert!(close(run("smooth_zoom", &out, &ctx(0.0)).scale, 1.10));
        assert!(close(run("smooth_zoom", &out, &ctx(1.0)).scale, 1.0));
        let at = [("point", ParamValue::Point { x: 0.2, y: 0.9 })];
        assert_eq!(run("smooth_zoom", &at, &ctx(1.0)).anchor, (0.2, 0.9));
        assert!(close(run("cinematic_push", &[], &ctx(1.0)).scale, 1.05));
        assert!(close(run("cinematic_push", &[], &ctx(0.5)).scale, 1.0 + 0.05 * Curve::Cinematic.apply(0.5)));
        let pan = run("smooth_pan", &[], &ctx(0.0));
        assert!(close(pan.scale, 1.06) && close(pan.offset.0, 0.03) && pan.offset.1 == 0.0, "{pan:?}");
        assert!(close(run("smooth_pan", &[], &ctx(1.0)).offset.0, -0.03), "the camera pans right: the picture slides left");
        let up = run("smooth_pan", &[("direction", ParamValue::Choice("up".into()))], &ctx(0.0));
        assert!(close(up.offset.1, -0.03) && up.offset.0 == 0.0, "{up:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn punch_in_happens_at_its_moment_in_timeline_time_and_holds() {
        let m = [("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(5))))];
        let at = |src_ms: i64, speed: f64| {
            let mut c = ctx(0.5);
            c.source_time = SourceTime(Time::from_millis(src_ms));
            c.speed = speed;
            run("punch_in", &m, &c).scale
        };
        assert!(close(at(4_999, 1.0), 1.0), "before the moment");
        assert!(close(at(5_000, 1.0), 1.0), "the moment itself starts it");
        assert!(close(at(5_060, 1.0), 1.0 + 0.15 * Curve::Snap.apply(0.5)));
        assert!(close(at(5_120, 1.0), 1.15), "0.12 s later: in");
        assert!(close(at(9_000, 1.0), 1.15), "and it holds");
        assert!(close(at(5_120, 2.0), 1.0 + 0.15 * Curve::Snap.apply(0.5)), "at 2× 0.12 s of source is 0.06 s on screen");
        assert!(close(run("punch_in", &[], &ctx(0.5)).scale, 1.0), "no moment: no punch");
    }

    #[test]
    fn strength_scales_the_motion_and_zero_is_none() {
        for d in registry() {
            let zero = run(d.id, &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0)), ("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::ZERO)))], &ctx(0.7));
            assert!(zero.is_identity(), "{}: {zero:?}", d.id);
        }
        let strong = run("smooth_zoom", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(1.0))], &ctx(1.0));
        assert!(close(strong.scale, 1.0 + 0.10 / 0.6), "100 % = ×1.67");
    }

    /// Spec §5 "без полей": at any strength and progress the moved picture
    /// still covers the frame it filled — no black edge or corner shows.
    #[test]
    fn motion_never_shows_a_border() {
        for aspect in [16.0 / 9.0, 9.0 / 16.0, 1.0] {
            let (w, h) = (100.0 * aspect, 100.0);
            let base = Placement { size: Vec2::new(w as f32, h as f32), anchor: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), position: Vec2::new((w / 2.0) as f32, (h / 2.0) as f32), scale: Vec2::new(1.0, 1.0), rotation: 0.0 };
            for d in registry() {
                for s in [0.3, 0.6, 1.0] {
                    for point in [(0.5, 0.5), (0.0, 0.0), (1.0, 0.3)] {
                        for dir in ["in", "out", "left", "right", "up", "down"] {
                            for i in 0..=40 {
                                let mut c = ctx(i as f64 / 40.0);
                                c.aspect = aspect;
                                c.source_time = SourceTime(Time::from_millis(4_900 + i * 10));
                                let set: Vec<(&str, ParamValue)> = [
                                    ("strength", ParamValue::Number(s)),
                                    ("point", ParamValue::Point { x: point.0, y: point.1 }),
                                    ("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(5)))),
                                    ("direction", ParamValue::Choice(dir.into())),
                                ]
                                .into_iter()
                                .filter(|(k, _)| d.param(k).is_some())
                                .collect();
                                let moved = run(d.id, &set, &c).apply(&base);
                                let inv = moved.to_canvas().inverse().unwrap();
                                for (x, y) in [(0.0, 0.0), (w, 0.0), (0.0, h), (w, h)] {
                                    let (lx, ly) = inv.apply(x, y);
                                    let eps = 1e-3;
                                    assert!(lx >= -eps && lx <= w + eps && ly >= -eps && ly <= h + eps, "{} s={s} p={point:?} {dir} i={i} aspect={aspect}: corner ({x}, {y}) shows ({lx}, {ly})", d.id);
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn handheld_is_seeded_bounded_and_smooth() {
        let at = |seed: u64, secs: f64| {
            let mut c = ctx(secs / 10.0);
            c.seed = seed;
            run("handheld", &[], &c)
        };
        let mut prev = at(7, 0.0);
        for f in 1..300 {
            let d = at(7, f as f64 / 30.0);
            assert!(d.offset.0.abs() <= 0.006 + 1e-12 && d.offset.1.abs() <= 0.006 + 1e-12 && d.rotation_deg.abs() <= 0.4 + 1e-12, "{d:?}");
            assert!((d.offset.0 - prev.offset.0).abs() < 0.001 && (d.rotation_deg - prev.rotation_deg).abs() < 0.05, "frame {f}: a shake, not a jump");
            prev = d;
        }
        assert_eq!(at(7, 3.3), at(7, 3.3), "deterministic");
        assert_ne!(at(7, 3.3), at(8, 3.3), "the seed (the effect's id) picks the shake");
        assert_eq!(at(7, 1.0).scale, at(7, 2.0).scale, "a constant cover scale: no breathing");
    }
}
```
