```rust
//! The recipe registry (effects spec §3) and what a new effect starts with.

use crate::motion;
use crate::params::{default_value, ParamDef, ParamKind, Params, NORMAL};
use crate::PlacementDelta;
use kadr_core::Time;
use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip, ParamValue, SourceTime, TrackKind};
use kadr_scene::SizeU;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Category {
    Motion,
    Look,
    Light,
    Transition,
    Audio,
}

impl Category {
    /// Tab order (spec §6.1).
    pub const ALL: [Category; 5] = [Category::Motion, Category::Look, Category::Light, Category::Transition, Category::Audio];

    pub fn key(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Category::Motion => "motion",
            Category::Look => "look",
            Category::Light => "light",
            Category::Transition => "transition",
            Category::Audio => "audio",
        }
    }

    /// i18n key of the filter chip: `fx.cat.<key>`.
    pub fn label_key(self) -> String {
        format!("fx.cat.{}", self.key())
    }
}

/// What an effect goes on.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Target {
    /// A clip with a picture (video track).
    Clip,
    /// A cut between two clips (transitions, E3).
    Cut,
    /// A clip on an audio track (E4).
    AudioClip,
}

impl Target {
    pub fn key(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Target::Clip => "clip",
            Target::Cut => "cut",
            Target::AudioClip => "audio_clip",
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum CostClass {
    Cheap,
    Medium,
}

/// What a `compile` knows about the frame (spec §3, refinement 2).
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct CompileCtx {
    /// Spec §1 at this frame (may lie outside 0..1 past the clip's edges).
    pub progress: f64,
    /// Seconds of the whole progress range at the clip's current length:
    /// `progress × clip_secs` is the effect's own clock, continuous across a split.
    pub clip_secs: f64,
    /// The source frame shown (source-bound parameters, «Момент»).
    pub source_time: SourceTime,
    /// Source seconds per timeline second.
    pub speed: f64,
    pub canvas: SizeU,
    /// The clip picture's width / height.
    pub aspect: f64,
    /// Timeline time of the frame.
    pub frame: Time,
    /// From the effect's id (noise).
    pub seed: u64,
}

/// What one effect adds to a frame. E1 recipes only move the picture;
/// colour primitives (E2), overlays and transition builds (E3) and audio
/// operations (E4) join here.
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Compiled {
    pub placement: PlacementDelta,
    pub effects: Vec<kadr_scene::Effect>,
}

impl Compiled {
    pub fn motion(placement: PlacementDelta) -> Compiled {
        Compiled { placement, effects: vec![] }
    }
}

pub struct RecipeDef {
    pub id: &'static str,
    pub category: Category,
    pub target: Target,
    /// The current `compile`'s version (spec §2 «Версии рецептов»).
    pub version: u16,
    /// 1–5 parameters, in inspector order.
    pub params: &'static [ParamDef],
    pub cost: CostClass,
    pub compile: fn(&Params, &CompileCtx) -> Compiled,
}

impl RecipeDef {
    /// i18n key of the effect's name: `fx.<id>`.
    pub fn name_key(&self) -> String {
        format!("fx.{}", self.id)
    }

    pub fn param(&self, key: &str) -> Option<&'static ParamDef> {
        self.params.iter().find(|p| p.key == key)
    }
}

const STRENGTH: ParamDef = ParamDef { key: "strength", kind: ParamKind::Number { min: 0.0, max: 1.0, default: NORMAL } };
const POINT: ParamDef = ParamDef { key: "point", kind: ParamKind::Point };
const MOMENT: ParamDef = ParamDef { key: "moment", kind: ParamKind::Moment };
const ZOOM_DIRECTION: ParamDef = ParamDef { key: "direction", kind: ParamKind::Choice { options: &["in", "out"], default: "in" } };
const PAN_DIRECTION: ParamDef = ParamDef { key: "direction", kind: ParamKind::Choice { options: &["left", "right", "up", "down"], default: "right" } };

static REGISTRY: [RecipeDef; 5] = [
    RecipeDef { id: "smooth_zoom", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[ZOOM_DIRECTION, STRENGTH, POINT], cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: motion::smooth_zoom },
    RecipeDef { id: "cinematic_push", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, POINT], cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: motion::cinematic_push },
    RecipeDef { id: "punch_in", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH, MOMENT, POINT], cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: motion::punch_in },
    RecipeDef { id: "smooth_pan", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[PAN_DIRECTION, STRENGTH], cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: motion::smooth_pan },
    RecipeDef { id: "handheld", category: Category::Motion, target: Target::Clip, version: 1, params: &[STRENGTH], cost: CostClass::Cheap, compile: motion::handheld },
];

/// Every recipe this build runs, in card order.
pub fn registry() -> &'static [RecipeDef] {
    &REGISTRY
}

pub fn recipe(id: &str) -> Option<&'static RecipeDef> {
    REGISTRY.iter().find(|d| d.id == id)
}

/// The definition that evaluates `e`: its recipe at its version. `None`
/// for a recipe or a version from a newer Kadr (kept, not run — spec §2).
pub fn definition(e: &AppliedEffect) -> Option<&'static RecipeDef> {
    recipe(&e.recipe).filter(|d| d.version == e.version)
}

/// Whether an effect for `target` goes on a clip of a `kind` track.
pub fn fits(target: Target, kind: TrackKind) -> bool {
    matches!((target, kind), (Target::Clip, TrackKind::Video) | (Target::AudioClip, TrackKind::Audio))
}

/// Where a new effect's «Момент» goes (spec §5): the source frame at `at`
/// when `at` is on the clip (the playhead, a drop point), else the clip's
/// visible middle (refinement 4).
pub fn default_moment(clip: &Clip, at: Time) -> SourceTime {
    let t = if clip.timeline_in <= at && at < clip.timeline_out { at } else { clip.timeline_in + Time(clip.duration().flicks() / 2) };
    SourceTime(clip.source_time_at(t))
}

/// Gives every declared parameter missing from `params` its default.
pub fn complete(def: &RecipeDef, params: &mut BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, clip: &Clip, at: Time) {
    for d in def.params {
        if !params.contains_key(d.key) {
            params.insert(d.key.to_string(), default_value(d, default_moment(clip, at)));
        }
    }
}

/// A new effect of `def` on `clip` at «Обычно», applied at timeline time `at`.
pub fn fresh(def: &RecipeDef, clip: &Clip, at: Time) -> AppliedEffect {
    let mut params = BTreeMap::new();
    complete(def, &mut params, clip, at);
    AppliedEffect::new(def.id, def.version, params)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `motion.rs`** (above its tests):

```rust
//! The motion recipes (effects spec §5 «Движение»): each turns its
//! parameters and the frame's progress into a `PlacementDelta`. Numbers
//! are at intensity `k = 1` («Обычно»); every motion keeps the picture
//! covering the frame it filled ("без полей").

use crate::params::{intensity, Params};
use crate::registry::{CompileCtx, Compiled};
use crate::{Noise1D, PlacementDelta};
use kadr_core::ease::Curve;

/// Плавный наезд: scale 1.00 → 1.10.
pub const ZOOM_AMOUNT: f64 = 0.10;
/// Кино-наезд: scale 1.00 → 1.05.
pub const PUSH_AMOUNT: f64 = 0.05;
/// Резкий наезд: scale 1.00 → 1.15 …
pub const PUNCH_AMOUNT: f64 = 0.15;
/// … in 0.12 timeline seconds.
pub const PUNCH_SECS: f64 = 0.12;
/// Плавная панорама: travel 6 % of the frame (with the same scale-up).
pub const PAN_TRAVEL: f64 = 0.06;
/// Ручная камера: 0.6 % of the frame, 0.4°, ~0.7 Hz.
pub const SHAKE_SHIFT: f64 = 0.006;
pub const SHAKE_DEG: f64 = 0.4;
pub const SHAKE_HZ: f64 = 0.7;
const SALT_Y: u64 = 0x9E37_79B9_7F4A_7C15;
const SALT_ROT: u64 = 0xC2B2_AE3D_27D4_EB4F;

fn zoom(scale: f64, p: &Params) -> Compiled {
    Compiled::motion(PlacementDelta { scale, anchor: p.point("point"), ..PlacementDelta::IDENTITY })
}

pub fn smooth_zoom(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    let u = Curve::EaseInOut.apply(ctx.progress);
    let w = if p.choice("direction") == "out" { 1.0 - u } else { u };
    zoom(1.0 + ZOOM_AMOUNT * intensity(p.number("strength")) * w, p)
}

pub fn cinematic_push(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    zoom(1.0 + PUSH_AMOUNT * intensity(p.number("strength")) * Curve::Cinematic.apply(ctx.progress), p)
}

/// In at the source moment, held to the end; no moment — no punch.
pub fn punch_in(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    let w = p.moment("moment").map_or(0.0, |m| {
        let elapsed = (ctx.source_time.0 - m.0).as_secs_f64() / ctx.speed.max(1e-9);
        if elapsed < 0.0 { 0.0 } else { Curve::Snap.apply(elapsed / PUNCH_SECS) }
    });
    zoom(1.0 + PUNCH_AMOUNT * intensity(p.number("strength")) * w, p)
}

/// The camera moves `direction` (refinement 7): the picture slides the
/// other way, from +travel/2 to −travel/2, scaled up by the travel so no
/// edge shows.
pub fn smooth_pan(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    let travel = PAN_TRAVEL * intensity(p.number("strength"));
    let (dx, dy) = match p.choice("direction") {
        "left" => (-1.0, 0.0),
        "up" => (0.0, -1.0),
        "down" => (0.0, 1.0),
        _ => (1.0, 0.0),
    };
    let s = travel * (0.5 - Curve::EaseInOut.apply(ctx.progress));
    Compiled::motion(PlacementDelta { scale: 1.0 + travel, anchor: (0.5, 0.5), offset: (dx * s, dy * s), rotation_deg: 0.0 })
}

/// Noise on x, y and rotation over the effect's own clock; one constant
/// cover scale for the largest shift and turn: a `w × h` picture turned by
/// θ and shifted by `d` (fractions) stays over the frame when
/// `s ≥ (1 + 2d)(1 + sin θ · max(w/h, h/w))`.
pub fn handheld(p: &Params, ctx: &CompileCtx) -> Compiled {
    let k = intensity(p.number("strength"));
    let (shift, deg) = (SHAKE_SHIFT * k, SHAKE_DEG * k);
    let t = ctx.progress * ctx.clip_secs;
    let noise = |salt: u64, amplitude: f64| Noise1D { seed: ctx.seed ^ salt, frequency: SHAKE_HZ, amplitude }.at(t);
    let aspect = if ctx.aspect.is_finite() && ctx.aspect > 0.0 { ctx.aspect } else { 16.0 / 9.0 };
    let cover = (1.0 + 2.0 * shift) * (1.0 + deg.to_radians().sin() * aspect.max(1.0 / aspect));
    Compiled::motion(PlacementDelta { scale: cover, anchor: (0.5, 0.5), offset: (noise(0, shift), noise(SALT_Y, shift)), rotation_deg: noise(SALT_ROT, deg) })
}
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

  - `kadr_timeline::fx::effect_seed(e: &AppliedEffect) -> u64`
  - `kadr_timeline::fx::place_with_effects(clip: &Clip, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, base: Placement) -> Placement`
  - `ops::split_clip` cuts every effect's progress range at the cut (ids kept).

```rust
//! The evaluator with effects (effects spec §1, §3, §9 «evaluator»).

use kadr_core::{FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, VideoInfo};
use kadr_project::*;
use kadr_scene::{Layer, OutputSpec, Placement, RenderQuality, SizeU};
use kadr_timeline::ops::{split_clip, trim_start, LinkRemap};
use kadr_timeline::scene::evaluate;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

const F30: Time = Time(705_600_000 / 30);

fn hd() -> MediaAsset {
    let video = VideoInfo { width: 1920, height: 1080, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "h264".into(), pixel_format: "yuv420p".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    MediaAsset::new("m", MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(600), container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None })
}

/// One clip on V1: timeline 10..20 s showing source 30..40 s, with `effects`.
fn project(effects: Vec<AppliedEffect>) -> (Project, kadr_core::ClipId) {
    let mut p = Project::new("fx");
    let a = hd();
    let mut c = Clip::new(a.id, "c", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(30), Time::from_secs(40)), Time::from_secs(10));
    c.effects = effects;
    let id = c.id;
    p.assets.push(a);
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    (p, id)
}

fn fx(recipe: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)]) -> AppliedEffect {
    let params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue> = set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect();
    AppliedEffect::new(recipe, 1, params)
}

fn layer(p: &Project, t: Time) -> Layer {
    let out = OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(960, 540), RenderQuality::PreviewHigh);
    evaluate(p, p.sequence(), t, &out).layers.into_iter().next().expect("one layer")
}

/// Where the clip picture's corners land on the canvas.
fn corners(l: &Layer) -> [(f64, f64); 4] {
    let m = l.placement.to_canvas();
    let (w, h) = (l.placement.size.x as f64, l.placement.size.y as f64);
    [(0.0, 0.0), (w, 0.0), (0.0, h), (w, h)].map(|(x, y)| m.apply(x, y))
}

fn same_place(a: &Layer, b: &Layer) -> bool {
    corners(a).iter().zip(corners(b)).all(|(p, q)| (p.0 - q.0).abs() < 0.01 && (p.1 - q.1).abs() < 0.01)
}

fn scale(p: &Project, ms: i64) -> f64 {
    layer(p, Time::from_millis(ms)).placement.scale.x as f64
}

/// Spec §3 and review focus 5: strength 0, a disabled effect, an unknown
/// recipe and a newer version change nothing — not one field of the layer.
#[test]
fn zero_strength_disabled_and_unknown_effects_change_nothing() {
    let (plain, _) = project(vec![]);
    let t = Time::from_secs(15);
    let base = layer(&plain, t);
    let mut off = fx("smooth_zoom", &[]);
    off.enabled = false;
    let mut newer = fx("smooth_zoom", &[]);
    newer.version = 2;
    for e in [fx("smooth_zoom", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0))]), off, newer, fx("glitch_v2", &[("amount", ParamValue::Number(1.0))])] {
        let (p, _) = project(vec![e.clone()]);
        assert_eq!(layer(&p, t), base, "{e:?}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn smooth_zoom_follows_the_visible_clip_about_its_point() {
    let (p, _) = project(vec![fx("smooth_zoom", &[("point", ParamValue::Point { x: 0.25, y: 0.75 })])]);
    let (plain, _) = project(vec![]);
    assert!(same_place(&layer(&p, Time::from_secs(10)), &layer(&plain, Time::from_secs(10))), "progress 0: no zoom yet");
    let mid = layer(&p, Time::from_secs(15));
    assert!((mid.placement.scale.x - 1.05).abs() < 1e-6, "{:?}", mid.placement.scale);
    let at_point = |l: &Layer| l.placement.to_canvas().apply(0.25 * l.placement.size.x as f64, 0.75 * l.placement.size.y as f64);
    let (a, b) = (at_point(&mid), at_point(&layer(&plain, Time::from_secs(15))));
    assert!((a.0 - b.0).abs() < 0.01 && (a.1 - b.1).abs() < 0.01, "the point stays put: {a:?} vs {b:?}");
}

/// Spec §1 / review focus 2: a trim stretches the effect over the new
/// visible clip, within its range.
#[test]
fn trimming_stretches_the_effect_over_the_new_visible_clip() {
    let (mut p, id) = project(vec![fx("smooth_zoom", &[])]);
    let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(id).unwrap();
    trim_start(&mut p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci], Time::from_secs(14)); // visible 14..20 s
    assert!((scale(&p, 14_000) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6, "the new first frame starts the zoom");
    assert!((scale(&p, 17_000) - 1.05).abs() < 1e-6, "half way through the shorter clip");
}

/// Spec §1: «Момент» is source-bound. Review focus 4: a moment before the
/// visible start is done on the first frame; one past the end never comes.
#[test]
fn the_punch_moment_stays_on_its_source_frame() {
    // Source 33 s = timeline 13 s.
    let (mut p, id) = project(vec![fx("punch_in", &[("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(33))))])]);
    assert!((scale(&p, 12_900) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6);
    assert!((scale(&p, 13_200) - 1.15).abs() < 1e-5);
    let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(id).unwrap();
    trim_start(&mut p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci], Time::from_secs(12));
    assert!((scale(&p, 12_900) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6, "the content frame decides, not the clip start");
    assert!((scale(&p, 13_200) - 1.15).abs() < 1e-5);
    trim_start(&mut p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci], Time::from_secs(14));
    assert!((scale(&p, 14_000) - 1.15).abs() < 1e-5, "punched before the visible start: held from the first frame");
    let (late, _) = project(vec![fx("punch_in", &[("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(45))))])]);
    assert!((scale(&late, 19_900) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6, "a moment past the end never comes");
}

#[test]
fn an_fx_track_overrides_the_stored_strength() {
    let e = fx("smooth_zoom", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0))]);
    let k = TrackKey::fx(e.id, "strength");
    let (mut p, id) = project(vec![e]);
    let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(id).unwrap();
    let c = &mut p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci];
    c.animation.set_key(k.clone(), SourceTime(Time::from_secs(30)), 0.6, Some(Ease::Hold));
    c.animation.set_key(k, SourceTime(Time::from_secs(36)), 0.0, None); // timeline 16 s
    assert!((scale(&p, 15_000) - 1.05).abs() < 1e-6, "the track's 0.6 before source 36 s");
    assert!((scale(&p, 17_000) - 1.0).abs() < 1e-6, "its 0 after: off");
}

/// Spec §1 / review focus 2: a split never changes the picture — every
/// frame of both halves places the clip where the whole clip did, for
/// every recipe, at 1× and 2× (Handheld's noise and Punch In's moment too).
#[test]
fn a_split_never_moves_the_picture() {
    let mut seed = 0x9e37_79b9_7f4a_7c15u64;
    let mut rnd = |m: u64| {
        seed ^= seed << 13;
        seed ^= seed >> 7;
        seed ^= seed << 17;
        seed % m
    };
    let dirs = ["in", "out", "left", "right", "up", "down"];
    for case in 0..60 {
        let def = &kadr_effects::registry()[rnd(5) as usize];
        let set: Vec<(&str, ParamValue)> = [
            ("strength", ParamValue::Number(rnd(101) as f64 / 100.0)),
            ("point", ParamValue::Point { x: rnd(101) as f64 / 100.0, y: rnd(101) as f64 / 100.0 }),
            ("moment", ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_millis(30_000 + rnd(10_000) as i64)))),
            ("direction", ParamValue::Choice(dirs[rnd(6) as usize].into())),
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .filter(|(k, _)| def.param(k).is_some())
        .collect();
        let (mut p, id) = project(vec![fx(def.id, &set)]);
        if case % 3 == 1 {
            let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(id).unwrap();
            p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci].source_out = Time::from_secs(50); // 2×
        }
        let whole = p.clone();
        let c = p.sequence().clip(id).unwrap().clone();
        let cut = c.timeline_in + F30.mul_ratio(1 + rnd(298) as i64, 1);
        let (l, r) = split_clip(&c, cut, &mut LinkRemap::default());
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips = vec![l, r];
        for f in 0..300 {
            let t = c.timeline_in + F30.mul_ratio(f, 1);
            assert!(same_place(&layer(&p, t), &layer(&whole, t)), "case {case} {} frame {f} (cut at {cut:?})", def.id);
        }
    }
}

/// Transition handles ask for a clip past its edges: still finite.
#[test]
fn an_effect_evaluated_past_the_clip_edge_stays_finite() {
    let (p, id) = project(vec![fx("handheld", &[]), fx("smooth_zoom", &[])]);
    let c = p.sequence().clip(id).unwrap();
    let base = Placement::fill(SizeU::new(1920, 1080));
    for t in [Time::from_secs(-5), Time::from_secs(9), Time::from_secs(25)] {
        let pl = kadr_timeline::fx::place_with_effects(c, t, SizeU::new(1920, 1080), base);
        assert!([pl.position.x, pl.position.y, pl.scale.x, pl.rotation].iter().all(|v| v.is_finite()), "{t:?}: {pl:?}");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `crates/timeline/Cargo.toml` `[dependencies]`: `kadr-effects = { path = "../effects" }`; `crates/timeline/src/lib.rs`: `pub mod fx;` (after `pub mod engine;`). `crates/timeline/src/fx.rs`:

```rust
//! Effects in the evaluator (effects spec §3): for each enabled, known
//! effect of a clip in list order — progress (§1), the parameter values at
//! the frame (`fx:` tracks over stored values), `compile`, and the motion
//! delta composed over the clip's placement.

use kadr_core::Time;
use kadr_effects::{definition, CompileCtx, Params};
use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip, SourceTime, TrackKey};
use kadr_scene::{Placement, SizeU};

/// Spec §1: `from + (to − from) · (t − timeline_in) / duration` — integer
/// flicks into one f64. `t` may lie outside the clip (transition handles).
pub fn effect_progress(clip: &Clip, e: &AppliedEffect, t: Time) -> f64 {
    let d = clip.duration().flicks();
    let frac = if d > 0 { (t - clip.timeline_in).flicks() as f64 / d as f64 } else { 0.0 };
    e.progress_at(frac)
}

/// The noise seed of an effect: from its id, so both halves of a split shake alike.
pub fn effect_seed(e: &AppliedEffect) -> u64 {
    let u = e.id.0.as_u128();
    (u as u64) ^ ((u >> 64) as u64)
}

/// `base` — the clip's own placement at `t` — with the clip's motion
/// effects applied in list order. Unknown recipes and newer versions are
/// skipped; strength 0 compiles nothing (§3).
pub fn place_with_effects(clip: &Clip, t: Time, canvas: SizeU, base: Placement) -> Placement {
    if clip.effects.is_empty() {
        return base;
    }
    let source_time = SourceTime(clip.source_time_at(t));
    let aspect = if base.size.y > 0.0 { (base.size.x / base.size.y) as f64 } else { 1.0 };
    let secs = clip.duration().as_secs_f64();
    let speed = Some(clip.speed()).filter(|s| s.is_finite() && *s > 0.0).unwrap_or(1.0);
    let mut placement = base;
    for e in clip.effects.iter().filter(|e| e.enabled) {
        let Some(def) = definition(e) else { continue };
        let params = Params::resolve(def.params, &e.params, |key| clip.animation.value_at(TrackKey::fx(e.id, key), source_time));
        if params.is_off() {
            continue;
        }
        let span = (e.progress.1 - e.progress.0).abs();
        let clip_secs = if span > 1e-9 { secs / span } else { secs };
        let progress = effect_progress(clip, e, t);
        if !progress.is_finite() || !clip_secs.is_finite() {
            continue;
        }
        let ctx = CompileCtx { progress, clip_secs, source_time, speed, canvas, aspect, frame: t, seed: effect_seed(e) };
        placement = (def.compile)(&params, &ctx).placement.apply(&placement);
    }
    placement
}
```

In `crates/project-scenes/src/lib.rs` replace `ProjectScenes::new` with the pair:

```rust
    /// Snapshots `project`'s active sequence. Whether each file exists is
    /// checked here, once.
    pub fn new(project: &Project, bypass: bool) -> Self {
        Self::new_with(project, bypass, |_| {})
    }

    /// As `new`, with `edit` applied to the snapshot's copy of the sequence
    /// (the hover preview of an effect, effects spec §6.1): the project
    /// itself is never touched. In the «До» view (`bypass`) the copy's clips
    /// have no effects at all — colour is stripped from the layers below,
    /// motion is dropped here.
    pub fn new_with(project: &Project, bypass: bool, edit: impl FnOnce(&mut kadr_project::Sequence)) -> Self {
        let mut seq = project.sequence().clone();
        edit(&mut seq);
        if bypass {
            for c in seq.tracks.iter_mut().flat_map(|t| t.clips.iter_mut()) {
                c.effects.clear();
            }
        }
        let used: std::collections::HashSet<AssetId> = seq.tracks.iter().flat_map(|t| t.clips.iter().map(|c| c.asset)).collect();
        let mut slim = Project::new(project.name.clone());
        slim.id = project.id;
        slim.assets = project.assets.iter().filter(|a| used.contains(&a.id)).cloned().collect();
        let rate = seq.frame_rate;
        let (duration, canvas) = (seq.duration(), SizeU::new(seq.width.max(1), seq.height.max(1)));
        slim.active_sequence = seq.id;
        slim.sequences = vec![seq];
        let media = slim.assets.iter().filter_map(|a| Some((a.id, media_source(a, rate)?))).collect();
        ProjectScenes { duration, rate, canvas, project: Arc::new(slim), bypass, media }
    }
```

`crates/timeline/tests/effect_edits.rs`:

```rust
//! Effect commands (effects spec §7): validation against the registry,
//! one instance per recipe, tracks go with their effect, one undo step.

use kadr_core::{EffectId, FrameRate, Time, TimeRange};
use kadr_project::*;
use kadr_timeline::*;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

fn s(x: i64) -> Time {
    Time::from_secs(x)
}

/// A 10 s clip on V1 and one on A1 (source 100..110 s, timeline 0..10 s).
fn setup() -> (Project, EditEngine, kadr_core::ClipId, kadr_core::ClipId) {
    let mut p = Project::new("t");
    p.sequence_mut().frame_rate = FrameRate::FPS_25;
    let v = Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "v", TimeRange::new(s(100), s(110)), s(0));
    let a = Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "a", TimeRange::new(s(100), s(110)), s(0));
    let (vid, aid) = (v.id, a.id);
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(v);
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[1].clips.push(a);
    (p, EditEngine::new(), vid, aid)
}

fn zoom() -> AppliedEffect {
    AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::from([("strength".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.6))]))
}

fn effects(p: &Project, id: kadr_core::ClipId) -> Vec<AppliedEffect> {
    p.sequence().clip(id).unwrap().effects.clone()
}

/// Entries the engine recorded (undo does not shorten the log).
fn entries(p: &Project) -> usize {
    p.history_log.len()
}

fn push(p: &mut Project, clip: kadr_core::ClipId, e: AppliedEffect) {
    let (ti, ci) = p.sequence().locate_clip(clip).unwrap();
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[ti].clips[ci].effects.push(e);
}

#[test]
fn add_effect_fills_defaults_and_is_one_undo_step() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: zoom() }).unwrap();
    let fx = effects(&p, v);
    assert_eq!(fx.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(fx[0].params["direction"], ParamValue::Choice("in".into()), "missing parameters get defaults");
    assert_eq!(fx[0].params["point"], ParamValue::Point { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 });
    assert_eq!(entries(&p), 1);
    e.undo(&mut p);
    assert!(effects(&p, v).is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn a_recipe_the_clip_has_is_replaced_in_place_with_its_tracks_dropped() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let first = zoom();
    let first_id = first.id;
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: first }).unwrap();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: AppliedEffect::new("handheld", 1, BTreeMap::new()) }).unwrap();
    let k = TrackKey::fx(first_id, "strength");
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: v, param: k.clone(), at: SourceTime(s(102)), value: 0.2, ease: None }).unwrap();
    let mut again = zoom();
    again.params.insert("strength".into(), ParamValue::Number(0.9));
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: again }).unwrap();
    let fx = effects(&p, v);
    assert_eq!(fx.iter().map(|x| x.recipe.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["smooth_zoom", "handheld"], "one instance, same place");
    assert_eq!((fx[0].id, fx[0].number("strength")), (first_id, Some(0.9)), "same id, new parameters");
    assert!(!p.sequence().clip(v).unwrap().animation.is_animated(&k), "its tracks went");
}

#[test]
fn applying_what_is_already_there_changes_nothing() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: zoom() }).unwrap();
    let before = entries(&p);
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: zoom() }), Err(EditError::NoOp));
    assert_eq!(entries(&p), before, "no empty history entry");
}

#[test]
fn unknown_newer_invalid_and_misplaced_effects_are_rejected() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, a) = setup();
    let add = |effect| EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect };
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, add(AppliedEffect::new("glitch_v2", 1, BTreeMap::new()))), Err(EditError::UnknownRecipe("glitch_v2".into())));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, add(AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 2, BTreeMap::new()))), Err(EditError::UnknownRecipe("smooth_zoom".into())));
    let bad = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::from([("strength".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(1.5))]));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, add(bad)), Err(EditError::InvalidParam(m)) if m.contains("strength")));
    let extra = AppliedEffect::new("smooth_zoom", 1, BTreeMap::from([("speed".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(1.0))]));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, add(extra)), Err(EditError::InvalidParam(_))));
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: a, effect: zoom() }), Err(EditError::EffectTarget), "a picture effect on a sound clip");
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].locked = true;
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, add(zoom())), Err(EditError::TrackLocked(_))));
    assert_eq!(entries(&p), 0);
}

#[test]
fn removing_an_effect_takes_its_tracks_and_undo_brings_both_back() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let z = zoom();
    let zid = z.id;
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: z }).unwrap();
    let k = TrackKey::fx(zid, "strength");
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: v, param: k.clone(), at: SourceTime(s(101)), value: 0.3, ease: None }).unwrap();
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: v, id: zid }).unwrap();
    let c = p.sequence().clip(v).unwrap();
    assert!(c.effects.is_empty() && !c.animation.is_animated(&k));
    e.undo(&mut p);
    let c = p.sequence().clip(v).unwrap();
    assert!(c.effect(zid).is_some() && c.animation.is_animated(&k), "one undo step brings both back");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: v, id: EffectId::new() }), Err(EditError::EffectNotFound));
}

#[test]
fn update_merges_parameters_toggles_and_rejects_no_change() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let z = zoom();
    let zid = z.id;
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: z }).unwrap();
    let upd = |params: &[(&str, ParamValue)], enabled| EditCommand::UpdateEffect {
        clip: v,
        id: zid,
        params: params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect(),
        enabled,
    };
    e.execute(&mut p, upd(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.3))], None)).unwrap();
    let fx = &effects(&p, v)[0];
    assert_eq!((fx.number("strength"), fx.params["direction"].clone()), (Some(0.3), ParamValue::Choice("in".into())), "merged, the rest kept");
    e.execute(&mut p, upd(&[], Some(false))).unwrap();
    assert!(!effects(&p, v)[0].enabled);
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, upd(&[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.3))], Some(false))), Err(EditError::NoOp));
    assert!(matches!(e.execute(&mut p, upd(&[("direction", ParamValue::Choice("left".into()))], None)), Err(EditError::InvalidParam(_))));
    let missing = EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: v, id: EffectId::new(), params: BTreeMap::new(), enabled: Some(true) };
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, missing), Err(EditError::EffectNotFound));
}

/// Review focus 5: an effect from a newer Kadr is kept — moved or removed,
/// never changed (its parameters cannot be validated here).
#[test]
fn an_unknown_effect_is_kept_moved_or_removed_but_not_changed() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let future = AppliedEffect::new("glitch_v2", 3, BTreeMap::from([("amount".to_string(), ParamValue::Number(0.5))]));
    let fid = future.id;
    push(&mut p, v, future);
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: zoom() }).unwrap();
    let upd = EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: v, id: fid, params: BTreeMap::new(), enabled: Some(false) };
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, upd), Err(EditError::UnknownRecipe("glitch_v2".into())));
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip: v, id: fid, to: 1 }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(effects(&p, v)[1].id, fid);
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: v, id: fid }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(effects(&p, v).iter().map(|x| x.recipe.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["smooth_zoom"]);
}

#[test]
fn move_effect_reorders_clamps_and_rejects_no_change() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let (a, b, c) = (zoom(), AppliedEffect::new("handheld", 1, BTreeMap::new()), AppliedEffect::new("smooth_pan", 1, BTreeMap::new()));
    let ids = [a.id, b.id, c.id];
    for x in [a, b, c] {
        e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: x }).unwrap();
    }
    e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip: v, id: ids[0], to: 99 }).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(effects(&p, v).iter().map(|x| x.id).collect::<Vec<_>>(), [ids[1], ids[2], ids[0]], "clamped to the end");
    assert_eq!(e.execute(&mut p, EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip: v, id: ids[1], to: 0 }), Err(EditError::NoOp));
}

#[test]
fn applying_to_several_clips_is_one_undo_entry() {
    let (mut p, mut e, v, _) = setup();
    let w = Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "w", TimeRange::new(s(0), s(5)), s(20));
    let wid = w.id;
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(w);
    let batch = EditCommand::Batch { label: "Эффект: Плавный наезд".into(), commands: vec![EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: v, effect: zoom() }, EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: wid, effect: zoom() }] };
    e.execute(&mut p, batch).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(entries(&p), 1);
    e.undo(&mut p);
    assert!(effects(&p, v).is_empty() && effects(&p, wid).is_empty());
}

#[test]
fn effect_commands_have_labels_and_error_keys() {
    let (clip, id) = (kadr_core::ClipId::new(), EffectId::new());
    assert_eq!(EditCommand::AddEffect { clip, effect: zoom() }.label(), "cmd.add_effect");
    assert_eq!(EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip, id }.label(), "cmd.remove_effect");
    assert_eq!(EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip, id, params: BTreeMap::new(), enabled: None }.label(), "cmd.update_effect");
    assert_eq!(EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip, id, to: 0 }.label(), "cmd.move_effect");
    for (err, key) in [
        (EditError::UnknownRecipe("x".into()), "err.edit.unknown_recipe"),
        (EditError::EffectTarget, "err.edit.effect_target"),
        (EditError::InvalidParam("x".into()), "err.edit.invalid_param"),
    ] {
        assert_eq!(EditCommand::error_key(&err), key);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — in `crates/project/src/effects.rs` add to `impl Clip`:

```rust
    /// Applies `e`, one instance per recipe (spec §2): a new recipe is
    /// appended; one the clip has is replaced in place — its id and position
    /// stay, its `fx:` tracks go. `false` when nothing changed.
    pub fn put_effect(&mut self, mut e: AppliedEffect) -> bool {
        match self.effects.iter().position(|x| x.recipe == e.recipe) {
            None => {
                if self.effect(e.id).is_some() {
                    e.id = EffectId::new(); // never two effects with one id on a clip
                }
                self.effects.push(e);
                true
            }
            Some(i) => {
                e.id = self.effects[i].id;
                let had_tracks = self.animation.remove_effect(e.id);
                if !had_tracks && self.effects[i] == e {
                    return false;
                }
                self.effects[i] = e;
                true
            }
        }
    }
```

New variants (before `Batch`):

```rust
    /// Applies an effect (effects spec §7), validated against the registry:
    /// a known recipe at its current version, a clip it fits, valid
    /// parameters (missing ones get defaults). A recipe the clip has is
    /// replaced in place — same id and position, the new parameters,
    /// enabled, progress (0, 1), its `fx:` tracks dropped (spec §2).
    AddEffect { clip: ClipId, effect: AppliedEffect },
    /// Removes an effect and its `fx:` tracks.
    RemoveEffect { clip: ClipId, id: EffectId },
    /// Sets the given parameters (validated, merged into the stored ones)
    /// and/or `enabled`. A recipe from a newer Kadr cannot be changed.
    UpdateEffect { clip: ClipId, id: EffectId, params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, enabled: Option<bool> },
    /// Moves an effect to position `to` (clamped) of the clip's list.
    MoveEffect { clip: ClipId, id: EffectId, to: usize },
```

`label()` arms:

```rust
            EditCommand::AddEffect { .. } => "cmd.add_effect",
            EditCommand::RemoveEffect { .. } => "cmd.remove_effect",
            EditCommand::UpdateEffect { .. } => "cmd.update_effect",
            EditCommand::MoveEffect { .. } => "cmd.move_effect",
```

`error_key()` arms:

```rust
            EditError::UnknownRecipe(_) => "err.edit.unknown_recipe",
            EditError::EffectTarget => "err.edit.effect_target",
            EditError::InvalidParam(_) => "err.edit.invalid_param",
```

`apply()` arms (before `Batch`):

```rust
            EditCommand::AddEffect { clip, effect } => {
                let def = kadr_effects::recipe(&effect.recipe)
                    .filter(|d| d.version == effect.version)
                    .ok_or_else(|| EditError::UnknownRecipe(effect.recipe.clone()))?;
                let (ti, ci) = seq.locate_clip(*clip).ok_or(EditError::ClipNotFound)?;
                let t = &mut seq.tracks[ti];
                if t.locked {
                    return Err(EditError::TrackLocked(t.name.clone()));
                }
                if !kadr_effects::fits(def.target, t.kind) {
                    return Err(EditError::EffectTarget);
                }
                kadr_effects::validate(def.params, &effect.params).map_err(EditError::InvalidParam)?;
                let c = &mut t.clips[ci];
                let mut e = effect.clone();
                // The moment of applying is unknown here: the clip's middle (refinement 4).
                let middle = c.timeline_in + Time(c.duration().flicks() / 2);
                kadr_effects::complete(def, &mut e.params, c, middle);
                e.enabled = true;
                e.progress = (0.0, 1.0);
                if c.put_effect(e) { Ok(()) } else { Err(EditError::NoOp) }
            }
            EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip, id } => {
                let c = effect_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                let i = c.effects.iter().position(|e| e.id == *id).ok_or(EditError::EffectNotFound)?;
                c.effects.remove(i);
                c.animation.remove_effect(*id);
                Ok(())
            }
            EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip, id, params, enabled } => {
                let c = effect_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                let e = c.effect_mut(*id).ok_or(EditError::EffectNotFound)?;
                // A recipe this build cannot run cannot be validated, so it is not changed (spec §7).
                let def = kadr_effects::definition(e).ok_or_else(|| EditError::UnknownRecipe(e.recipe.clone()))?;
                kadr_effects::validate(def.params, params).map_err(EditError::InvalidParam)?;
                let before = e.clone();
                e.params.extend(params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), v.clone())));
                if let Some(on) = enabled {
                    e.enabled = *on;
                }
                if *e == before { Err(EditError::NoOp) } else { Ok(()) }
            }
            EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip, id, to } => {
                let c = effect_clip(seq, *clip)?;
                let from = c.effects.iter().position(|e| e.id == *id).ok_or(EditError::EffectNotFound)?;
                let to = (*to).min(c.effects.len() - 1);
                if from == to {
                    return Err(EditError::NoOp);
                }
                let e = c.effects.remove(from);
                c.effects.insert(to, e);
                Ok(())
            }
```

and a helper next to `anim_clip`:

```rust
/// The unlocked clip `id` for an effect edit (any track kind: audio effects are E4).
fn effect_clip(seq: &mut Sequence, id: ClipId) -> Result<&mut Clip, EditError> {
    let (ti, ci) = seq.locate_clip(id).ok_or(EditError::ClipNotFound)?;
    let t = &mut seq.tracks[ti];
    if t.locked {
        return Err(EditError::TrackLocked(t.name.clone()));
    }
    Ok(&mut t.clips[ci])
}
```

`UpdateEffect` mutates only after both checks; a `NoOp` there leaves `e` as it was (the merge produced the same values), so the command stays atomic inside a `Batch`.

i18n (both catalogs):

| key | en | ru |
|---|---|---|
| `cmd.add_effect` | Apply effect | Применение эффекта |
| `cmd.remove_effect` | Remove effect | Удаление эффекта |
| `cmd.update_effect` | Change effect | Изменение эффекта |
| `cmd.move_effect` | Reorder effects | Порядок эффектов |
| `err.edit.unknown_recipe` | This effect is not available in this version of Kadr | Этот эффект недоступен в этой версии Kadr |
| `err.edit.effect_target` | This effect does not fit this clip | Этот эффект не подходит для такого клипа |
| `err.edit.invalid_param` | Invalid effect setting | Недопустимое значение параметра эффекта |

```rust
        assert_eq!(all.as_array().unwrap().len(), 5);
        let zoom = &all[0];
        assert_eq!((zoom["id"].as_str(), zoom["category"].as_str(), zoom["target"].as_str()), (Some("smooth_zoom"), Some("motion"), Some("clip")));
        assert_eq!(zoom["params"][0], json!({"key": "direction", "type": "choice", "options": ["in", "out"], "default": "in"}));
        assert_eq!(zoom["params"][1], json!({"key": "strength", "type": "number", "min": 0.0, "max": 1.0, "default": 0.6, "animatable": true}));
        assert_eq!(zoom["params"][2]["type"], "point");
    }

    #[test]
    fn params_from_json_converts_and_checks_each_value() {
        let c = clip();
        let def = recipe("punch_in").unwrap();
        let p = params_from_json(def, &c, &obj(json!({"strength": 0.9, "point": {"x": 0.25, "y": 1}, "moment": {"at_ms": 12_000}}))).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(p["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.9));
        assert_eq!(p["point"], ParamValue::Point { x: 0.25, y: 1.0 });
        assert_eq!(p["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(32))));
        let src = params_from_json(def, &c, &obj(json!({"moment": {"source_ms": 45_000}}))).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(src["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(45))), "source_ms may lie off the clip");
        let err = |j: Value| params_from_json(def, &c, &obj(j)).unwrap_err();
        assert!(err(json!({"strength": 1.4})).contains("strength must be 0..1"));
        assert!(err(json!({"strength": "high"})).contains("strength must be a number"));
        assert!(err(json!({"speed": 1})).contains("punch_in has no parameter \"speed\""));
        assert!(err(json!({"point": {"x": 0.5}})).contains("point must be"));
        assert!(err(json!({"moment": 12_000})).contains("moment must be"));
        assert!(err(json!({"moment": {"at_ms": i64::MIN}})).contains("within"));
    }

    /// Review focus 4: a timeline moment off the visible clip is refused.
    #[test]
    fn params_from_json_rejects_a_moment_off_the_clip() {
        let def = recipe("punch_in").unwrap();
        let msg = params_from_json(def, &clip(), &obj(json!({"moment": {"at_ms": 25_000}}))).unwrap_err();
        assert!(msg.contains("outside the clip (10000..20000 ms)") && msg.contains("source_ms"), "{msg}");
        assert!(params_from_json(def, &clip(), &obj(json!({"moment": {"at_ms": 20_000}}))).is_err(), "timeline_out is outside");
    }

    #[test]
    fn effects_are_shown_with_both_times_of_a_moment() {
        let c = clip();
        let mut e = crate::fresh(recipe("punch_in").unwrap(), &c, Time::from_secs(12));
        e.params.insert("moment".into(), ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(45))));
        let j = effect_json(&c, &e);
        assert_eq!(j["recipe"], "punch_in");
        assert_eq!((j["known"].as_bool(), j["enabled"].as_bool()), (Some(true), Some(true)));
        assert_eq!(j["progress"], json!([0.0, 1.0]));
        assert_eq!(j["params"]["moment"], json!({"at_ms": 25_000, "source_ms": 45_000, "visible": false}));
        assert_eq!(j["params"]["strength"], json!(0.6));
        assert_eq!(effect_json(&c, &AppliedEffect::new("glitch_v2", 1, BTreeMap::new()))["known"], false);
    }
}
```

```rust
    /// V1 10..20 s (source 30..40 s) and a sound clip on A1.
    fn fx_project() -> (Project, String) {
        let mut p = Project::new("fx");
        let c = Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(30), Time::from_secs(40)), Time::from_secs(10));
        let id = c.id.to_string();
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
        let a = Clip::new(kadr_core::AssetId::new(), "a", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(5)), Time::ZERO);
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[1].clips.push(a);
        (p, id)
    }

    fn cmd(j: serde_json::Value) -> AiCommand {
        serde_json::from_value(j).unwrap()

    #[test]
    fn add_effect_is_checked_against_the_registry() {
        let (p, id) = fx_project();
        let perms = Permissions::default();
        let ok = validate(&[cmd(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "punch_in", "params": {"strength": 0.9, "moment": {"at_ms": 12_000}}}))], &p, &perms).unwrap();
        let EditCommand::AddEffect { effect, .. } = &ok[0] else { panic!("{ok:?}") };
        assert_eq!(effect.params["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.9));
        assert_eq!(effect.params["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(32))));
        assert_eq!(effect.params["point"], ParamValue::Point { x: 0.5, y: 0.5 }, "defaults filled");
        let err = |j: serde_json::Value| validate(&[cmd(j)], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "glitch"})).contains("unknown effect \"glitch\""));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "handheld", "params": {"strength": 2.0}})).contains("strength"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "handheld", "params": {"speed": 1}})).contains("no parameter"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "punch_in", "params": {"moment": {"at_ms": 25_000}}})).contains("outside the clip"));
        let audio = p.sequence().tracks[1].clips[0].id.to_string();
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": audio, "recipe": "handheld"})).contains("video clips"));
        assert!(serde_json::from_value::<AiCommand>(json!({"type": "add_effect", "clip_id": id, "recipe": "handheld", "extra": 1})).is_err(), "unknown fields are rejected");
    }

    /// R24 for effects: a number with keyframes ignores its stored value,
    /// so writing it is rejected with the reason.
    #[test]
    fn set_effect_rejects_animated_params_no_change_and_newer_effects() {
        let (mut p, id) = fx_project();
        let cid = kadr_core::ClipId::parse(&id).unwrap();
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_zoom").unwrap(), p.sequence().clip(cid).unwrap(), Time::from_secs(12));
        let fid = e.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].effects.push(e);
        let perms = Permissions::default();
        let set = |params: serde_json::Value| cmd(json!({"type": "set_effect", "clip_id": id, "effect_id": fid.to_string(), "params": params}));
        let ok = validate(&[set(json!({"direction": "out"}))], &p, &perms).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(&ok[0], EditCommand::UpdateEffect { params, enabled: None, .. } if params["direction"] == ParamValue::Choice("out".into())));
        assert!(validate(&[set(json!({"direction": "in"}))], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("no change"));
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].animation.set_key(TrackKey::fx(fid, "strength"), SourceTime(Time::from_secs(31)), 0.2, None);
        assert_eq!(validate(&[set(json!({"strength": 0.9}))], &p, &perms).unwrap_err(), ValidationError::Animated { index: 0, param: "strength" });
        assert!(validate(&[set(json!({"direction": "out"}))], &p, &perms).is_ok(), "only the given params count");
        let future = AppliedEffect::new("glitch_v2", 1, Default::default());
        let gid = future.id.to_string();
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].effects.push(future);
        let msg = validate(&[cmd(json!({"type": "set_effect", "clip_id": id, "effect_id": gid, "enabled": false}))], &p, &perms).unwrap_err().to_string();
        assert!(msg.contains("newer Kadr"), "{msg}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn remove_effect_is_a_destructive_edit() {
        let (mut p, id) = fx_project();
        let cid = kadr_core::ClipId::parse(&id).unwrap();
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("handheld").unwrap(), p.sequence().clip(cid).unwrap(), Time::ZERO);
        let fid = e.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].effects.push(e);
        let rm = cmd(json!({"type": "remove_effect", "clip_id": id, "effect_id": fid.to_string()}));
        assert_eq!(validate(&[rm.clone()], &p, &Permissions::default()).unwrap(), vec![EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: cid, id: fid }]);
        let no = Permissions { allow_destructive: false, max_commands: 10 };
        assert!(matches!(validate(&[rm], &p, &no), Err(ValidationError::Permission { .. })));
        let gone = cmd(json!({"type": "remove_effect", "clip_id": id, "effect_id": kadr_core::EffectId::new().to_string()}));
        assert!(validate(&[gone], &p, &Permissions::default()).unwrap_err().to_string().contains("no effect"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_prompt_schema_and_context_know_the_effects() {
        let s = schema_for_prompt();
        for d in kadr_effects::registry() {
            assert!(s.contains(d.id), "schema lacks {}", d.id);
        }
        assert!(s.contains("\"add_effect\"") && s.contains("\"set_effect\"") && s.contains("\"remove_effect\""));
        let (mut p, id) = fx_project();
        let cid = kadr_core::ClipId::parse(&id).unwrap();
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("handheld").unwrap(), p.sequence().clip(cid).unwrap(), Time::ZERO);
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].effects.push(e);
        let ctx = crate::context::project_context(&p, &Default::default());
        assert_eq!(ctx["tracks"][0]["clips"][0]["effects"][0]["recipe"], "handheld");
        assert!(ctx["tracks"][1]["clips"][0].get("effects").is_none(), "a clip without effects says nothing");
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `json.rs`** (above its tests) and add `pub mod json;` to `lib.rs`:

```rust
//! JSON forms of effects for MCP and the AI assistant (effects spec §7):
//! the registry, an effect as `get_state` shows it, and parameters as
//! clients write them — numbers, choice strings, `{x, y}` points and
//! `{at_ms}` / `{source_ms}` moments (`at_ms` is timeline time inside the clip).

use crate::params::{ParamDef, ParamKind};
use crate::registry::{definition, registry, RecipeDef};
use kadr_core::Time;
use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip, ParamValue, SourceTime};
use serde_json::{json, Map, Value};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

/// Largest |milliseconds| accepted, as MCP's other ops.
const MAX_MS: u64 = 10_000_000_000;

pub fn param_schema(d: &ParamDef) -> Value {
    match d.kind {
        ParamKind::Number { min, max, default } => json!({"key": d.key, "type": "number", "min": min, "max": max, "default": default, "animatable": true}),
        ParamKind::Choice { options, default } => json!({"key": d.key, "type": "choice", "options": options, "default": default}),
        ParamKind::Point => json!({"key": d.key, "type": "point", "default": {"x": 0.5, "y": 0.5}}),
        ParamKind::Moment => json!({"key": d.key, "type": "moment", "default": "the source frame where the effect is applied (MCP / AI: the clip's middle)"}),
    }
}

pub fn recipe_json(d: &RecipeDef) -> Value {
    json!({
        "id": d.id, "category": d.category.key(), "target": d.target.key(), "version": d.version,
        "params": d.params.iter().map(param_schema).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
    })
}

/// `list_effects`: every recipe in card order.
pub fn registry_json() -> Value {
    Value::Array(registry().iter().map(recipe_json).collect())
}

/// A stored value as clients read it; a moment in both time domains.
pub fn value_json(clip: &Clip, v: &ParamValue) -> Value {
    match v {
        ParamValue::Number(n) => json!(n),
        ParamValue::Choice(c) => json!(c),
        ParamValue::Point { x, y } => json!({"x": x, "y": y}),
        ParamValue::Color(c) => json!(c),
        ParamValue::Moment(m) => {
            let tl = clip.timeline_time_of(m.0);
            json!({"at_ms": tl.as_millis(), "source_ms": m.0.as_millis(), "visible": tl >= clip.timeline_in && tl < clip.timeline_out})
        }
        ParamValue::Unknown(v) => v.clone(),
    }
}

/// An effect as `get_state` and the AI context show it; `known: false` for
/// a recipe or version from a newer Kadr.
pub fn effect_json(clip: &Clip, e: &AppliedEffect) -> Value {
    json!({
        "id": e.id.to_string(), "recipe": e.recipe, "version": e.version, "enabled": e.enabled,
        "known": definition(e).is_some(), "progress": [e.progress.0, e.progress.1],
        "params": e.params.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.clone(), value_json(clip, v))).collect::<Map<_, _>>(),
    })
}

/// Client parameters for `def` on `clip`, each checked like a command
/// would; the error names the key and the reason.
pub fn params_from_json(def: &RecipeDef, clip: &Clip, json: &Map<String, Value>) -> Result<BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, String> {
    let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
    for (k, v) in json {
        let d = def
            .param(k)
            .ok_or_else(|| format!("{} has no parameter {k:?} (it has {})", def.id, def.params.iter().map(|d| d.key).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")))?;
        let value = match d.kind {
            ParamKind::Number { .. } => ParamValue::Number(v.as_f64().ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be a number"))?),
            ParamKind::Choice { .. } => ParamValue::Choice(v.as_str().ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be a string"))?.to_string()),
            ParamKind::Point => {
                let o = v.as_object().filter(|o| o.len() == 2).ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be {{\"x\": 0..1, \"y\": 0..1}}"))?;
                let get = |c: &str| o.get(c).and_then(Value::as_f64).ok_or_else(|| format!("{k} must be {{\"x\": 0..1, \"y\": 0..1}}"));
                ParamValue::Point { x: get("x")?, y: get("y")? }
            }
            ParamKind::Moment => ParamValue::Moment(moment_from_json(clip, k, v)?),
        };
        d.check(&value)?;
        out.insert(k.clone(), value);
    }
    Ok(out)
}

fn moment_from_json(clip: &Clip, k: &str, v: &Value) -> Result<SourceTime, String> {
    let shape = || format!("{k} must be {{\"at_ms\": int}} (timeline, inside the clip) or {{\"source_ms\": int}}");
    let o = v.as_object().filter(|o| o.len() == 1).ok_or_else(shape)?;
    let ms = |key: &str| -> Result<Option<i64>, String> {
        o.get(key)
            .map(|x| x.as_i64().filter(|m| m.unsigned_abs() <= MAX_MS).ok_or_else(|| format!("{k}.{key} must be an integer within ±{MAX_MS}")))
            .transpose()
    };
    if let Some(ms) = ms("at_ms")? {
        let t = Time::from_millis(ms);
        if t < clip.timeline_in || t >= clip.timeline_out {
            return Err(format!(
                "{k}.at_ms {ms} is outside the clip ({}..{} ms); use source_ms for a moment off the clip",
                clip.timeline_in.as_millis(),
                clip.timeline_out.as_millis()
            ));
        }
        return Ok(SourceTime(clip.source_time_at(t)));
    }
    match ms("source_ms")? {
        Some(ms) => Ok(SourceTime(Time::from_millis(ms))),
        None => Err(shape()),
    }
}
```

`reason()`: add `| AiCommand::AddEffect { reason, .. } | AiCommand::SetEffect { reason, .. } | AiCommand::RemoveEffect { reason, .. }` to the arm that returns `reason`.

`validate()` arms (before `AddCaption`):

```rust
            AiCommand::AddEffect { clip_id, recipe, params, .. } => {
                let id = clip(i, clip_id)?;
                let c = seq.clip(id).unwrap();
                let range = |msg: String| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg };
                let ids = || kadr_effects::registry().iter().map(|d| d.id).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
                let def = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("unknown effect {recipe:?} (one of {})", ids())))?;
                let (ti, _) = seq.locate_clip(id).unwrap();
                if !kadr_effects::fits(def.target, seq.tracks[ti].kind) {
                    return Err(range(format!("{recipe} is for video clips")));
                }
                let mut values = kadr_effects::json::params_from_json(def, c, params).map_err(range)?;
                kadr_effects::complete(def, &mut values, c, c.timeline_in + Time(c.duration().flicks() / 2));
                let effect = AppliedEffect::new(def.id, def.version, values);
                if !c.clone().put_effect(effect.clone()) {
                    return Err(range(format!("no change: the clip already has {recipe} with these parameters")));
                }
                EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: id, effect }
            }
            AiCommand::SetEffect { clip_id, effect_id, params, enabled, .. } => {
                let id = clip(i, clip_id)?;
                let c = seq.clip(id).unwrap();
                let range = |msg: String| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg };
                let e = EffectId::parse(effect_id).and_then(|e| c.effect(e)).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("the clip has no effect {effect_id}")))?;
                let def = kadr_effects::definition(e).ok_or_else(|| range(format!("effect {} ({}) was made by a newer Kadr and cannot be changed", e.id, e.recipe)))?;
                if params.is_empty() && enabled.is_none() {
                    return Err(range("give params and/or enabled".into()));
                }
                let values = kadr_effects::json::params_from_json(def, c, params).map_err(range)?;
                // R24: a number with keyframes ignores its stored value.
                if let Some(d) = def.params.iter().find(|d| values.contains_key(d.key) && c.animation.is_animated(TrackKey::fx(e.id, d.key))) {
                    return Err(ValidationError::Animated { index: i, param: d.key });
                }
                let mut probe = e.clone();
                probe.params.extend(values.clone());
                if let Some(on) = enabled {
                    probe.enabled = *on;
                }
                if probe == *e {
                    return Err(range("no change: the effect already has these settings".into()));
                }
                EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: id, id: e.id, params: values, enabled: *enabled }
            }
            AiCommand::RemoveEffect { clip_id, effect_id, .. } => {
                destructive(i)?;
                let id = clip(i, clip_id)?;
                let e = EffectId::parse(effect_id)
                    .filter(|e| seq.clip(id).unwrap().effect(*e).is_some())
                    .ok_or_else(|| ValidationError::Range { index: i, msg: format!("the clip has no effect {effect_id}") })?;
                EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: id, id: e }
            }
```

`schema_for_prompt` becomes:

```rust
/// JSON schema description embedded in LLM prompts.
pub fn schema_for_prompt() -> String {
    let effects: Vec<String> = kadr_effects::registry()
        .iter()
        .map(|d| {
            let params: Vec<String> = d
                .params
                .iter()
                .map(|p| match p.kind {
                    kadr_effects::ParamKind::Number { min, max, default } => format!("{} {min}..{max} (default {default})", p.key),
                    kadr_effects::ParamKind::Choice { options, .. } => format!("{} {}", p.key, options.join("|")),
                    kadr_effects::ParamKind::Point => format!("{} {{\"x\":0..1,\"y\":0..1}}", p.key),
                    kadr_effects::ParamKind::Moment => format!("{} {{\"at_ms\":int}}", p.key),
                })
                .collect();
            format!("   {}: {}", d.id, params.join(", "))
        })
        .collect();
    format!(
        r#"Respond with ONLY a JSON object: {{"summary": string, "confidence": 0..1, "commands": [Command...]}}
Command is one of (times are sequence milliseconds, integers):
 {{"type":"split_clip","at_ms":int,"clip_id"?:string,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"delete_range","start_ms":int,"end_ms":int,"ripple":bool,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"delete_clip","clip_id":string,"ripple":bool,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"trim_clip","clip_id":string,"edge":"start"|"end","to_ms":int,"ripple":bool,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"move_clip","clip_id":string,"to_ms":int,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"set_transform","clip_id":string,"scale"?:num,"x"?:num,"y"?:num,"rotation_deg"?:num,"opacity"?:num,"reason":string}} (not for a property with keyframes: rejected)
 {{"type":"change_speed","clip_id":string,"speed":num,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"set_audio_gain","clip_id":string,"gain_db":num,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"add_marker","at_ms":int,"name":string,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"add_transition","at_ms":int,"kind":"cross_dissolve"|"dip_to_black"|"wipe","duration_ms":int,"reason":string}}
 {{"type":"add_effect","clip_id":string,"recipe":string,"params"?:{{...}},"reason":string}} (video clips; one per recipe per clip: an existing one is replaced; omitted params get defaults, strength 0.6 = normal)
 {{"type":"set_effect","clip_id":string,"effect_id":string,"params"?:{{...}},"enabled"?:bool,"reason":string}} (effect ids are in each clip's "effects"; not for a param with keyframes: rejected)
 {{"type":"remove_effect","clip_id":string,"effect_id":string,"reason":string}}
Effects (recipe: params):
{}
When deleting several ranges with ripple, list them from the LATEST to the EARLIEST.
If the request cannot be done with these commands, return an empty commands list and explain in summary."#,
        effects.join("\n")
    )
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_core::{AssetId, Time, TimeRange};
    use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip};
    use serde_json::json;

    fn project() -> (Project, String, Vec<String>) {
        let mut p = Project::new("t");
        let mut c = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(10)), Time::ZERO);
        let ids: Vec<String> = ["smooth_zoom", "handheld"].iter().map(|r| {
            let e = AppliedEffect::new(*r, 1, Default::default());
            let id = e.id.to_string();
            c.effects.push(e);
            id
        }).collect();
        let cid = c.id.to_string();
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
        (p, cid, ids)
    }

    fn op(p: &Project, v: Value) -> Result<Vec<EditCommand>, BridgeError> {
        fx_op(p, p.sequence(), &v).expect("owned op")
    }

    #[test]
    fn move_effect_names_its_reasons() {
        let (p, c, ids) = project();
        let ok = op(&p, json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": ids[0], "to": 1})).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(ok[..], [EditCommand::MoveEffect { to: 1, .. }]));
        let err = |v: Value| op(&p, v).unwrap_err().message;
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": ids[0], "to": 0})).contains("no change"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": ids[0], "to": 2})).contains("past the last position (1)"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": ids[0], "to": -1})).contains("non-negative"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": ids[0], "to": 1, "speed": 2})).contains("unknown field `speed`"));
        assert!(err(json!({"type": "move_effect", "clip_id": c, "effect_id": kadr_core::EffectId::new().to_string(), "to": 1})).contains("no effect"));
        assert!(fx_op(&p, p.sequence(), &json!({"type": "split_clip"})).is_none(), "not ours");
    }

    #[test]
    fn list_effects_names_each_recipe_in_both_languages() {
        let v = list_effects();
        let all = v["effects"].as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(all.len(), kadr_effects::registry().len());
        assert_eq!(all[0]["name"], json!({"ru": "Плавный наезд", "en": "Smooth Zoom"}));
        assert_eq!(all[0]["params"][1]["key"], "strength");
    }
}
```

```rust
    #[test]
    fn effect_numbers_animate_through_the_keyframe_ops() {
        let (mut p, id) = project();
        let cid = ClipId::parse(&id).unwrap();
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_zoom").unwrap(), p.sequence().clip(cid).unwrap(), Time::from_secs(6));
        let fid = e.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips[0].effects.push(e);
        let strength = format!("fx:{fid}:strength");
        run(&mut p, json!({"type": "set_keyframe", "clip_id": id, "param": strength, "value": 0.9, "at_ms": 6_000})).unwrap();
        assert!(p.sequence().tracks[0].clips[0].animation.is_animated(TrackKey::fx(fid, "strength")));
        let err = |p: &Project, op: Value| run(&mut p.clone(), op).unwrap_err().message;
        assert!(err(&p, json!({"type": "set_keyframe", "clip_id": id, "param": strength, "value": 1.5, "at_ms": 7_000})).contains("out of range 0..1"));
        assert!(err(&p, json!({"type": "set_keyframe", "clip_id": id, "param": format!("fx:{fid}:direction"), "value": 1.0, "at_ms": 7_000})).contains("only numbers"));
        assert!(err(&p, json!({"type": "set_keyframe", "clip_id": id, "param": format!("fx:{}:strength", kadr_core::EffectId::new()), "value": 0.5, "at_ms": 7_000})).contains("no effect"));
        assert!(err(&p, json!({"type": "set_keyframe", "clip_id": id, "param": "fx:junk", "value": 0.5, "at_ms": 7_000})).contains("malformed"));
        run(&mut p, json!({"type": "clear_animation", "clip_id": id, "params": [strength]})).unwrap();
        let c = &p.sequence().tracks[0].clips[0];
        assert!(c.animation.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(c.effect(fid).unwrap().number("strength"), Some(0.6), "the effect's default");
    }
```

`crates/i18n/src/lib.rs` (after `t`):

```rust
/// `key` in language `l`, whatever the UI language (search by either name,
/// MCP names); English, then the key itself, when missing.
pub fn t_in(l: Lang, key: &str) -> String {
    let (en, ru) = catalogs();
    let c = if l == Lang::Ru { ru } else { en };
    c.get(key).or_else(|| en.get(key)).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| key.to_string())
}
```

`apps/editor/src/mcp_fx.rs` (above its tests):

```rust
//! MCP for effects (effects spec §7): `list_effects` and the `edit` op
//! `move_effect`. `add_effect` / `set_effect` / `remove_effect` are AI
//! commands (validated in kadr-ai), reached through `edit`'s fallback.
//! Pure: tested without a window.

use kadr_core::{ClipId, EffectId};
use kadr_i18n::{t_in, Lang};
use kadr_mcp_bridge::BridgeError;
use kadr_project::{Project, Sequence};
use kadr_timeline::EditCommand;
use serde_json::{json, Value};

fn bad(msg: impl Into<String>) -> BridgeError {
    BridgeError::new("edit_rejected", msg.into())
}

/// `list_effects`: the registry, each recipe named in both languages.
pub fn list_effects() -> Value {
    let all: Vec<Value> = kadr_effects::registry()
        .iter()
        .map(|d| {
            let mut v = kadr_effects::json::recipe_json(d);
            v["name"] = json!({"ru": t_in(Lang::Ru, &d.name_key()), "en": t_in(Lang::En, &d.name_key())});
            v
        })
        .collect();
    json!({ "effects": all })
}

/// `move_effect {clip_id, effect_id, to}`; `None` for every other `type`.
pub fn fx_op(_project: &Project, seq: &Sequence, op: &Value) -> Option<Result<Vec<EditCommand>, BridgeError>> {
    if op.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str)? != "move_effect" {
        return None;
    }
    Some((|| {
        if let Some(extra) = op.as_object().into_iter().flat_map(|o| o.keys()).find(|k| !matches!(k.as_str(), "type" | "reason" | "clip_id" | "effect_id" | "to")) {
            return Err(bad(format!("unknown field `{extra}` for move_effect")));
        }
        let clip = op.get("clip_id").and_then(Value::as_str).and_then(ClipId::parse).ok_or_else(|| bad("clip_id missing or malformed"))?;
        let c = seq.clip(clip).ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("unknown clip {clip}")))?;
        if seq.locate_clip(clip).is_some_and(|(ti, _)| seq.tracks[ti].locked) {
            return Err(bad("the clip's track is locked"));
        }
        let id = op.get("effect_id").and_then(Value::as_str).and_then(EffectId::parse).ok_or_else(|| bad("effect_id missing or malformed"))?;
        let from = c.effects.iter().position(|e| e.id == id).ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("the clip has no effect {id}")))?;
        let to = op
            .get("to")
            .and_then(Value::as_u64)
            .ok_or_else(|| bad("`to` must be a non-negative integer: the position in the clip's effect list, 0 = applied first"))? as usize;
        if to >= c.effects.len() {
            return Err(bad(format!("`to` {to} is past the last position ({})", c.effects.len() - 1)));
        }
        if to == from {
            return Err(bad("no change: the effect is already there"));
        }
        Ok(vec![EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip, id, to }])
    })())
}
```

`apps/editor/src/mcp_anim.rs` — keyframe ops address effect numbers. Import `kadr_effects::ParamKind`. Replace `param_named`, `param`, `value_range` and add `default_of`:

```rust
/// A keyframe op's `param`: a clip property, or `fx:<effect-id>:<key>` — a
/// number of a known effect on this clip.
fn param_named(c: &Clip, k: &str) -> Result<TrackKey, BridgeError> {
    if k.starts_with("fx:") {
        let Some(TrackKey::Fx(f)) = TrackKey::parse(k) else {
            return Err(bad(format!("malformed effect param {k:?} (fx:<effect-id>:<key>)")));
        };
        let e = c.effect(f.effect).ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("the clip has no effect {}", f.effect)))?;
        let def = kadr_effects::definition(e).ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("effect {} ({}) was made by a newer Kadr and cannot be animated", e.id, e.recipe)))?;
        let d = def.param(&f.key).ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("{} has no parameter {:?}", def.id, f.key)))?;
        if !d.animatable() {
            return Err(bad(format!("{} is a {}: only numbers can be animated", f.key, d.kind.type_name())));
        }
        return Ok(TrackKey::Fx(f));
    }
    AnimParam::from_key(k)
        .map(TrackKey::Clip)
        .ok_or_else(|| bad(format!("unknown param {k:?} (one of {}, or fx:<effect-id>:<key>)", AnimParam::ALL.map(|p| p.key()).join(", "))))
}

fn param(c: &Clip, op: &Value) -> Result<TrackKey, BridgeError> {
    param_named(c, op.get("param").and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or_default())
}

/// The accepted `value` range (same limits as `set_transform` for the
/// transform params; fractions of the frame for crops; the registry's for
/// effect numbers).
fn value_range(c: &Clip, k: &TrackKey) -> (f64, f64) {
    match k {
        TrackKey::Clip(p) => match p {
            AnimParam::X | AnimParam::Y => (-20_000.0, 20_000.0),
            AnimParam::Scale => (0.01, 16.0),
            AnimParam::Rotation => (-3600.0, 3600.0),
            AnimParam::Opacity | AnimParam::CropLeft | AnimParam::CropRight | AnimParam::CropTop | AnimParam::CropBottom => (0.0, 1.0),
            AnimParam::Exposure => (-2.0, 2.0),
            AnimParam::Contrast | AnimParam::Saturation => (0.0, 2.0),
            AnimParam::Temperature => (-1.0, 1.0),
        },
        TrackKey::Fx(f) => match number_def(c, f) {
            Some(ParamKind::Number { min, max, .. }) => (min, max),
            _ => (0.0, 0.0),
        },
    }
}

/// A track's static value after `clear_animation` without `keep_ms`.
fn default_of(c: &Clip, k: &TrackKey) -> f64 {
    match k {
        TrackKey::Clip(p) => p.default_value(),
        TrackKey::Fx(f) => match number_def(c, f) {
            Some(ParamKind::Number { default, .. }) => default,
            _ => 0.0,
        },
    }
}

fn number_def(c: &Clip, f: &kadr_project::FxParam) -> Option<ParamKind> {
    Some(kadr_effects::definition(c.effect(f.effect)?)?.param(&f.key)?.kind)
}
```

then make the rest of the module work on `TrackKey`:
- `find` and `existing` take `p: Option<&TrackKey>`; inside `find`: `anim_ui::visible_key_near(c, p.cloned(), t, frame)` and `c.animation.times().into_iter().filter(|a| p.is_none_or(|q| c.animation.keys_at(*a).contains(q)))…`;
- `set_keyframe`:

```rust
fn set_keyframe(seq: &Sequence, id: ClipId, c: &Clip, op: &Value) -> Result<EditCommand, BridgeError> {
    let p = param(c, op)?;
    let value = op.get("value").and_then(Value::as_f64).filter(|v| v.is_finite()).ok_or_else(|| bad("value missing or not a number"))?;
    let (lo, hi) = value_range(c, &p);
    if !(lo..=hi).contains(&value) {
        return Err(bad(format!("{} out of range {lo}..{hi} (got {value})", p.key_string())));
    }
    let ease = ease(op)?;
    // A key within half a frame is updated, not shadowed by a near-duplicate.
    let at = match find(seq, c, Some(&p), op, "at_ms", "source_ms")? {
        Some(at) => {
            if c.animation.track(&p).iter().any(|k| k.at == at && k.value == value && ease.as_ref().is_none_or(|e| *e == k.ease)) {
                return Err(bad("no change: that keyframe already has this value and ease"));
            }
            at
        }
        None => new_time(seq, c, op, "at_ms", "source_ms")?,
    };
    Ok(EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: id, param: p, at, value, ease })
}
```

- `clear_animation`:

```rust
fn clear_animation(seq: &Sequence, id: ClipId, c: &Clip, op: &Value) -> Result<EditCommand, BridgeError> {
    let listed = match op.get("params").filter(|v| !v.is_null()) {
        None => None,
        Some(Value::Array(a)) => Some(a.iter().map(|v| param_named(c, v.as_str().unwrap_or_default())).collect::<Result<Vec<_>, _>>()?),
        Some(_) => return Err(bad("`params` must be an array of param names")),
    };
    // Only animated tracks with a static slot are reset (a track of a removed effect has none).
    let animated: Vec<TrackKey> = c.animation.keys().into_iter().filter(|k| c.static_value(k).is_some()).filter(|k| listed.as_ref().is_none_or(|l| l.contains(k))).collect();
    if animated.is_empty() {
        return Err(bad(if listed.is_some() { "clip has no animation on the listed params" } else { "clip has no animation" }));
    }
    let keep = ms_arg(op, "keep_ms")?.map(|ms| seq.frame_rate.snap(Time::from_millis(ms)));
    let statics = animated
        .into_iter()
        .map(|k| {
            let v = keep.and_then(|t| c.track_value(&k, t)).unwrap_or_else(|| default_of(c, &k));
            (k, v)
        })
        .collect();
    Ok(EditCommand::ClearAnimation { clip: id, statics })
}
```

- in `anim_op`'s match: `remove_keyframe` → `let p = param(c, op)?; let at = existing(seq, c, Some(&p), op, "at_ms", "source_ms")?; EditCommand::RemoveKeyframe { clip: id, param: p, at, keep: None }`; `set_keyframe_ease` → `let p = param(c, op)?; … existing(seq, c, Some(&p), …)?; if c.animation.track(&p).iter().any(…) …; EditCommand::SetKeyframeEase { clip: id, param: p, at, ease }`; `move_keyframes`:

```rust
            "move_keyframes" => {
                let p = op.get("param").filter(|v| !v.is_null()).map(|_| param(c, op)).transpose()?;
                let from = existing(seq, c, p.as_ref(), op, "from_ms", "from_source_ms")?;
                let to_ms = ms_arg(op, "to_ms")?.ok_or_else(|| bad("to_ms missing"))?;
                let to = SourceTime(c.source_time_at(at_timeline(seq, c, to_ms)?));
                if to == from {
                    return Err(bad("no change: the keyframes are already at that moment"));
                }
                let params: Vec<TrackKey> = p.map_or_else(|| c.animation.keys_at(from), |p| vec![p]);
                if let Some(q) = params.iter().find(|q| c.animation.track(*q).iter().any(|k| k.at == to)) {
                    return Err(bad(format!("{} already has a keyframe at {to_ms} ms; move or remove it first", q.key_string())));
                }
                EditCommand::MoveKeyframes { clip: id, moves: params.into_iter().map(|q| (q, from, to)).collect() }
            }
```

`apps/kadr-mcp/src/tools.rs` — in `EDIT_DESCRIPTION` change the `set_keyframe` line's "Params and value ranges:" sentence to start "Params and value ranges (effect numbers: param \"fx:<effect-id>:<key>\", the range from `list_effects`, e.g. strength 0..1):", and append before the `Millisecond arguments` line:

```text
- \"add_effect\": clip_id, recipe (an id from `list_effects`), params? (object: numbers; choice strings; point {\"x\": 0..1, \"y\": 0..1} as fractions of the clip's picture; moment {\"at_ms\": timeline ms inside the clip} or {\"source_ms\": int}; omitted ones get defaults: strength 0.6, the picture's centre, the clip's middle). One effect per recipe per clip: adding a recipe the clip has replaces its parameters (same effect id, its keyframes dropped); repeating it unchanged is rejected as no change. Motion effects go on video clips only
- \"set_effect\": clip_id, effect_id, params?, enabled? (merges the given params; a param with keyframes is rejected as \"<key> is animated\": use set_keyframe / clear_animation with param \"fx:<effect-id>:<key>\"; an effect from a newer Kadr (known: false in get_state) cannot be changed; no change is rejected)
- \"remove_effect\": clip_id, effect_id (its keyframes go with it)
- \"move_effect\": clip_id, effect_id, to (position in the clip's effect list, 0 = applied first; the same position is rejected as no change)
```

```markdown
Эффекты клипа (`apps/editor/src/mcp_fx.rs`, команды ассистента в
`crates/ai/src/command.rs`): `add_effect` (`clip_id`, `recipe` из
`list_effects`, `params?` — числа, строки вариантов, точка `{x, y}` в долях
кадра клипа, момент `{at_ms}` — время таймлайна внутри клипа — или
`{source_ms}`; пропущенные получают значения по умолчанию: сила 0,6, центр,
середина клипа). Один экземпляр рецепта на клип: повторный `add_effect`
заменяет параметры (тот же `effect_id`, ключевые кадры эффекта удаляются),
повтор без изменений — отказ «no change». `set_effect` (`effect_id`,
`params?`, `enabled?`) сливает переданные параметры; параметр с ключевыми
кадрами — отказ «<key> is animated» (как `set_transform`); эффект из более
новой версии Kadr (`known: false`) не меняется, только удаляется или
переносится. `remove_effect` удаляет эффект вместе с его ключевыми кадрами,
`move_effect` (`to` — позиция в списке, 0 — применяется первым) меняет
порядок. Числа эффектов анимируются обычными ключевыми операциями с
`param: "fx:<effect-id>:<key>"` и диапазоном из `list_effects`. `get_state`
показывает у клипа `effects`: `{id, recipe, version, enabled, known,
progress, params}`.
```

```rust
        assert_eq!(o.clips(), vec![id]);
```
