- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the pure part and the App methods** — above the tests in `effects_ui.rs`:

```rust
//! The «Эффекты» tab (effects spec §6.1): categories, search, cards.
//! Hovering a card shows its effect on the selected clips without touching
//! the project (`PreviewOverride`, applied to the preview's snapshot only);
//! a click applies it to them, a drag to the one clip under the pointer.
//! Pure parts are free functions, tested without a window.

use crate::app::App;
use crate::timeline_ui::{rows, Row};
use crate::{FxCardView, FxCategoryView};
use kadr_core::{ClipId, Time};
use kadr_effects::{Category, RecipeDef};
use kadr_i18n::{t, t_in, tf, Lang};
use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Sequence};
use kadr_timeline::EditCommand;
use std::time::Duration;

/// A press that moves less than this (window pixels) is a click.
const DRAG_PX: f64 = 5.0;
/// Hover this long before the preview shows the card's effect (spec §6.1).
pub const HOVER_DELAY: Duration = Duration::from_millis(150);

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct FxUi {
    /// The category chip (`""` = all).
    pub category: String,
    pub search: String,
    /// The card under the pointer and the generation of that hover: a newer
    /// hover makes an older delayed preview stale.
    pub hover: Option<(&'static str, u64)>,
    hover_gen: u64,
    /// The hovered card's effect on the selected clips — app state only.
    pub preview: Option<PreviewOverride>,
    pub drag: Option<FxDrag>,
}

pub struct FxDrag {
    recipe: &'static str,
    start: (f64, f64),
    active: bool,
}

/// An effect shown on clips by the preview only (spec §6.1).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct PreviewOverride {
    pub recipe: &'static str,
    pub effects: Vec<(ClipId, AppliedEffect)>,
}

impl PreviewOverride {
    /// The effects put on a copy of the sequence (the preview's snapshot),
    /// as applying them would.
    pub fn apply(&self, seq: &mut Sequence) {
        for (id, e) in &self.effects {
            if let Some((ti, ci)) = seq.locate_clip(*id) {
                seq.tracks[ti].clips[ci].put_effect(e.clone());
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn clips(&self) -> Vec<ClipId> {
        self.effects.iter().map(|(c, _)| *c).collect()
    }
}

/// The UI name of an effect: its recipe's name, or the raw recipe id of an
/// effect from a newer Kadr.
pub fn fx_name(e: &AppliedEffect) -> String {
    kadr_effects::recipe(&e.recipe).map_or_else(|| e.recipe.clone(), |d| t(&d.name_key()))
}

/// The categories that have recipes, in tab order (refinement 8).
pub fn categories() -> Vec<Category> {
    Category::ALL.into_iter().filter(|c| kadr_effects::registry().iter().any(|d| d.category == *c)).collect()
}

/// Recipes listed for `category` (`""` = all) whose Russian or English name
/// contains `query`, any case (spec §6.1: search by name, RU/EN).
pub fn visible_recipes(category: &str, query: &str) -> Vec<&'static RecipeDef> {
    let q = query.trim().to_lowercase();
    kadr_effects::registry()
        .iter()
        .filter(|d| category.is_empty() || d.category.key() == category)
        .filter(|d| q.is_empty() || Lang::ALL.iter().any(|l| t_in(*l, &d.name_key()).to_lowercase().contains(&q)))
        .collect()
}

/// The selected clips an effect of `def` goes on: those it fits, on unlocked tracks.
pub fn targets(seq: &Sequence, selection: &[ClipId], def: &RecipeDef) -> Vec<ClipId> {
    selection
        .iter()
        .copied()
        .filter(|id| seq.locate_clip(*id).is_some_and(|(ti, _)| !seq.tracks[ti].locked && kadr_effects::fits(def.target, seq.tracks[ti].kind)))
        .collect()
}

/// Why a card is inactive (an i18n key): a selection none of which it fits.
pub fn card_hint(seq: &Sequence, selection: &[ClipId], def: &RecipeDef) -> Option<&'static str> {
    (!selection.is_empty() && targets(seq, selection, def).is_empty()).then_some("fx.hint.unfit")
}

/// What a card dropped on the lanes at time `t`, lane y `y` hits.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub enum DropHit {
    Clip(ClipId),
    Unfit,
    Locked,
    Nothing,
}

/// Only a clip really under the pointer on a real row (review focus 3: no
/// fallback to the top row as the media drop has), that the effect fits.
pub fn drop_hit(seq: &Sequence, rows: &[Row], t: Time, y: f64, def: &RecipeDef) -> DropHit {
    let Some(r) = rows.iter().find(|r| y >= r.y && y < r.y + r.h) else { return DropHit::Nothing };
    let track = &seq.tracks[r.track];
    let Some(c) = track.clip_at(t) else { return DropHit::Nothing };
    if !kadr_effects::fits(def.target, track.kind) {
        DropHit::Unfit
    } else if track.locked {
        DropHit::Locked
    } else {
        DropHit::Clip(c.id)
    }
}

/// The edit applying `def` at «Обычно» to `clips` (spec §6.1: one undo entry
/// «Эффект: …»), applied at timeline time `at`; only clips it changes —
/// `None` when it changes none.
pub fn apply_batch(seq: &Sequence, def: &RecipeDef, clips: &[ClipId], at: Time) -> Option<EditCommand> {
    let commands: Vec<EditCommand> = clips
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|id| {
            let c = seq.clip(*id)?;
            let effect = kadr_effects::fresh(def, c, at);
            c.clone().put_effect(effect.clone()).then_some(EditCommand::AddEffect { clip: *id, effect })
        })
        .collect();
    (!commands.is_empty()).then(|| EditCommand::Batch { label: tf("cmd.effect_named", &[("name", &t(&def.name_key()))]), commands })
}

impl App {
    /// The left panel switched tabs (0 media, 1 effects).
    pub fn fx_tab_changed(&mut self, tab: i32) {
        self.fx_clear_hover();
        if tab == 1 {
            self.refresh_effects();
        }
    }

    /// A project was opened or created: nothing hovered, dragged or picked.
    pub fn fx_reset(&mut self) {
        self.fx.hover = None;
        self.fx.preview = None;
        self.fx.drag = None;
    }

    /// Chips, cards and the hint of the tab — only while it shows. Rows keep
    /// their elements (`sync_rows_by_key`): a press in flight survives.
    pub fn refresh_effects(&mut self) {
        let ui = self.ui();
        if ui.get_left_tab() != 1 {
            return;
        }
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let mut chips = vec![FxCategoryView { key: "".into(), label: t("fx.cat.all").into() }];
        chips.extend(categories().into_iter().map(|c| FxCategoryView { key: c.key().into(), label: t(&c.label_key()).into() }));
        crate::util::sync_rows(ui.get_fx_categories(), chips, |m| ui.set_fx_categories(m));
        ui.set_fx_category(self.fx.category.clone().into());
        let sel = &self.tl.selection;
        let cards: Vec<FxCardView> = visible_recipes(&self.fx.category, &self.fx.search)
            .into_iter()
            .map(|d| {
                let hint = card_hint(seq, sel, d);
                FxCardView {
                    id: d.id.into(),
                    name: t(&d.name_key()).into(),
                    category: d.category.key().into(),
                    thumb: Default::default(),
                    has_thumb: false,
                    enabled: hint.is_none(),
                    hint: hint.map(t).unwrap_or_default().into(),
                }
            })
            .collect();
        crate::util::sync_rows_by_key(ui.get_fx_cards(), cards, |c| c.id.clone(), |m| ui.set_fx_cards(m));
        ui.set_fx_hint(if sel.is_empty() { t("fx.hint.select_clip") } else { String::new() }.into());
        // A hover preview follows the selection it was made for, or ends.
        let stale = self.fx.preview.as_ref().is_some_and(|o| kadr_effects::recipe(o.recipe).is_none_or(|d| targets(seq, sel, d) != o.clips()));
        if stale {
            self.fx_clear_preview();
        }
    }

    pub fn fx_category(&mut self, key: &str) {
        self.fx.category = key.to_string();
        self.refresh_effects();
    }

    pub fn fx_search(&mut self, text: &str) {
        self.fx.search = text.to_string();
        self.refresh_effects();
    }

    /// The pointer entered or left a card. Entering starts the 150 ms delay;
    /// leaving restores the preview at once.
    pub fn fx_card_hover(&mut self, id: &str, on: bool) {
        let Some(def) = kadr_effects::recipe(id) else { return };
        if on {
            self.fx.hover_gen += 1;
            let generation = self.fx.hover_gen;
            self.fx.hover = Some((def.id, generation));
            slint::Timer::single_shot(HOVER_DELAY, move || {
                crate::app::with_app(|app| app.fx_hover_fire(generation));
            });
        } else if self.fx.hover.is_some_and(|(r, _)| r == def.id) {
            self.fx_clear_hover();
        }
    }

    /// The delayed hover: the card's effect at «Обычно» on the selected
    /// clips, in the frame under the playhead (spec §6.1).
    fn fx_hover_fire(&mut self, generation: u64) {
        let Some((recipe, g)) = self.fx.hover else { return };
        if g != generation || self.fx.drag.as_ref().is_some_and(|d| d.active) {
            return;
        }
        let Some(def) = kadr_effects::recipe(recipe) else { return };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let effects: Vec<(ClipId, AppliedEffect)> =
            targets(seq, &self.tl.selection, def).into_iter().filter_map(|id| Some((id, kadr_effects::fresh(def, seq.clip(id)?, self.playhead)))).collect();
        if effects.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        self.fx.preview = Some(PreviewOverride { recipe: def.id, effects });
        self.preview.invalidate();
        self.request_frame();
    }

    /// Nothing hovered any more: the preview shows the project again.
    pub fn fx_clear_hover(&mut self) {
        self.fx.hover = None;
        self.fx_clear_preview();
    }

    fn fx_clear_preview(&mut self) {
        if self.fx.preview.take().is_some() {
            self.preview.invalidate();
            self.request_frame();
        }
    }

    pub fn fx_card_pressed(&mut self, id: &str, x: f32, y: f32) {
        let Some(def) = kadr_effects::recipe(id) else { return };
        self.fx.drag = Some(FxDrag { recipe: def.id, start: (x as f64, y as f64), active: false });
    }

    /// What a drop at window point (`wx`, `wy`) hits.
    fn fx_drop_at(&self, wx: f64, wy: f64, def: &RecipeDef) -> DropHit {
        let ui = self.ui();
        let (x, y) = (wx - ui.get_lanes_abs_x() as f64, wy - ui.get_lanes_abs_y() as f64);
        if x < 0.0 || y < 0.0 || x > ui.get_lanes_width() as f64 || y > ui.get_lanes_height() as f64 {
            return DropHit::Nothing;
        }
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        drop_hit(seq, &rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y), self.x_to_time(x), y, def)
    }

    /// A card drag: past `DRAG_PX` the label follows the pointer and the
    /// clip it would land on is outlined (the timeline's ghost rectangle).
    pub fn fx_card_moved(&mut self, _id: &str, x: f32, y: f32) {
        let (x, y) = (x as f64, y as f64);
        let Some(d) = self.fx.drag.as_mut() else { return };
        if !d.active && ((x - d.start.0).abs() > DRAG_PX || (y - d.start.1).abs() > DRAG_PX) {
            d.active = true;
        }
        if !d.active {
            return;
        }
        let Some(def) = kadr_effects::recipe(d.recipe) else { return };
        self.fx_clear_preview();
        let name = t(&def.name_key());
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let (label, ghost) = match self.fx_drop_at(x, y, def) {
            DropHit::Clip(id) => match (seq.clip(id), seq.locate_clip(id)) {
                (Some(c), Some((ti, _))) => {
                    let row = rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y).into_iter().find(|r| r.track == ti);
                    let x0 = (c.timeline_in.as_secs_f64() - self.tl.scroll) * self.tl.pps;
                    let w = c.duration().as_secs_f64() * self.tl.pps;
                    (tf("fx.drop.on", &[("name", &name), ("clip", &c.name)]), row.map(|r| (x0, r.y, w, r.h)))
                }
                _ => (tf("fx.drag_hint", &[("name", &name)]), None),
            },
            DropHit::Unfit => (t("fx.hint.unfit"), None),
            DropHit::Locked => (t("fx.drop.locked"), None),
            DropHit::Nothing => (tf("fx.drag_hint", &[("name", &name)]), None),
        };
        let ui = self.ui();
        ui.set_drag_active(true);
        ui.set_drag_x(x as f32);
        ui.set_drag_y(y as f32);
        ui.set_drag_label(label.into());
        self.tl.ghost = ghost;
        self.refresh_timeline();
    }

    /// The end of a card press: a drag applies to the clip under the pointer
    /// (and selects it); a click applies to the selected clips.
    pub fn fx_card_released(&mut self, _id: &str, x: f32, y: f32) {
        let ui = self.ui();
        ui.set_drag_active(false);
        self.tl.ghost = None;
        let Some(d) = self.fx.drag.take() else { return };
        let Some(def) = kadr_effects::recipe(d.recipe) else { return };
        if d.active {
            match self.fx_drop_at(x as f64, y as f64, def) {
                DropHit::Clip(id) => {
                    let at = self.x_to_time(x as f64 - ui.get_lanes_abs_x() as f64);
                    let cmd = apply_batch(self.project.sequence(), def, &[id], at);
                    if cmd.is_some_and(|cmd| self.execute(cmd)) {
                        self.tl.selection = vec![id];
                        self.refresh_inspector();
                    }
                }
                DropHit::Unfit => self.toast_warn(t("fx.hint.unfit")),
                DropHit::Locked => self.toast_warn(t("fx.drop.locked")),
                DropHit::Nothing => {}
            }
            self.refresh_timeline();
            return;
        }
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        if self.tl.selection.is_empty() {
            return self.toast(t("fx.hint.select_clip"));
        }
        let clips = targets(seq, &self.tl.selection, def);
        if clips.is_empty() {
            return self.toast_warn(t("fx.hint.unfit"));
        }
        if let Some(cmd) = apply_batch(seq, def, &clips, self.playhead) {
            self.execute(cmd);
        }
    }
}
```

`apps/editor/src/preview.rs` — `preview_scenes` becomes (the snapshot carries the hover override; nothing else changes):

```rust
    /// The snapshot the preview shows (taken now if the project changed),
    /// with the hovered effect card's override applied to its copy only.
    pub fn preview_scenes(&mut self) -> Arc<ProjectScenes> {
        let bypass = self.ui().get_bypass();
        let out = self.preview.output(&self.project);
        let mcp = self.preview.mcp_resolver.clone();
        let snapshot = |project: &Project, fx: &crate::effects_ui::FxUi| match &fx.preview {
            Some(o) => ProjectScenes::new_with(project, bypass, |seq| o.apply(seq)),
            None => ProjectScenes::new(project, bypass),
        };
        match &mut self.preview.cpu {
            Some(cpu) => {
                cpu.set_output(out);
                if cpu.offline_media_returned() {
                    cpu.dirty = true;
                }
                match &cpu.scenes {
                    Some(s) if !cpu.dirty && s.bypass() == bypass => s.clone(),
                    _ => {
                        let s = Arc::new(snapshot(&self.project, &self.fx));
                        cpu.set_scenes(s.clone(), mcp.as_deref());
                        s
                    }
                }
            }
            None => Arc::new(snapshot(&self.project, &self.fx)),
        }
    }
```

(Setting or clearing the override calls `self.preview.invalidate()`, which marks `cpu.dirty`, so the next call rebuilds the snapshot.) `apps/editor/src/persistence.rs` `set_project`: after `self.live_edit.forget();` add `self.fx_reset();`. `apps/editor/src/inspector.rs` `refresh_inspector`: after `ui.set_inspector(d);` add `self.refresh_effects();` (a selection change, an edit, undo and a language change all pass here).

```slint
// A panel header whose title is a row of tabs (the left panel: Media ·
// Effects). The tab list is a fixed model, so the buttons never rebuild.
export component PanelTabs inherits Rectangle {
    in property <[string]> tabs;
    in property <int> current;
    callback selected(int);
    height: Theme.panel-header;
    background: Theme.bg1;
    Rectangle { y: parent.height - 1px; height: 1px; background: Theme.border; }
    HorizontalLayout {
        padding-left: 6px;
        padding-right: 6px;
        spacing: 2px;
        for tab[i] in root.tabs: Rectangle {
            width: tt.preferred-width + 16px;
            tt := Text {
                text: tab;
                vertical-alignment: center;
                font-size: Theme.font-sm;
                font-weight: 600;
                letter-spacing: 0.6px;
                color: i == root.current || tta.has-hover ? Theme.text : Theme.text-faint;
            }
            Rectangle { y: parent.height - 2px; height: 2px; background: i == root.current ? Theme.accent : transparent; }
            tta := TouchArea {
                mouse-cursor: pointer;
                clicked => {
                    if (i != root.current) { root.selected(i); }
                }
            }
        }
        Rectangle { horizontal-stretch: 1; }
        @children
    }
}
```

`apps/editor/ui/effects.slint` (new):

```slint
// The «Эффекты» tab (effects spec §6.1): filter chips, search, a grid of
// cards. Rust builds the rows (stable models, keyed by recipe id) and does
// hover delays, click and drag; Slint only draws and reports the pointer.

import { Theme, Icons } from "theme.slint";
import { Tr } from "i18n.slint";
import { Icon, PanelTabs, EmptyState, Tooltip } from "widgets.slint";
import { ScrollView, LineEdit } from "std-widgets.slint";

export struct FxCategoryView {
    key: string,
    label: string,
}

export struct FxCardView {
    id: string,
    name: string,
    category: string,
    thumb: image,
    has-thumb: bool,
    enabled: bool,
    hint: string,
}

component FxCard inherits Rectangle {
    in property <FxCardView> c;
    callback hover(bool);
    callback pressed(length, length);
    callback moved(length, length);
    callback released(length, length);
    border-radius: Theme.radius;
    background: ta.has-hover && root.c.enabled ? Theme.bg3 : Theme.bg2;
    border-width: 1px;
    border-color: ta.has-hover && root.c.enabled ? Theme.accent : Theme.border;
    opacity: root.c.enabled ? 1 : 0.45;
    animate background, border-color { duration: 90ms; }
    VerticalLayout {
        padding: 4px;
        spacing: 4px;
        Rectangle {
            height: (root.width - 8px) * 9 / 16;
            border-radius: 4px;
            background: #000000;
            clip: true;
            if root.c.has-thumb: Image { width: parent.width; height: parent.height; source: root.c.thumb; image-fit: cover; }
            if !root.c.has-thumb: Icon { icon: root.c.category == "motion" ? Icons.motion : Icons.sparkle; width: 22px; height: 22px; color: Theme.text-faint; }
        }
        Text { text: root.c.name; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text; overflow: elide; horizontal-alignment: center; }
    }
    ta := TouchArea {
        mouse-cursor: !root.c.enabled ? not-allowed : self.pressed ? grabbing : pointer;
        changed has-hover => { root.hover(self.has-hover); }
        pointer-event(e) => {
            if (e.button == PointerEventButton.left && e.kind == PointerEventKind.down) { root.pressed(root.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, root.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y); }
            if (e.button == PointerEventButton.left && e.kind == PointerEventKind.up) { root.released(root.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, root.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y); }
        }
        moved => {
            if (self.pressed) { root.moved(root.absolute-position.x + self.mouse-x, root.absolute-position.y + self.mouse-y); }
        }
    }
    if ta.has-hover && root.c.hint != "": Tooltip {
        text: root.c.hint;
        x: (root.width - self.width) / 2;
        y: root.height + 4px;
    }
}

export component EffectsPanel inherits Rectangle {
    in property <[FxCategoryView]> categories;
    in property <string> category;
    in property <[FxCardView]> cards;
    // "Select a clip to try effects on it" (empty with a selection).
    in property <string> hint;
    in-out property <string> search;
    callback tab-selected(int);
    callback category-selected(string);
    callback search-changed(string);
    callback card-hover(string, bool);
    callback card-pressed(string, length, length);
    callback card-moved(string, length, length);
    callback card-released(string, length, length);
    background: Theme.bg1;

    VerticalLayout {
        PanelTabs { tabs: [Tr.t("media.title"), Tr.t("fx.title")]; current: 1; selected(i) => { root.tab-selected(i); } }
        VerticalLayout {
            padding: 8px;
            spacing: 6px;
            LineEdit {
                placeholder-text: Tr.t("fx.search");
                text <=> root.search;
                edited(t) => { root.search-changed(t); }
            }
            HorizontalLayout {
                spacing: 4px;
                alignment: start;
                for cat in root.categories: Rectangle {
                    width: ct.preferred-width + 18px;
                    height: 24px;
                    border-radius: 12px;
                    background: root.category == cat.key ? Theme.bg4 : cta.has-hover ? Theme.bg3 : Theme.bg2;
                    border-width: 1px;
                    border-color: root.category == cat.key ? Theme.border-strong : Theme.border;
                    ct := Text { text: cat.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: root.category == cat.key ? Theme.text : Theme.text-dim; }
                    cta := TouchArea { mouse-cursor: pointer; clicked => { root.category-selected(cat.key); } }
                }
            }
            if root.hint != "": Text { text: root.hint; font-size: Theme.font-xs; color: Theme.text-faint; wrap: word-wrap; }
        }
        if root.cards.length == 0: EmptyState { icon: Icons.sparkle; title: Tr.t("fx.no_match"); subtitle: ""; }
        if root.cards.length > 0: sv := ScrollView {
            vertical-stretch: 1;
            // At least two columns of 16:9 cards (thumbnails are rendered at 160 × 90).
            property <length> gap: 8px;
            property <int> cols: max(2, floor((sv.visible-width - 8px) / 168px));
            property <length> card-w: (sv.visible-width - 16px - (self.cols - 1) * self.gap) / self.cols;
            property <length> card-h: self.card-w * 9 / 16 + 30px;
            viewport-height: ceil(root.cards.length / self.cols) * (self.card-h + self.gap) + 8px;
            for card[i] in root.cards: FxCard {
                x: 8px + mod(i, sv.cols) * (sv.card-w + sv.gap);
                y: 4px + floor(i / sv.cols) * (sv.card-h + sv.gap);
                width: sv.card-w;
                height: sv.card-h;
                c: card;
                hover(on) => { root.card-hover(card.id, on); }
                pressed(x, y) => { root.card-pressed(card.id, x, y); }
                moved(x, y) => { root.card-moved(card.id, x, y); }
                released(x, y) => { root.card-released(card.id, x, y); }
            }
        }
    }
}
```

```slint
                    card-moved(id, x, y) => { root.fx-card-moved(id, x, y); }
                    card-released(id, x, y) => { root.fx-card-released(id, x, y); root.focus-editor(); }
                }
```

### Task 10: Live card thumbnails and the hover loop

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/editor/src/effects_ui.rs` (`FxUi.thumbs`, cards show thumbnails, loop on hover, cancel on leaving)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `kadr_effects::{fresh, ParamKind, RecipeDef}` (Task 4); `ProjectScenes`, `Resolver::prepare`, `CpuRenderer`; `App::preview.mcp_resolver()` (its own export-mode resolver, also used by MCP `get_frame`); `effects_ui::visible_recipes` (Task 9); `crate::app::post`.
- Produces:
  - `pub struct ThumbKey { pub clip: Option<ClipId>, pub frame: Time, pub recipe: &'static str, pub version: u16, pub phase: u8 }` (`Copy, Eq, Hash`; spec cache key: clip, frame, recipe, version), `ThumbKey::new(base: &ThumbBase, d: &RecipeDef, phase: u8)`, `phase_f(&self) -> f64`; `pub struct ThumbBase { pub clip: Option<ClipId>, pub at: Time, pub frame: Time }`, `thumb_base(project: &Project, inspected: Option<ClipId>, playhead: Time) -> ThumbBase`.
  - `demo_effect(def, clip, at: Time, phase: f64) -> AppliedEffect`, `thumb_project(project, clip, effect) -> Project`, `sample_frame() -> Arc<CpuFrame>`, `sample_project(def, phase) -> Project`, `render(p: &Project, at: Time, resolver: Option<&Resolver>, sample: Option<&Arc<CpuFrame>>) -> Option<Vec<u8>>`.
  - `pub struct Thumbs` (`Default`): `get`, `plan(keys) -> Option<HashSet<ThumbKey>>`, `start(keys) -> CancelToken`, `insert`, `cancel`, `sample()`, `pub loop_frame: u8`, `pub timer: Option<slint::Timer>`.
  - `App::{fx_thumb(&self, d, base) -> Option<slint::Image>, fx_thumb_ready(&mut self, ThumbKey, Vec<u8>)}`; constants `THUMB_W = 160`, `THUMB_H = 90`, `LOOP_FRAMES = 12`, `STILL = u8::MAX`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `apps/editor/src/fx_thumbs.rs` with only its tests (and `mod fx_thumbs;` in `main.rs`):

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use kadr_core::AssetId;

    /// Timeline 10..20 s showing source 30..40 s.
    fn clip() -> Clip {
        Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "c", TimeRange::new(Time::from_secs(30), Time::from_secs(40)), Time::from_secs(10))
    }

    #[test]
    fn demo_effects_hold_a_phase_and_place_the_moment_around_it() {
        let def = kadr_effects::recipe("punch_in").unwrap();
        let e = demo_effect(def, &clip(), Time::from_secs(12), 0.0);
        assert_eq!(e.progress, (0.0, 0.0), "a constant progress: the frame shows the phase");
        assert_eq!(e.params["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(32) + Time::from_millis(100))), "phase 0: the punch is just ahead");
        let done = demo_effect(def, &clip(), Time::from_secs(12), 1.0);
        assert_eq!(done.params["moment"], ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(Time::from_secs(32) - Time::from_millis(200))), "phase 1: well past it");
        assert_eq!(done.params["strength"], ParamValue::Number(0.6), "«Обычно»");
        assert_eq!(ThumbKey { clip: None, frame: Time::ZERO, recipe: "x", version: 1, phase: STILL }.phase_f(), 0.85);
        assert_eq!(ThumbKey { clip: None, frame: Time::ZERO, recipe: "x", version: 1, phase: LOOP_FRAMES - 1 }.phase_f(), 1.0);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_thumbnail_project_shows_the_clip_at_full_frame_with_only_that_effect() {
        let mut p = Project::new("t");
        let mut c = clip();
        c.transform.scale = 0.3;
        c.video_fade.fade_in = Time::from_secs(1);
        c.color.saturation = 0.5;
        c.effects.push(AppliedEffect::new("handheld", 1, Default::default()));
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c.clone());
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_zoom").unwrap(), &c, Time::from_secs(12));
        let t = thumb_project(&p, &c, e);
        let tc = &t.sequence().tracks[0].clips[0];
        assert_eq!(t.sequence().tracks.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!((tc.transform.scale, tc.video_fade.fade_in, tc.color.saturation), (1.0, Time::ZERO, 0.5), "full frame, no fade, its own colour");
        assert_eq!(tc.effects.iter().map(|e| e.recipe.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>(), ["smooth_zoom"]);
        assert_eq!((t.sequence().width, t.sequence().height), (p.sequence().width, p.sequence().height));
    }

    /// No decoder needed: the sample picture renders, and a zoom changes it.
    #[test]
    fn the_sample_renders_and_a_zoom_changes_it() {
        let sample = sample_frame();
        let def = kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_zoom").unwrap();
        let start = render(&sample_project(def, 0.0), SAMPLE_AT, None, Some(&sample)).unwrap();
        let end = render(&sample_project(def, 1.0), SAMPLE_AT, None, Some(&sample)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(start.len(), (THUMB_W * THUMB_H * 4) as usize);
        assert!(start.chunks(4).any(|p| p[0] > 40), "not a black frame");
        assert_ne!(start, end, "phase 1 is zoomed in");
        assert!(render(&sample_project(def, 0.0), SAMPLE_AT, None, None).is_none(), "no decoder and no sample: nothing");
    }

    #[test]
    fn thumbnails_follow_the_inspected_video_clip_or_fall_back_to_the_sample() {
        let mut p = Project::new("t");
        let c = clip();
        let id = c.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
        let a = Clip::new(AssetId::new(), "a", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(5)), Time::ZERO);
        let aid = a.id;
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[1].clips.push(a);
        assert_eq!(thumb_base(&p, Some(id), Time::from_secs(12)), ThumbBase { clip: Some(id), at: Time::from_secs(12), frame: Time::from_secs(32) });
        assert_eq!(thumb_base(&p, Some(id), Time::from_secs(3)).at, Time::from_secs(15), "playhead off the clip: its middle");
        assert_eq!(thumb_base(&p, Some(aid), Time::ZERO).clip, None, "a sound clip has no picture: the sample");
        assert_eq!(thumb_base(&p, None, Time::ZERO), ThumbBase { clip: None, at: SAMPLE_AT, frame: SAMPLE_AT });
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_batch_restarts_only_for_keys_the_cache_lacks() {
        let key = |phase| ThumbKey { clip: None, frame: SAMPLE_AT, recipe: "smooth_zoom", version: 1, phase };
        let mut th = Thumbs::default();
        let need = th.plan([key(STILL), key(0)]).expect("nothing cached");
        assert_eq!(need.len(), 2);
        let token = th.start(need);
        assert!(th.plan([key(STILL), key(0)]).is_none(), "the running batch covers them");
        th.insert(key(STILL), slint::Image::default());
        assert!(th.get(&key(STILL)).is_some());
        assert_eq!(th.plan([key(STILL), key(0), key(1)]).map(|n| n.len()), Some(2), "a new key restarts the batch with every missing one");
        th.cancel();
        assert!(token.is_cancelled(), "leaving the tab stops the work");
        assert_eq!(th.plan([key(0)]).map(|n| n.len()), Some(1), "cancelled keys are asked for again");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — above the tests in `fx_thumbs.rs`:

```rust
//! Card thumbnails of the «Эффекты» tab (effects spec §6.1): the inspected
//! clip's frame at the playhead with the card's effect at «Обычно»,
//! rendered in the background at 160 × 90 and cached by (clip, source frame,
//! recipe, version, phase); a new batch cancels the previous one, leaving
//! the tab cancels it; a hovered card plays a short loop of its motion.
//! Without a clip a built-in sample picture stands in (no decoding).

use crate::app::App;
use crate::effects_ui::visible_recipes;
use kadr_core::color::AlphaMode;
use kadr_core::{CancelToken, ClipId, ColorInfo, CpuFrame, FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, VideoInfo};
use kadr_effects::{ParamKind, RecipeDef};
use kadr_playback::{Mode, Resolver, SceneSource};
use kadr_project::{Animation, AppliedEffect, Clip, EdgeFades, MediaAsset, ParamValue, Project, SourceTime, Track, TrackKind, Transform};
use kadr_render::{CpuRenderer, CpuTarget, LayerInput, PreparedFrame, RenderInputs, RenderTarget, Renderer};
use kadr_scene::{LayerContent, OutputSpec, RenderQuality, SizeU};
use std::collections::{HashMap, HashSet};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::Duration;

pub const THUMB_W: u32 = 160;
pub const THUMB_H: u32 = 90;
/// Frames of a hovered card's loop (~1 s at 12 fps).
pub const LOOP_FRAMES: u8 = 12;
pub const LOOP_TICK: Duration = Duration::from_millis(83);
/// The phase byte of a still thumbnail.
pub const STILL: u8 = u8::MAX;
/// A still shows the motion well under way.
const STILL_PHASE: f64 = 0.85;
const MAX_IMAGES: usize = 300;
const SAMPLE_W: u32 = 320;
const SAMPLE_H: u32 = 180;
/// The sample clip's frame the thumbnails show.
pub const SAMPLE_AT: Time = Time::from_secs(5);

/// What the thumbnails show now: the inspected video clip at the playhead
/// (its middle when the playhead is off it), or the sample.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct ThumbBase {
    pub clip: Option<ClipId>,
    /// Timeline time rendered.
    pub at: Time,
    /// The source frame there (the cache key's "кадр").
    pub frame: Time,
}

pub fn thumb_base(project: &Project, inspected: Option<ClipId>, playhead: Time) -> ThumbBase {
    let seq = project.sequence();
    let video = inspected.and_then(|id| seq.locate_clip(id)).filter(|(ti, _)| seq.tracks[*ti].kind == TrackKind::Video);
    match video {
        Some((ti, ci)) => {
            let c = &seq.tracks[ti].clips[ci];
            let at = if c.timeline_in <= playhead && playhead < c.timeline_out { playhead } else { c.timeline_in + Time(c.duration().flicks() / 2) };
            ThumbBase { clip: Some(c.id), at, frame: c.source_time_at(at) }
        }
        None => ThumbBase { clip: None, at: SAMPLE_AT, frame: SAMPLE_AT },
    }
}

#[derive(Clone, Copy, Debug, PartialEq, Eq, Hash)]
pub struct ThumbKey {
    pub clip: Option<ClipId>,
    pub frame: Time,
    pub recipe: &'static str,
    pub version: u16,
    /// `STILL`, or a loop frame `0..LOOP_FRAMES`.
    pub phase: u8,
}

impl ThumbKey {
    pub fn new(base: &ThumbBase, d: &RecipeDef, phase: u8) -> ThumbKey {
        ThumbKey { clip: base.clip, frame: base.frame, recipe: d.id, version: d.version, phase }
    }

    /// The motion phase 0..1 this thumbnail shows.
    pub fn phase_f(&self) -> f64 {
        if self.phase == STILL { STILL_PHASE } else { self.phase as f64 / (LOOP_FRAMES - 1) as f64 }
    }
}

/// The effect as a thumbnail shows it at `phase`: «Обычно», the progress
/// held at `phase`, and every «Момент» placed so its motion is `phase` of
/// the way through (just ahead at 0, done from ~0.75).
pub fn demo_effect(def: &RecipeDef, clip: &Clip, at: Time, phase: f64) -> AppliedEffect {
    let mut e = kadr_effects::fresh(def, clip, at);
    e.progress = (phase, phase);
    let s = clip.source_time_at(at);
    for d in def.params.iter().filter(|d| matches!(d.kind, ParamKind::Moment)) {
        let lead = Time::from_secs_f64((phase * 0.3 - 0.1) * clip.speed());
        e.params.insert(d.key.to_string(), ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(s - lead)));
    }
    e
}

/// A one-clip project of `clip`'s own picture at full frame (transform,
/// animation, fades and other effects dropped, its colour kept) with only `effect`.
pub fn thumb_project(project: &Project, clip: &Clip, effect: AppliedEffect) -> Project {
    let mut c = clip.clone();
    c.transform = Transform::default();
    c.animation = Animation::default();
    c.video_fade = EdgeFades::default();
    c.enabled = true;
    c.effects = vec![effect];
    let src = project.sequence();
    let mut p = Project::new("thumb");
    p.assets = project.asset(c.asset).cloned().into_iter().collect();
    let seq = p.sequence_mut();
    (seq.width, seq.height, seq.frame_rate) = (src.width, src.height, src.frame_rate);
    seq.tracks = vec![Track::new(TrackKind::Video, "V1")];
    seq.tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    p
}

/// The picture cards show without a clip: a dusk sky, a sun and two hills.
pub fn sample_frame() -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
    let mut px = vec![0u8; (SAMPLE_W * SAMPLE_H * 4) as usize];
    for y in 0..SAMPLE_H {
        for x in 0..SAMPLE_W {
            let (u, v) = (x as f32 / SAMPLE_W as f32, y as f32 / SAMPLE_H as f32);
            let mut c = [0.16 + 0.55 * v, 0.20 + 0.30 * v, 0.42 - 0.12 * v];
            if (u - 0.68).powi(2) + ((v - 0.42) * 0.5625).powi(2) < 0.006 {
                c = [1.0, 0.86, 0.55];
            }
            if v > 0.80 - 0.06 * (u * 5.0 + 1.3).cos() {
                c = [0.10, 0.16, 0.12];
            } else if v > 0.70 - 0.08 * (u * 9.0).sin() {
                c = [0.22, 0.30, 0.26];
            }
            let i = ((y * SAMPLE_W + x) * 4) as usize;
            for k in 0..3 {
                px[i + k] = (c[k].clamp(0.0, 1.0) * 255.0) as u8;
            }
            px[i + 3] = 255;
        }
    }
    Arc::new(CpuFrame::from_rgba8(SAMPLE_W, SAMPLE_H, ColorInfo { alpha: AlphaMode::Opaque, ..ColorInfo::WORKING_SDR }, px))
}

/// The sample as a 10 s clip on a 16:9 sequence, with `def` at `phase`.
pub fn sample_project(def: &RecipeDef, phase: f64) -> Project {
    let video = VideoInfo { width: SAMPLE_W, height: SAMPLE_H, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "sample".into(), pixel_format: "rgba".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    let a = MediaAsset::new("kadr-sample", MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(10), container: "sample".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None });
    let mut c = Clip::new(a.id, "sample", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(10)), Time::ZERO);
    c.effects = vec![demo_effect(def, &c, SAMPLE_AT, phase)];
    let mut p = Project::new("sample");
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    p.assets.push(a);
    p
}

/// One thumbnail: `p` at `at` into 160 × 90 premultiplied RGBA. With
/// `sample` every media layer shows it; otherwise `resolver` decodes and
/// waits (export mode). `None` without a way to get the picture.
pub fn render(p: &Project, at: Time, resolver: Option<&Resolver>, sample: Option<&Arc<CpuFrame>>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    let out = OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(THUMB_W, THUMB_H), RenderQuality::PreviewHigh);
    let scenes = crate::scene_source::ProjectScenes::new(p, false);
    let scene = scenes.scene_at(at, &out);
    let inputs = match sample {
        Some(f) => RenderInputs { layers: scene.layers.iter().map(|l| match l.content { LayerContent::Media { .. } => LayerInput::Cpu(f.clone()), _ => LayerInput::None }).collect() },
        None => {
            let mut perf = kadr_core::perf::FramePerf::default();
            resolver?.prepare(&scene, &scenes, Mode::Export, &mut perf)
        }
    };
    let mut px = vec![0u8; (THUMB_W * THUMB_H * 4) as usize];
    let mut target = RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(THUMB_W, THUMB_H, &mut px));
    CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &scene, inputs: &inputs }, &mut target).ok()?;
    Some(px)
}

/// Rendered thumbnails, the batch in flight and the hovered card's loop.
#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Thumbs {
    images: HashMap<ThumbKey, slint::Image>,
    pending: HashSet<ThumbKey>,
    cancel: Option<CancelToken>,
    sample: Option<Arc<CpuFrame>>,
    /// The hovered card's loop frame.
    pub loop_frame: u8,
    /// Advances `loop_frame` while a card is hovered (dropping it stops it).
    pub timer: Option<slint::Timer>,
}

impl Thumbs {
    pub fn get(&self, k: &ThumbKey) -> Option<slint::Image> {
        self.images.get(k).cloned()
    }

    /// The keys a new batch must render — every wanted key not cached — or
    /// `None` when the batch in flight already covers them.
    pub fn plan(&self, keys: impl IntoIterator<Item = ThumbKey>) -> Option<HashSet<ThumbKey>> {
        let need: HashSet<ThumbKey> = keys.into_iter().filter(|k| !self.images.contains_key(k)).collect();
        (!need.is_empty() && !need.is_subset(&self.pending)).then_some(need)
    }

    /// A new batch for `keys`; the previous one is cancelled.
    pub fn start(&mut self, keys: HashSet<ThumbKey>) -> CancelToken {
        self.cancel();
        let token = CancelToken::new();
        self.cancel = Some(token.clone());
        self.pending = keys;
        token
    }

    pub fn insert(&mut self, k: ThumbKey, img: slint::Image) {
        self.pending.remove(&k);
        if self.images.len() >= MAX_IMAGES {
            self.images.clear();
        }
        self.images.insert(k, img);
    }

    /// Stops the batch in flight (the tab was left, the frame changed).
    pub fn cancel(&mut self) {
        if let Some(t) = self.cancel.take() {
            t.cancel();
        }
        self.pending.clear();
    }

    pub fn sample(&mut self) -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
        self.sample.get_or_insert_with(sample_frame).clone()
    }
}

impl App {
    /// What a card shows for `d`: its loop frame while hovered (once
    /// rendered), else its still.
    pub fn fx_thumb(&self, d: &RecipeDef, base: &ThumbBase) -> Option<slint::Image> {
        let looping = self.fx.hover.is_some_and(|(r, _)| r == d.id);
        looping
            .then(|| self.fx.thumbs.get(&ThumbKey::new(base, d, self.fx.thumbs.loop_frame)))
            .flatten()
            .or_else(|| self.fx.thumbs.get(&ThumbKey::new(base, d, STILL)))
    }

    /// Starts rendering what the tab needs and lacks: stills of the listed
    /// cards and the hovered card's loop. Not while playing.
    pub fn fx_request_thumbs(&mut self, base: ThumbBase) {
        if self.playing {
            return;
        }
        let mut wanted: Vec<(ThumbKey, &'static RecipeDef)> = visible_recipes(&self.fx.category, &self.fx.search).into_iter().map(|d| (ThumbKey::new(&base, d, STILL), d)).collect();
        if let Some(d) = self.fx.hover.and_then(|(r, _)| kadr_effects::recipe(r)) {
            wanted.extend((0..LOOP_FRAMES).map(|i| (ThumbKey::new(&base, d, i), d)));
        }
        let Some(need) = self.fx.thumbs.plan(wanted.iter().map(|(k, _)| *k)) else { return };
        let jobs: Vec<(ThumbKey, Project, Time)> = wanted
            .into_iter()
            .filter(|(k, _)| need.contains(k))
            .filter_map(|(k, d)| match base.clip {
                Some(id) => {
                    let c = self.project.sequence().clip(id)?;
                    Some((k, thumb_project(&self.project, c, demo_effect(d, c, base.at, k.phase_f())), base.at))
                }
                None => Some((k, sample_project(d, k.phase_f()), SAMPLE_AT)),
            })
            .collect();
        let resolver = self.preview.mcp_resolver();
        let sample = self.fx.thumbs.sample();
        let token = self.fx.thumbs.start(need);
        let spawned = std::thread::Builder::new().name("kadr-fx-thumbs".into()).spawn(move || {
            for (key, project, at) in jobs {
                if token.is_cancelled() {
                    return;
                }
                if let Some(px) = render(&project, at, resolver.as_deref(), key.clip.is_none().then_some(&sample)) {
                    crate::app::post(move |app| app.fx_thumb_ready(key, px));
                }
            }
        });
        if let Err(e) = spawned {
            tracing::warn!(error = %e, "thumbnail thread not started");
            self.fx.thumbs.cancel();
        }
    }

    /// A thumbnail came back from the background (UI thread).
    pub fn fx_thumb_ready(&mut self, key: ThumbKey, px: Vec<u8>) {
        let buf = slint::SharedPixelBuffer::<slint::Rgba8Pixel>::clone_from_slice(&px, THUMB_W, THUMB_H);
        self.fx.thumbs.insert(key, slint::Image::from_rgba8_premultiplied(buf));
        self.refresh_effects();
    }

    /// The hovered card's loop advances (~12 fps).
    pub fn fx_loop_tick(&mut self) {
        self.fx.thumbs.loop_frame = (self.fx.thumbs.loop_frame + 1) % LOOP_FRAMES;
        self.refresh_effects();
    }
}
```

```rust
        let mut c = clip_10_20();
        let def = kadr_effects::recipe("smooth_zoom").unwrap();
        let e = kadr_effects::fresh(def, &c, Time::from_secs(12));
        c.effects.push(e.clone());
        assert!(reset_commands(&c, &e, def, Time::from_secs(12)).is_none(), "already the defaults: no command");
        let mut out = e.clone();
        out.params.insert("direction".into(), ParamValue::Choice("out".into()));
        c.effects[0] = out.clone();
        assert!(matches!(reset_commands(&c, &out, def, Time::from_secs(12)).as_deref(), Some([EditCommand::UpdateEffect { .. }])));
        let k = TrackKey::fx(e.id, "strength");
        c.animation.set_key(k.clone(), SourceTime(Time::from_secs(31)), 0.1, None);
        let cmds = reset_commands(&c, &out, def, Time::from_secs(12)).unwrap();
        assert!(matches!(&cmds[..], [EditCommand::ClearAnimation { statics, .. }, EditCommand::UpdateEffect { .. }] if *statics == vec![(k.clone(), 0.6)]));
    }
```

```slint
export struct FxParamRow {
    key: string,
    // "fx:<effect-id>:<key>": a number row's live / commit / key actions.
    prop: string,
    kind: int, // 0 number, 1 choice, 2 point, 3 moment
    label: string,

    minimum: float,

    // Point: being picked on the preview now.
    active: bool,
}

export struct FxRow {
    id: string,
    name: string,
    enabled: bool,
    // false: a recipe or version from a newer Kadr (kept, never run).
    known: bool,
    first: bool,
    last: bool,
    params: [FxParamRow],
}

// A choice drawn with icons (zoom in / out, pan arrows) and a tooltip naming
// each option. Paths, not arrow glyphs (Windows may draw those as emoji).
component ChoiceSegments inherits Rectangle {
    in property <[string]> options;
    in property <[string]> tips;
    in property <int> current;
    callback selected(int);
    pure function icon-of(key: string) -> string {
        return key == "in" ? Icons.zoom-in : key == "out" ? Icons.zoom-out : key == "left" ? Icons.arrow-left : key == "right" ? Icons.arrow-right : key == "up" ? Icons.arrow-up : key == "down" ? Icons.arrow-down : Icons.sparkle;
    }

    width: root.options.length * 30px + 4px;
    border-radius: 5px;
    background: Theme.bg2;
    border-width: 1px;
    border-color: Theme.border;
    HorizontalLayout {
        padding: 2px;
        for o[i] in root.options: Rectangle {
            width: 30px;
            border-radius: 4px;
            background: root.current == i ? Theme.bg4 : sa.has-hover ? Theme.bg3 : transparent;
            Icon { icon: root.icon-of(o); width: 14px; height: 14px; color: root.current == i ? Theme.accent : Theme.text-dim; }
            sa := TouchArea {
                mouse-cursor: pointer;
                clicked => {
                    if (i != root.current) { root.selected(i); }
                }
            }

        }
    }
}

// «Мягко / Обычно / Сильно» under the Strength track: the label column
// (84 + 8 px) on the left; the value (62 + 8 px) and ◀ ◆ ▶ on the right.
component MarkCaptions inherits Rectangle {
    in property <[float]> marks;
    in property <[string]> labels;
    height: 12px;
    Rectangle {
        x: 92px;
        width: max(0px, parent.width - 92px - 70px - Theme.key-nav);

            color: Theme.text-faint;
        }
    }
}

// One effect of the clip (spec §6.2, mockup A): a header — switch, name,
// ⋯ with Сбросить / Удалить / Выше / Ниже — and its 1–5 parameters.
export component FxBlock inherits VerticalLayout {
    in property <FxRow> fx;
    // The playhead is off the clip: animated numbers are locked (keyframes spec §6.1).
    in property <bool> outside;
    callback action(string /* effect id */, string /* toggle | reset | remove | up | down | choice:<key>:<i> | pick:<key> | moment:<key> */);
    callback live(string, float);
    callback commit(string, float);
    callback key-action(string, string);
    spacing: 2px;
    padding-top: 4px;
    menu := ContextMenuArea {
        Menu {
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.reset"); enabled: root.fx.known; activated => { root.action(root.fx.id, "reset"); } }
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.remove"); activated => { root.action(root.fx.id, "remove"); } }
            MenuSeparator {}
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.up"); enabled: !root.fx.first; activated => { root.action(root.fx.id, "up"); } }
            MenuItem { title: Tr.t("fx.menu.down"); enabled: !root.fx.last; activated => { root.action(root.fx.id, "down"); } }
        }
        HorizontalLayout {
            height: 28px;
            spacing: 6px;
            VerticalLayout { alignment: center; Switch { enabled: root.fx.known; checked: root.fx.enabled; toggled => { root.action(root.fx.id, "toggle"); } } }
            Text { text: root.fx.name; font-size: Theme.font; font-weight: 600; color: root.fx.known && root.fx.enabled ? Theme.text : Theme.text-faint; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; horizontal-stretch: 1; }
            VerticalLayout {
                alignment: center;
                more := IconButton {
                    icon: Icons.more;
                    filled: true;
                    size: 24px;
                    icon-size: 14px;
                    tooltip: Tr.t("fx.menu.tip");
                    tooltip-right: true;
                    clicked => { menu.show({ x: more.absolute-position.x - menu.absolute-position.x, y: more.absolute-position.y - menu.absolute-position.y + more.height }); }
                }
            }
        }
    }
    if !root.fx.known: Text { text: Tr.t("fx.unavailable"); font-size: Theme.font-xs; color: Theme.text-faint; wrap: word-wrap; }
    for p in root.fx.params: VerticalLayout {
        if p.kind == 0 && p.strength: AnimRow {
            prop: p.prop; k: p.k; outside: root.outside; label: p.label; value: p.value;
            minimum: p.minimum; maximum: p.maximum; default-value: p.default-value;
            display-scale: 100; decimals: 0; unit: "%";
            marks: [0.3, 0.6, 0.9]; snap: 0.025;
            live(a, v) => { root.live(a, v); }
            commit(a, v) => { root.commit(a, v); }
            key-action(a, b) => { root.key-action(a, b); }
        }
        if p.kind == 0 && p.strength: MarkCaptions { marks: [0.3, 0.6, 0.9]; labels: [Tr.t("fx.strength.soft"), Tr.t("fx.strength.normal"), Tr.t("fx.strength.strong")]; }
        if p.kind == 0 && !p.strength: AnimRow {
            prop: p.prop; k: p.k; outside: root.outside; label: p.label; value: p.value;
            minimum: p.minimum; maximum: p.maximum; default-value: p.default-value;
            live(a, v) => { root.live(a, v); }
            commit(a, v) => { root.commit(a, v); }
            key-action(a, b) => { root.key-action(a, b); }
        }
        if p.kind == 1: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 30px;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            VerticalLayout { alignment: center; ChoiceSegments { options: p.options; tips: p.tips; current: p.current; selected(i) => { root.action(root.fx.id, "choice:" + p.key + ":" + i); } } }
            Rectangle { horizontal-stretch: 1; }
        }
        if p.kind == 2: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 28px;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            Text { text: p.text; font-size: Theme.font-sm; font-family: Theme.mono; color: Theme.text; vertical-alignment: center; horizontal-stretch: 1; }
            VerticalLayout {
                alignment: center;
                IconButton { icon: Icons.crosshair; active: p.active; enabled: p.can-act || p.active; size: 24px; icon-size: 14px; tooltip: p.active ? Tr.t("fx.pick.cancel_tip") : Tr.t("fx.pick.tip"); tooltip-right: true; clicked => { root.action(root.fx.id, "pick:" + p.key); } }
            }
        }
        if p.kind == 3: HorizontalLayout {
            height: 28px;
            spacing: 8px;
            Text { width: 84px; text: p.label; font-size: Theme.font-sm; color: Theme.text-dim; vertical-alignment: center; overflow: elide; }
            Text { text: p.text; font-size: Theme.font-sm; font-family: Theme.mono; color: Theme.text; vertical-alignment: center; horizontal-stretch: 1; }
            VerticalLayout {
                alignment: center;
                IconButton { icon: Icons.to-playhead; enabled: p.can-act; size: 24px; icon-size: 14px; tooltip: Tr.t("fx.moment.to_playhead"); tooltip-right: true; clicked => { root.action(root.fx.id, "moment:" + p.key); } }
            }
        }
        if p.hint != "": Text { text: p.hint; font-size: Theme.font-xs; color: Theme.text-faint; wrap: word-wrap; }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rust** — `apps/editor/src/anim_ui.rs` (imports: `use kadr_i18n::{t, tf};`, `TrackKey` from `kadr_project`):

```rust
/// The track an inspector row edits: a clip property (`PROPS`) or an
/// effect's number (`fx:<effect-id>:<key>`, effects spec §6.2).
pub fn track_of(prop: &str) -> Option<TrackKey> {
    param_of(prop).map(TrackKey::Clip).or_else(|| TrackKey::parse(prop).filter(|k| k.as_fx().is_some()))
}

/// The UI name of a track: the property's label, or «Сила (Плавный наезд)».
pub fn track_label(c: &Clip, k: &TrackKey) -> String {
    match k {
        TrackKey::Clip(p) => t(label_key(*p)),
        TrackKey::Fx(f) => {
            let effect = c.effect(f.effect).map_or_else(String::new, crate::effects_ui::fx_name);
            tf("fx.param_of", &[("param", &t(&format!("fx.param.{}", f.key))), ("effect", &effect)])
        }
    }
}

/// A slider value as stored: an effect's number rounded to 4 decimals (the
/// f32 slider would store 0.6000000238 for 60 %); clip properties as they come.
pub fn slider_value(k: &TrackKey, v: f32) -> f64 {
    match k {
        TrackKey::Fx(_) => (v as f64 * 10_000.0).round() / 10_000.0,
        TrackKey::Clip(_) => v as f64,
    }
}
```

```rust
            // An animated property: one keyframe at the playhead, none at a hidden time.

                let Some(tt) = anim_ui::edit_time(cur, self.playhead, frame) else { return self.show_rolled_back() };
                let value = anim_ui::slider_value(&k, v);
```

`inspector_key` — its head and command construction become:

```rust
        let (Some(id), Some(p)) = (self.inspected(), anim_ui::track_of(prop)) else { return };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let Some(c) = seq.clip(id) else { return };
        let frame = seq.frame_rate.frame_duration();
        let info = anim_ui::key_info(c, &p, self.playhead, frame);
        let edit = anim_ui::edit_time(c, self.playhead, frame);
        let here = anim_ui::key_here(c, &p, self.playhead, frame);
        // The picture at the playhead (or the nearest edge) stays as it is:
        // a first keyframe takes this value, removing the last one keeps it.
        let Some(value_here) = c.track_value(&p, anim_ui::shown_time(c, self.playhead, frame)) else { return };
        let cmd = match action {
            "prev" | "next" => {
                if let Some(tt) = if action == "prev" { info.prev } else { info.next } {
                    self.set_playhead(tt);
                }
                return;
            }
            "toggle" => match (edit, here) {
                (Some(_), Some(k)) => EditCommand::RemoveKeyframe { clip: id, param: p.clone(), at: k.at, keep: Some(value_here) },
                (Some(tt), None) => EditCommand::SetKeyframe { clip: id, param: p.clone(), at: SourceTime(c.source_time_at(tt)), value: value_here, ease: None },
                (None, _) => return,
            },
            "clear" if c.animation.is_animated(&p) => EditCommand::ClearAnimation { clip: id, statics: vec![(p.clone(), value_here)] },
            a if a.starts_with("ease:") => {
                let (Some(k), Some(e)) = (here, Ease::from_key(&a[5..])) else { return };
                if k.ease == e {
                    return;
                }
                EditCommand::SetKeyframeEase { clip: id, param: p.clone(), at: k.at, ease: e }
            }
            _ => return,
        };
        let label = tf(key_label(&cmd), &[("name", &anim_ui::track_label(c, &p))]);
        self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands: vec![cmd] });
```

`apps/editor/src/effects_ui.rs` — imports: add `crate::anim_ui`, `crate::{FxParamRow, FxRow, KeyState}`, `kadr_core::{EffectId, FrameRate, Timecode}`, `kadr_effects::{ParamDef, ParamKind}`, `kadr_project::{Clip, ParamValue, SourceTime, TrackKey}`, `slint::{Model, ModelRc, SharedString, VecModel}`, `std::collections::{BTreeMap, HashMap}`; then:

```rust
/// One parameter row of an effect block at the playhead (spec §6.2): a
/// number with ◀ ◆ ▶ (its value at the playhead, or at the nearest edge),
/// a choice, the point (pick on the preview) or the moment (to the playhead).
pub fn param_row(c: &Clip, e: &AppliedEffect, p: &ParamDef, playhead: Time, rate: FrameRate, picking: bool) -> FxParamRow {
    let frame = rate.frame_duration();
    let on_clip = anim_ui::edit_time(c, playhead, frame).is_some();
    let key = TrackKey::fx(e.id, p.key);
    let mut row = FxParamRow { key: p.key.into(), prop: key.key_string().into(), label: t(&p.label_key()).into(), ..Default::default() };
    match p.kind {
        ParamKind::Number { min, max, default } => {
            row.kind = 0;
            row.value = c.track_value(&key, anim_ui::shown_time(c, playhead, frame)).unwrap_or(default) as f32;
            (row.minimum, row.maximum, row.default_value) = (min as f32, max as f32, default as f32);
            let info = anim_ui::key_info(c, &key, playhead, frame);
            let ease = anim_ui::key_here(c, &key, playhead, frame).map_or("", |k| anim_ui::ease_key(&k.ease));
            row.k = KeyState { state: info.state, prev: info.prev.is_some(), next: info.next.is_some(), enabled: on_clip, ease: ease.into() };
            row.strength = p.key == "strength";
        }
        ParamKind::Choice { options, .. } => {
            row.kind = 1;
            let current = match e.params.get(p.key) {
                Some(ParamValue::Choice(v)) => v.as_str(),
                _ => "",
            };
            row.current = options.iter().position(|o| *o == current).map_or(-1, |i| i as i32);
            row.options = ModelRc::new(VecModel::from(options.iter().map(|o| SharedString::from(*o)).collect::<Vec<_>>()));
            row.tips = ModelRc::new(VecModel::from(options.iter().map(|o| SharedString::from(t(&format!("fx.choice.{o}")))).collect::<Vec<_>>()));
        }
        ParamKind::Point => {
            row.kind = 2;
            let (x, y) = match e.params.get(p.key) {
                Some(ParamValue::Point { x, y }) => (*x, *y),
                _ => (0.5, 0.5),
            };
            row.text = format!("{:.0} %, {:.0} %", x * 100.0, y * 100.0).into();
            row.can_act = on_clip;
            row.active = picking;
            row.hint = if picking { t("fx.pick.hint") } else if on_clip { String::new() } else { t("fx.pick.off_clip") }.into();
        }
        ParamKind::Moment => {
            row.kind = 3;
            row.can_act = on_clip;
            if let Some(ParamValue::Moment(m)) = e.params.get(p.key) {
                let tl = c.timeline_time_of(m.0);
                row.text = Timecode::from_time(tl.max(Time::ZERO), rate).to_string().into();
                if tl < c.timeline_in || tl >= c.timeline_out {
                    row.hint = t("fx.moment.outside").into();
                }
                row.can_act = on_clip && SourceTime(c.source_time_at(playhead)) != *m;
            }
        }
    }
    row
}

/// «Сбросить» of one effect: its defaults (the moment: the frame at `at`)
/// and no keyframes on its numbers — `None` when it is so already.
pub fn reset_commands(c: &Clip, e: &AppliedEffect, def: &RecipeDef, at: Time) -> Option<Vec<EditCommand>> {
    let defaults = kadr_effects::fresh(def, c, at).params;
    let animated: Vec<(TrackKey, f64)> = def
        .params
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|p| {
            let k = TrackKey::fx(e.id, p.key);
            let v = defaults.get(p.key)?.as_number()?;
            c.animation.is_animated(&k).then_some((k, v))
        })
        .collect();
    if animated.is_empty() && defaults.iter().all(|(k, v)| e.params.get(k) == Some(v)) {
        return None;
    }
    let mut commands = vec![];
    if !animated.is_empty() {
        commands.push(EditCommand::ClearAnimation { clip: c.id, statics: animated });
    }
    commands.push(EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: c.id, id: e.id, params: defaults, enabled: None });
    Some(commands)
}

/// `rows` into the model on screen, each row keeping its nested option
/// lists (a fresh `ModelRc` would make every row look changed and rebuild
/// its elements on each refresh).
fn reuse_param_rows(old: Option<ModelRc<FxParamRow>>, mut rows: Vec<FxParamRow>) -> ModelRc<FxParamRow> {
    if let Some(old) = &old {
        for (i, r) in rows.iter_mut().enumerate() {
            if let Some(o) = old.row_data(i) {
                r.options = crate::util::reuse_rows(Some(o.options), r.options.iter().collect());
                r.tips = crate::util::reuse_rows(Some(o.tips), r.tips.iter().collect());
            }
        }
    }
    crate::util::reuse_rows(old, rows)
}

impl App {
    /// The «Эффекты клипа» rows of `c` (spec §6.2, mockup A), updating the
    /// models on screen in place.
    pub fn fx_rows(&self, c: &Clip, old: ModelRc<FxRow>) -> ModelRc<FxRow> {
        let rate = self.project.sequence().frame_rate;
        let old_params: HashMap<SharedString, ModelRc<FxParamRow>> = old.iter().map(|r| (r.id, r.params)).collect();
        let rows: Vec<FxRow> = c
            .effects
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, e)| {
                let def = kadr_effects::definition(e);
                let params: Vec<FxParamRow> = def.map_or_else(Vec::new, |d| d.params.iter().map(|p| param_row(c, e, p, self.playhead, rate, false)).collect());
                let id: SharedString = e.id.to_string().into();
                let params = reuse_param_rows(old_params.get(&id).cloned(), params);
                FxRow { id, name: fx_name(e).into(), enabled: e.enabled, known: def.is_some(), first: i == 0, last: i + 1 == c.effects.len(), params }
            })
            .collect();
        crate::util::reuse_rows(Some(old), rows)
    }

    /// An effect block's action (spec §6.2): `toggle`, `reset`, `remove`,
    /// `up`, `down`, `choice:<key>:<index>`, `moment:<key>` (to the
    /// playhead). One undo entry each; nothing is sent when nothing would change.
    pub fn fx_action(&mut self, effect: &str, action: &str) {
        let (Some(id), Some(eid)) = (self.inspected(), EffectId::parse(effect)) else { return };
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let Some(c) = seq.clip(id) else { return };
        let Some(e) = c.effect(eid) else { return };
        let name = fx_name(e);
        let def = kadr_effects::definition(e);
        let pos = c.effects.iter().position(|x| x.id == eid).unwrap_or(0);
        let update = |params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue>, enabled: Option<bool>| EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: id, id: eid, params, enabled };
        let (label, commands): (String, Vec<EditCommand>) = match action {
            "toggle" if def.is_some() => {
                let key = if e.enabled { "cmd.effect_off_named" } else { "cmd.effect_on_named" };
                (tf(key, &[("name", &name)]), vec![update(BTreeMap::new(), Some(!e.enabled))])
            }
            "remove" => (tf("cmd.remove_effect_named", &[("name", &name)]), vec![EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: id, id: eid }]),
            "up" if pos > 0 => (t("cmd.move_effect"), vec![EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip: id, id: eid, to: pos - 1 }]),
            "down" if pos + 1 < c.effects.len() => (t("cmd.move_effect"), vec![EditCommand::MoveEffect { clip: id, id: eid, to: pos + 1 }]),
            "reset" => match def.and_then(|d| reset_commands(c, e, d, self.playhead)) {
                Some(commands) => (tf("cmd.reset_effect_named", &[("name", &name)]), commands),
                None => return,
            },
            a if a.starts_with("choice:") => {
                let Some((key, index)) = a["choice:".len()..].rsplit_once(':') else { return };
                let Some(ParamKind::Choice { options, .. }) = def.and_then(|d| d.param(key)).map(|p| p.kind) else { return };
                let Some(option) = index.parse::<usize>().ok().and_then(|i| options.get(i)) else { return };
                let v = ParamValue::Choice(option.to_string());
                if e.params.get(key) == Some(&v) {
                    return;
                }
                (tf("cmd.effect_named", &[("name", &name)]), vec![update(BTreeMap::from([(key.to_string(), v)]), None)])
            }
            a if a.starts_with("moment:") => {
                let key = &a["moment:".len()..];
                let Some(tt) = anim_ui::edit_time(c, self.playhead, seq.frame_rate.frame_duration()) else { return };
                let v = ParamValue::Moment(SourceTime(c.source_time_at(tt)));
                if def.and_then(|d| d.param(key)).is_none() || e.params.get(key) == Some(&v) {
                    return;
                }
                (tf("cmd.effect_named", &[("name", &name)]), vec![update(BTreeMap::from([(key.to_string(), v)]), None)])
            }
            _ => return,
        };
        self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands });
    }

    /// «Сбросить» of the section: every effect of the clip, after asking (spec §6.2).
    pub fn fx_ask_clear(&mut self, id: ClipId) {
        let Some(n) = self.project.sequence().clip(id).map(|c| c.effects.len()).filter(|n| *n > 0) else { return };
        let body = kadr_i18n::tn("dlg.clear_effects.body", n as i64, &[]);
        self.ask(&t("dlg.clear_effects.title"), &body, &t("dlg.clear_effects.ok"), "", true, crate::app::Confirm::ClearEffects(id));
    }

    /// The confirmed section reset: one undo step removes every effect (and its keyframes).
    pub fn fx_clear_now(&mut self, id: ClipId) {
        let Some(c) = self.project.sequence().clip(id) else { return };
        let commands: Vec<EditCommand> = c.effects.iter().map(|e| EditCommand::RemoveEffect { clip: id, id: e.id }).collect();
        if !commands.is_empty() {
            self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label: t("cmd.clear_effects"), commands });
        }
    }
}
```

`apps/editor/src/app.rs`: `Confirm` gets `ClearEffects(ClipId),`; `wire_callbacks`: `cb!(ui.on_inspector_fx, |e, a| |app: &mut App| app.fx_action(&e, &a));`. `apps/editor/src/persistence.rs` `confirm_answer`: `(Confirm::ClearEffects(id), true) => self.fx_clear_now(id),`.

```rust
    fn layer_with(placement: Placement, crop: Option<RectF>) -> Layer {
        Layer { id: LayerId(1), content: LayerContent::Solid(Rgba::BLACK), crop: crop.unwrap_or(placement.full_crop()), placement, opacity: 1.0, blend: BlendMode::Normal, effects: vec![] }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_click_picks_the_point_of_the_picture_as_shown() {
        let canvas = SizeU::new(1920, 1080);
        let base = Placement::fill(canvas);
        assert_eq!(pick_point(&layer_with(base, None), canvas, 0.25, 0.75), Some((0.25, 0.75)));
        // Zoomed ×2 about the centre: a click a quarter across shows the picture's 3/8.
        let zoomed = PlacementDelta { scale: 2.0, ..PlacementDelta::IDENTITY }.apply(&base);
        let (x, y) = pick_point(&layer_with(zoomed, None), canvas, 0.25, 0.5).unwrap();
        assert!((x - 0.375).abs() < 1e-6 && (y - 0.5).abs() < 1e-6, "{x} {y}");
        let back = point_on_canvas(&layer_with(zoomed, None), canvas, (0.375, 0.5));
        assert!((back.0 - 0.25).abs() < 1e-6 && (back.1 - 0.5).abs() < 1e-6, "the marker lands where the click was");
    }

    /// Review focus 4: outside the clip's visible picture nothing is picked.

    fn a_click_off_the_picture_picks_nothing() {
        let canvas = SizeU::new(1920, 1080);
        // A vertical video pillarboxed in a 16:9 frame: 607.5 px wide in the middle.
        let p = Placement { size: Vec2::new(607.5, 1080.0), anchor: Vec2::new(0.5, 0.5), position: Vec2::new(960.0, 540.0), scale: Vec2::new(1.0, 1.0), rotation: 0.0 };
        assert!(pick_point(&layer_with(p, None), canvas, 0.1, 0.5).is_none(), "the black bar");
        assert!(pick_point(&layer_with(p, None), canvas, 0.5, 0.5).is_some());
        let left_cropped = RectF::new(303.75, 0.0, 607.5, 1080.0);
        assert!(pick_point(&layer_with(p, Some(left_cropped)), canvas, 0.45, 0.5).is_none(), "a cropped-away part");
        assert!(pick_point(&layer_with(p, Some(left_cropped)), canvas, 0.55, 0.5).is_some());

            ..layer_with(Placement::fill(SizeU::new(100, 100)), None)
        };
        assert_eq!(layer_of(std::slice::from_ref(&tr), clip), Some(&inner));
        assert_eq!(layer_of(&[], clip), None);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — in `effects_ui.rs` (imports: `kadr_scene::{Layer, LayerContent, LayerId, SizeU}`, `kadr_core::ClipId` is already there):

```rust
/// The effect point being picked on the preview (spec §6.2 «Точка»).
#[derive(Clone, Debug, PartialEq)]
pub struct Pick {
    pub clip: ClipId,
    pub effect: EffectId,
    pub key: String,
}

/// The layer of `clip` in a scene's layers, also inside a transition.
pub fn layer_of(layers: &[Layer], clip: ClipId) -> Option<&Layer> {
    let id = LayerId::from(clip);
    layers.iter().find_map(|l| match &l.content {
        LayerContent::Transition(t) => layer_of(&t.from, clip).or_else(|| layer_of(&t.to, clip)),
        _ => (l.id == id).then_some(l),
    })
}

/// The point of `layer`'s picture under a click at canvas fraction (`fx`,
/// `fy`) — through the placement as shown (zoomed, panned, turned) — as
/// fractions of the picture; `None` off its visible part (review focus 4).
pub fn pick_point(layer: &Layer, canvas: SizeU, fx: f64, fy: f64) -> Option<(f64, f64)> {
    let inv = layer.placement.to_canvas().inverse()?;
    let (x, y) = inv.apply(fx * canvas.w as f64, fy * canvas.h as f64);
    let c = layer.crop;
    let inside = x >= c.x0 as f64 && x <= c.x1 as f64 && y >= c.y0 as f64 && y <= c.y1 as f64;
    let (w, h) = (layer.placement.size.x as f64, layer.placement.size.y as f64);
    (inside && w > 0.0 && h > 0.0).then(|| ((x / w).clamp(0.0, 1.0), (y / h).clamp(0.0, 1.0)))
}

/// Where the point `(px, py)` of `layer`'s picture shows, as canvas fractions.
pub fn point_on_canvas(layer: &Layer, canvas: SizeU, (px, py): (f64, f64)) -> (f64, f64) {
    let (x, y) = layer.placement.to_canvas().apply(px * layer.placement.size.x as f64, py * layer.placement.size.y as f64);
    (x / canvas.w.max(1) as f64, y / canvas.h.max(1) as f64)
}
```

and the methods:

```rust
impl App {
    /// «Выбрать на превью»: pick mode for the effect's point (crosshair,
    /// Esc cancels), or its end when it is on already.
    fn fx_toggle_pick(&mut self, pick: Pick) {
        if self.fx.picking.as_ref().is_some_and(|p| p.effect == pick.effect) {
            return self.fx_cancel_pick();
        }
        self.fx.picking = Some(pick);
        self.ui().set_fx_picking(true);
        self.refresh_inspector();
        self.fx_update_marker();
    }

    pub fn fx_cancel_pick(&mut self) {
        if self.fx.picking.take().is_some() {
            self.ui().set_fx_picking(false);
            self.refresh_inspector();
            self.fx_update_marker();
        }
    }

    /// The clip's layer in the frame the preview shows — the preview's own
    /// snapshot, so «До» and the hover preview count — with the canvas size.
    fn fx_shown_layer(&mut self, clip: ClipId) -> Option<(Layer, SizeU)> {
        let scenes = self.preview_scenes();
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let t = self.playhead.min((seq.duration() - seq.frame_rate.frame_duration()).max(Time::ZERO));
        let scene = kadr_playback::SceneSource::scene_at(&*scenes, t, &self.preview.output(&self.project));
        layer_of(&scene.layers, clip).cloned().map(|l| (l, scene.canvas))
    }

    /// A click on the preview in pick mode, at canvas fraction (`fx`, `fy`):
    /// the point of the picture under it becomes the effect's point (one
    /// undo entry). Off the picture, pick mode stays and says why.
    pub fn fx_point_picked(&mut self, fx: f32, fy: f32) {
        let Some(pick) = self.fx.picking.clone() else { return };
        let Some((layer, canvas)) = self.fx_shown_layer(pick.clip) else {
            return self.toast_warn(t("fx.pick.off_clip"));
        };
        let Some((x, y)) = pick_point(&layer, canvas, fx as f64, fy as f64) else {
            return self.toast_warn(t("fx.pick.miss"));
        };
        self.fx_cancel_pick();
        let round = |v: f64| (v * 10_000.0).round() / 10_000.0;
        let v = ParamValue::Point { x: round(x), y: round(y) };
        let Some(e) = self.project.sequence().clip(pick.clip).and_then(|c| c.effect(pick.effect)) else { return };
        if e.params.get(&pick.key) == Some(&v) {
            return;
        }
        let label = tf("cmd.effect_named", &[("name", &fx_name(e))]);
        let set = EditCommand::UpdateEffect { clip: pick.clip, id: pick.effect, params: BTreeMap::from([(pick.key.clone(), v)]), enabled: None };
        self.execute(EditCommand::Batch { label, commands: vec![set] });
    }

    /// The inspected clip's focused (or picked) effect with a point, and that point.
    fn fx_marker_target(&self) -> Option<(ClipId, (f64, f64))> {
        let id = self.inspected()?;
        let effect = self.fx.picking.as_ref().map(|p| p.effect).or(self.fx.focus)?;
        let e = self.project.sequence().clip(id)?.effect(effect)?;
        let key = kadr_effects::definition(e)?.params.iter().find(|p| matches!(p.kind, ParamKind::Point))?.key;
        Some(match e.params.get(key) {
            Some(ParamValue::Point { x, y }) => (id, (*x, *y)),
            _ => (id, (0.5, 0.5)),
        })
    }

    /// The point marker on the preview (spec §6.2): where the clip shows the
    /// focused effect's point, while paused.
    pub fn fx_update_marker(&mut self) {
        let shown = match self.fx_marker_target() {
            Some((clip, point)) if !self.playing => self.fx_shown_layer(clip).map(|(l, canvas)| point_on_canvas(&l, canvas, point)),
            _ => None,
        };
        let ui = self.ui();
        ui.set_fx_point_shown(shown.is_some());
        if let Some((x, y)) = shown {
            ui.set_fx_point_x(x as f32);
            ui.set_fx_point_y(y as f32);
        }
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — in `effects_ui.rs` (imports: `kadr_project::{FadeEdge, TrackKind}`):

```rust
/// Width of the `fx N` badge in a clip's name bar (timeline.slint draws it this wide).
pub const BADGE_W: f64 = 30.0;

/// The badge of a clip (spec §6.3): `fx N` for N effects, `""` without.
pub fn fx_badge(c: &Clip) -> String {
    if c.effects.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("fx {}", c.effects.len()) }
}

/// The badge's tooltip: the effects in order of application, a switched-off
/// one marked, one from a newer Kadr by its recipe id.
pub fn fx_tip(c: &Clip) -> String {
    let names: Vec<String> = c.effects.iter().map(|e| if e.enabled { fx_name(e) } else { tf("fx.tip.off", &[("name", &fx_name(e))]) }).collect();
    tf("fx.tip", &[("list", &names.join(", "))])
}

/// Where the badge is in a clip `width` px wide (clip x range): right-aligned
/// in the name bar, left of a fade-out handle that stays on screen
/// (`pinned_out`) — the same padding timeline.slint gives the name bar.
pub fn badge_span(width: f64, fade_out_px: f64, pinned_out: bool) -> (f64, f64) {
    let pad = if pinned_out { (width - anim_ui::handle_x(0.0, width, fade_out_px, FadeEdge::Out) + 7.0).max(6.0) } else { 6.0 };
    (width - pad - BADGE_W, width - pad)
}
```

`FxUi` gets `/// The clip whose badge tooltip shows. pub tip_clip: Option<ClipId>,`; `impl App`:

```rust
    /// The badge under lane point (`x`, `y`) and its tooltip text.
    fn fx_badge_at(&self, x: f64, y: f64) -> Option<(ClipId, String)> {
        let seq = self.project.sequence();
        let r = rows(seq, self.tl.scroll_y).into_iter().find(|r| y >= r.y && y < r.y + anim_ui::HEADER_H)?;
        let track = &seq.tracks[r.track];
        if r.kind != TrackKind::Video {
            return None;
        }
        let c = track.clip_at(self.x_to_time(x)).filter(|c| !c.effects.is_empty())?;
        let x0 = (c.timeline_in.as_secs_f64() - self.tl.scroll) * self.tl.pps;
        let width = c.duration().as_secs_f64() * self.tl.pps;
        let fo = c.video_fade.fade_out.as_secs_f64() * self.tl.pps;
        let (_, shown_out) = anim_ui::shown_handles(width, true, c.video_fade.fade_in, c.video_fade.fade_out);
        let pinned = shown_out && !track.locked && !self.tl.blade && (fo > 0.0 || self.tl.selection.contains(&c.id));
        let (a, b) = badge_span(width, fo, pinned);
        (x - x0 >= a && x - x0 <= b).then(|| (c.id, fx_tip(c)))
    }

    /// The badge tooltip (spec §6.3) while the pointer rests on a badge
    /// (`None`: the pointer left the lanes); a drag's own tips are untouched.
    pub fn fx_badge_hover(&mut self, at: Option<(f64, f64)>) {
        match at.and_then(|(x, y)| self.fx_badge_at(x, y).map(|hit| (hit, x, y))) {
            Some(((id, text), x, y)) => {
                self.fx.tip_clip = Some(id);
                let ui = self.ui();
                ui.set_tl_tip_text(text.into());
                ui.set_tl_tip_x(x as f32);
                ui.set_tl_tip_y(y as f32);
            }
            None => {
                if self.fx.tip_clip.take().is_some() {
                    self.ui().set_tl_tip_text("".into());
                }
            }
        }
    }
```
