### Task 14: End-to-end pixels, the bench row, live acceptance, review

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/project-scenes/tests/effects_pixels.rs`, `docs/perf/2026-10-11-effects-e1.md`
- Modify: `apps/kadr-bench/Cargo.toml` (`kadr-effects`), `apps/kadr-bench/src/scene_bench.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above.
- Produces: a pixel-level guarantee (evaluate → `CpuRenderer`) for strength 0, a switched-off effect, a split and a zoom; a bench row `scene / 10 clips × 5 motion effects`; the E1 live acceptance record.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the pixel test** — `crates/project-scenes/tests/effects_pixels.rs`:

```rust
//! Evaluator → renderer with motion effects (effects spec §9): strength 0
//! and a switched-off effect are pixel-identical to no effect; a split
//! keeps every pixel; Smooth Zoom magnifies about its point. No decoder:
//! the media layer gets a synthetic gradient frame.

use kadr_core::color::AlphaMode;
use kadr_core::{ColorInfo, CpuFrame, FrameRate, MediaInfo, MediaKind, Time, TimeRange, VideoInfo};
use kadr_project::{AppliedEffect, Clip, MediaAsset, ParamValue, Project};
use kadr_render::{CpuRenderer, CpuTarget, LayerInput, PreparedFrame, RenderInputs, RenderTarget, Renderer};
use kadr_scene::{LayerContent, OutputSpec, RenderQuality, SizeU};
use kadr_timeline::ops::{split_clip, LinkRemap};
use kadr_timeline::scene::evaluate;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::sync::Arc;

const W: u32 = 64;
const H: u32 = 36;

/// One 4 s clip of a 64 × 36 picture on a 64 × 36, 30 fps sequence.
fn project(effects: Vec<AppliedEffect>) -> Project {
    let mut p = Project::new("px");
    let seq = p.sequence_mut();
    (seq.frame_rate, seq.width, seq.height) = (FrameRate::FPS_30, W, H);
    let video = VideoInfo { width: W, height: H, frame_rate: Some(FrameRate::FPS_30), variable_frame_rate: false, codec: "h264".into(), pixel_format: "yuv420p".into(), rotation: 0, sar: (1, 1), color: None };
    let a = MediaAsset::new("g.mp4", MediaInfo { kind: MediaKind::Video, duration: Time::from_secs(60), container: "mp4".into(), size_bytes: 0, video: Some(video), audio: None, timecode: None });
    let mut c = Clip::new(a.id, "g", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(4)), Time::ZERO);
    c.effects = effects;
    p.assets.push(a);
    p.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips.push(c);
    p
}

/// Red grows left → right, green top → bottom: any move shows.
fn gradient() -> Arc<CpuFrame> {
    let mut px = vec![0u8; (W * H * 4) as usize];
    for y in 0..H {
        for x in 0..W {
            let i = ((y * W + x) * 4) as usize;
            px[i..i + 4].copy_from_slice(&[(x * 4) as u8, (y * 7) as u8, 128, 255]);
        }
    }
    Arc::new(CpuFrame::from_rgba8(W, H, ColorInfo { alpha: AlphaMode::Opaque, ..ColorInfo::WORKING_SDR }, px))
}

fn render(p: &Project, t: Time) -> Vec<u8> {
    let out = OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(W, H), RenderQuality::Export);
    let scene = evaluate(p, p.sequence(), t, &out);
    let g = gradient();
    let inputs = RenderInputs { layers: scene.layers.iter().map(|l| match l.content { LayerContent::Media { .. } => LayerInput::Cpu(g.clone()), _ => LayerInput::None }).collect() };
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; (W * H * 4) as usize];
    CpuRenderer::new().render(&PreparedFrame { scene: &scene, inputs: &inputs }, &mut RenderTarget::Cpu(CpuTarget::packed(W, H, &mut buf))).unwrap();
    buf
}

fn fx(recipe: &str, set: &[(&str, ParamValue)]) -> AppliedEffect {
    let params: BTreeMap<String, ParamValue> = set.iter().map(|(k, v)| (k.to_string(), v.clone())).collect();
    AppliedEffect::new(recipe, 1, params)
}

#[test]
fn zero_strength_and_a_switched_off_effect_are_pixel_identical_to_no_effect() {
    let plain = project(vec![]);
    let mut off = fx("handheld", &[]);
    off.enabled = false;
    for t in [0, 1_000, 2_500, 3_900].map(Time::from_millis) {
        let want = render(&plain, t);
        assert_eq!(render(&project(vec![fx("smooth_zoom", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(0.0))])]), t), want, "strength 0 at {t:?}");
        assert_eq!(render(&project(vec![off.clone()]), t), want, "switched off at {t:?}");
    }
}

/// Review focus 2 at the pixel level: the frames around a cut are the
/// frames of the whole clip (within the renderer's ±1 LSB).
#[test]
fn a_split_keeps_every_pixel() {
    let whole = project(vec![fx("handheld", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(1.0))]), fx("smooth_zoom", &[])]);
    let mut split = whole.clone();
    let c = split.sequence().tracks[0].clips[0].clone();
    let (l, r) = split_clip(&c, FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(37), &mut LinkRemap::default());
    split.sequence_mut().tracks[0].clips = vec![l, r];
    for f in 30..45 {
        let t = FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(f);
        let worst = render(&whole, t).iter().zip(render(&split, t)).map(|(a, b)| a.abs_diff(b)).max().unwrap();
        assert!(worst <= 1, "frame {f}: off by {worst}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn smooth_zoom_magnifies_about_its_point() {
    let last = Time::from_secs(4) - FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_duration();
    let plain = render(&project(vec![]), last);
    let zoomed = render(&project(vec![fx("smooth_zoom", &[("strength", ParamValue::Number(1.0))])]), last);
    let red = |buf: &[u8], x: u32, y: u32| buf[((y * W + x) * 4) as usize] as i32;
    assert!(red(&zoomed, 0, 18) > red(&plain, 0, 18) + 8, "the left edge shows content from further in: {} vs {}", red(&zoomed, 0, 18), red(&plain, 0, 18));
    assert!((red(&zoomed, 32, 18) - red(&plain, 32, 18)).abs() <= 2, "the centre (the point) stays");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Bench row** — `apps/kadr-bench/Cargo.toml` `[dependencies]`: `kadr-effects = { path = "../../crates/effects" }`. In `scene_bench.rs` (module doc: add "and the effects row: 10 clips × 5 motion effects (effects spec §9)"):

```rust
/// 10 full-length picture-in-picture clips on 10 video tracks, each with all
/// five motion effects at 100 %.
fn motion_project() -> Project {
    let mut p = Project::new("bench-motion");
    let a = asset(MediaKind::Video, 1920, 1080);
    p.assets.push(a.clone());
    for i in 1..10 {
        p.sequence_mut().tracks.insert(i, Track::new(TrackKind::Video, format!("V{}", i + 1)));
    }
    for i in 0..10 {
        let mut c = Clip::new(a.id, "c", TimeRange::new(Time::ZERO, Time::from_secs(60)), Time::ZERO);
        c.transform.scale = 0.3; // none of the ten covers the canvas: nothing is culled
        for def in kadr_effects::registry() {
            let mut e = kadr_effects::fresh(def, &c, Time::from_secs(30));
            e.params.insert("strength".into(), kadr_project::ParamValue::Number(1.0));
            c.effects.push(e);
        }
        p.sequence_mut().tracks[i].clips.push(c);
    }
    p
}
```

in `run()` after the animated row:

```rust
    let s = sample(&motion_project());
    for (metric, v) in [("p50", s.p50), ("p90", s.p90), ("p99", s.p99), ("max", s.max)] {
        r.push("scene", "10 clips × 5 motion effects", metric, us(v), "µs");
    }
```

and a test:

```rust
    /// The effects row really evaluates ten layers with five effects each.
    #[test]
    fn motion_bench_scene_keeps_ten_layers_with_their_effects() {
        let p = motion_project();
        let out = OutputSpec::new(SizeU::new(1920, 1080), RenderQuality::PreviewHigh);
        for f in (0..1800).step_by(30) {
            assert_eq!(evaluate(&p, p.sequence(), FrameRate::FPS_30.frame_to_time(f), &out).layers.len(), 10, "frame {f}");
        }
        assert!(p.sequence().tracks.iter().flat_map(|t| &t.clips).all(|c| c.effects.len() == 5));
    }
```

  4. Smooth Zoom at ¼, ½, ¾ of the clip follows ease-in-out (½ ≈ half the full zoom); it looks smooth in playback.
  5. Split at the middle: the frames on both sides of the cut equal the unsplit ones; trim from the start: the zoom restarts at the new first frame and spans the new length; Punch In stays on its content frame after a trim.
  6. Handheld at 100 % on the vertical and the VFR clip: natural motion, no black at the frame edges (`get_frame` corners).
  7. Strength: snaps to 30 / 60 / 90 %, one undo entry per drag; ◆ at two moments animates the strength (MCP `set_keyframe` with `fx:<id>:strength` gives the same).
  8. Point: «Выбрать на превью» shows the crosshair and the hint; Esc (`ui_dispatch_key_event`) cancels; a click on the subject centres the zoom there; the marker shows while the effect is in focus; a click on a letterbox bar keeps pick mode with «Щёлкните внутри кадра клипа».
  9. Moment «к плейхеду» moves the punch; after a trim that hides it the inspector says it is outside.
  10. ⋯: Сбросить, Удалить, Выше, Ниже each one undo entry; the section's «Сбросить» asks, then removes all in one entry.

  13. MCP: `list_effects`; `edit` `add_effect` / `set_effect` / `remove_effect` / `move_effect`; `get_state` shows effects; `set_effect` on an animated strength is rejected with "strength is animated".
  14. AI assistant: «добавь плавный наезд на выделенный клип» proposes an `add_effect` plan; applying it is one undo entry.
  15. A hand-edited project with `"recipe": "glitch_v2"`, an extra field and an `fx:` track of that effect opens; the inspector shows «Недоступен (создан в более новой версии)»; saving keeps the effect and its track as JSON values; «Удалить» works and is undoable.
  16. «До» (`\`) shows the clip without motion.
  17. Performance (`get_perf`, reset before each): playback of a 1080p clip with Handheld + Smooth Zoom against the same clip without effects — frame time p90 and dropped frames no worse; the first frame after a hover within ~200 ms; card thumbnails render while paused and never during playback.
